BASE_DIR   = os.path.abspath(os.path.dirname(__file__))
DATA_DIR   = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'data')
UPLOAD_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'static', 'uploads')

os.makedirs(UPLOAD_DIR, exist_ok=True)

app = Flask(__name__)
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = f'sqlite:///{os.path.join(DATA_DIR,"odms.db")}'
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False
app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'] = UPLOAD_DIR

def get_wib():
    return datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=7)

@app.template_filter('from_json')
def from_json_filter(s):
    if not s: return []
    try:
        return json.loads(s)
    except:
        return []

class User(UserMixin, db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'users'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   username      = db.Column(db.String(80),  unique=True, nullable=False)
   email         = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
   full_name     = db.Column(db.String(200))
   role          = db.Column(db.String(20), default='user')  # user/operation/admin
   password_hash = db.Column(db.String(256), nullable=False)
   is_active_acc = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=True)
   site          = db.Column(db.String(200))
   divisi        = db.Column(db.String(100))
   job_position  = db.Column(db.String(100))
   user_signature = db.Column(db.Text)  # Base64 signature
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   last_login    = db.Column(db.DateTime)

   def set_password(self, pw): self.password_hash = generate_password_hash(pw)
   def check_password(self, pw): return check_password_hash(self.password_hash, pw)
   @property
   def is_admin(self): return self.role == 'admin'
   @property
   def is_lead_engineer(self): return self.role == 'lead_engineer'
   @property
   def is_run(self): return self.role == 'run'
   @property
   def is_build(self): return self.role == 'build'
   @property
   def is_manager(self): return self.role == 'manager'
   @property
   def is_director(self): return self.role == 'director'
   @property
   def is_user_role(self): return self.role == 'user'
   @property
   def role_label(self):
       return {'admin':'Admin','run':'RUN TEAM','build':'BUILD TEAM','lead_engineer':'LEAD ENGINEER','manager':'Manager','director':'Director','user':'User'}.get(self.role, self.role)


class DropdownOption(db.Model):
   """Dynamic dropdown options managed by admin."""
   __tablename__ = 'dropdown_options'
   id         = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   category   = db.Column(db.String(50), nullable=False, index=True)  # sites/device_types/impacts/gates/divisions
   value      = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
   sort_order = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
   is_active  = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=True)

   @staticmethod
   def get(category):
       return [o.value for o in DropdownOption.query
               .filter_by(category=category, is_active=True)
               .order_by(DropdownOption.sort_order, DropdownOption.value).all()]


class Ticket(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'tickets'
   id               = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   ticket_id        = db.Column(db.String(20), unique=True, nullable=False)
   # Reporter identity
   nama_pelapor     = db.Column(db.String(200))
   divisi_pelapor   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   phone_telegram   = db.Column(db.String(50))   # nomor telepon Telegram pelapor
   # Location
   site             = db.Column(db.String(200))
   gate_area        = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')   # JSON list
   # Device
   device_type      = db.Column(db.String(200))          # may include "Other: ..."
   device_type_other = db.Column(db.String(200))
   # Issue
   issue_desc       = db.Column(db.Text)
   impact           = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')   # JSON list
   impact_other     = db.Column(db.String(500))
   urgency          = db.Column(db.String(20))
   priority         = db.Column(db.String(20))           # may differ after RUN correction
   # Status
   status           = db.Column(db.String(30), default='open')
   assigned_to      = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   created_by       = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   location_id      = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'), nullable=True)
   # Timestamps (UTC)
   created_at       = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at       = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
   sla_deadline     = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   response_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   resolved_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   closed_at        = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   # Validation
   validated_by     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   validation_note  = db.Column(db.Text)
   # Attachments
   attachment_files = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   sig_reporter     = db.Column(db.Text)   # base64 PNG
   sig_engineer     = db.Column(db.Text)   # base64 PNG
   sig_admin        = db.Column(db.Text)   # base64 PNG

   updates   = db.relationship('TicketUpdate', backref='ticket', lazy='dynamic', order_by='TicketUpdate.created_at')
   creator   = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by])
   assignee  = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[assigned_to])
   validator = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[validated_by])
   location  = db.relationship('AssetLocation', backref=db.backref('tickets', lazy='dynamic'))

   @property
   def gate_area_list(self):
       # Support both legacy JSON array and new plain text
       val = self.gate_area or ''
       if val.startswith('['):
           try: return json.loads(val)
           except: pass
       return [val] if val else []
   @property
   def gate_area_display(self):
       val = self.gate_area or ''
       if val.startswith('['):
           try:
               gl = json.loads(val)
               return ', '.join(gl) if gl else '—'
           except: pass
       return val or '—'
   @property
   def impact_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.impact or '[]')
       except: return []
   @property
   def attachments(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.attachment_files or '[]')
       except: return []
   @property
   def sla_minutes(self):
       s = get_settings()
       return {'critical':s.sla_critical,'high':s.sla_high,
               'medium':s.sla_medium,'low':s.sla_low}.get(self.priority or 'medium', 480)
   @property
   def sla_remaining_seconds(self):
       if not self.sla_deadline: return None
       if self.status in ('closed','resolved'): return 0
       delta = (self.sla_deadline - datetime.utcnow()).total_seconds()
       return max(0, delta)
   @property
   def sla_breached(self):
       if not self.sla_deadline: return False
       return datetime.utcnow() > self.sla_deadline and self.status not in ('closed','resolved')
   @property
   def sla_pct(self):
       total = self.sla_minutes * 60
       remaining = self.sla_remaining_seconds or 0
       if not total: return 0
       return max(0, min(100, round((remaining / total) * 100)))
   @property
   def mttr_minutes(self):
       if self.resolved_at and self.created_at:
           return round((self.resolved_at - self.created_at).total_seconds() / 60)
       return None
   @property
   def status_label(self):
       return {'open':'Open','in_progress':'In Progress','monitoring':'Monitoring',
               'resolved':'Resolved','closed':'Closed','reopened':'Reopened'}.get(self.status, self.status)
   @property
   def status_color(self):
       return {'open':'red','in_progress':'amber','monitoring':'blue',
               'resolved':'teal','closed':'green','reopened':'purple'}.get(self.status,'gray')
   @property
   def priority_color(self):
       return {'critical':'red','high':'amber','medium':'blue','low':'green'}.get(self.priority,'gray')
   def to_dict(self):
       return {'id':self.id,'ticket_id':self.ticket_id,'site':self.site,
               'device_type':self.device_type,'priority':self.priority,'status':self.status,
               'created_at':self.created_at.isoformat(),'sla_remaining':self.sla_remaining_seconds,
               'sla_breached':self.sla_breached, 'location_id':self.location_id}


class TicketUpdate(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'ticket_updates'
   id                 = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   ticket_id_fk       = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('tickets.id'), nullable=False)
   engineer_id        = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   old_status         = db.Column(db.String(30))
   new_status         = db.Column(db.String(30))
   urgency_corrected  = db.Column(db.String(20))   # if RUN changed urgency
   root_symptoms      = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   technical_analysis = db.Column(db.Text)
   actions_taken      = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   actions_detail     = db.Column(db.Text)
   short_term_sol     = db.Column(db.Text)
   long_term_sol      = db.Column(db.Text)
   mitigation_plan    = db.Column(db.Text)
   attachments        = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   note               = db.Column(db.Text)
   created_at         = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

   engineer = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[engineer_id])

   @property
   def root_symptoms_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.root_symptoms or '[]')
       except: return []
   @property
   def actions_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.actions_taken or '[]')
       except: return []
   @property
   def attachment_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.attachments or '[]')
       except: return []


class LocationGroup(db.Model):
    """Master data for asset location groups (e.g., Premium, Parkee, etc.)"""
    __tablename__ = 'location_groups'
    id         = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name       = db.Column(db.String(100), unique=True, nullable=False)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=get_wib)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {'id': self.id, 'name': self.name}

class AssetLocation(db.Model):
   """Asset locations with GPS coordinates for the Asset Monitoring map."""
   __tablename__ = 'asset_locations'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   name          = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
   location_code = db.Column(db.String(50))
   address       = db.Column(db.Text)
   city          = db.Column(db.String(100))
   lat           = db.Column(db.Float)
   lng           = db.Column(db.Float)
   asset_type    = db.Column(db.String(100))
   group_id      = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('location_groups.id'), nullable=True)
   device_count  = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
   nuc_count     = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
   camera_count  = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0)
   status        = db.Column(db.String(30), default='active')
   notes         = db.Column(db.Text)
   cp_manager    = db.Column(db.String(200))
   cp_manager_phone = db.Column(db.String(50))
   cp_manager_email = db.Column(db.String(120))
   area_manager  = db.Column(db.String(200))
   area_manager_phone = db.Column(db.String(50))
   area_manager_email = db.Column(db.String(120))
   created_by    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

   # Technical Details (LPR Engine / NUC)
   ssh_port      = db.Column(db.String(20))
   grafana_cred  = db.Column(db.String(255))
   anydesk_cred  = db.Column(db.String(255))
   user_password = db.Column(db.String(255))
   tailscale_ip  = db.Column(db.String(100))
   engine_mata_ver = db.Column(db.String(50))
   abtool_ver     = db.Column(db.String(50))
   etl_ver        = db.Column(db.String(50))
   nuc_received_date = db.Column(db.Date)
   nuc_setup_date   = db.Column(db.Date)
   nuc_handover_date = db.Column(db.Date)
   nuc_handover_details = db.Column(db.Text)
   nuc_handover_att  = db.Column(db.String(255))

   # Deployment Details
   lpr_db_main_server = db.Column(db.String(200))
   lpr_db_name        = db.Column(db.String(100))
   lpr_db_restore_status = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=False)
   parkee_redis_host  = db.Column(db.String(200))
    
   etl_activated_by   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   etl_activated_at   = db.Column(db.Date)
   etl_attachment     = db.Column(db.String(255))
   etl_status         = db.Column(db.String(50))
    
   parkee_integration_by = db.Column(db.String(100))
   parkee_integration_at = db.Column(db.Date)
   parkee_integration_attachment = db.Column(db.String(255))
   parkee_integration_status = db.Column(db.String(50))

   creator = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by])

   @property
   def open_issues_count(self):
       return AssetIssue.query.filter(AssetIssue.location_id == self.id, AssetIssue.status != 'resolved').count()

   @property
   def has_open_issues(self):
       return self.open_issues_count > 0

   @property
   def effective_status(self):
       if self.status == 'offline': return 'offline'
       if self.open_issues_count > 0: return 'maintenance'
       return self.status

   def to_dict(self):
       eff = self.effective_status
       g = db.session.get(LocationGroup, self.group_id) if self.group_id else None
       
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'name': self.name, 'location_code': self.location_code,
           'address': self.address, 'city': self.city,
           'lat': self.lat, 'lng': self.lng,
           'asset_type': self.asset_type,
           'device_count': self.device_count,
           'nuc_count': self.nuc_count or 0,
           'camera_count': self.camera_count or 0,
           'status': self.status,
           'effective_status': eff,
           'open_issues': self.open_issues_count,
           'has_issues': self.has_open_issues,
           'issues': [iss.to_dict() for iss in AssetIssue.query.filter_by(location_id=self.id, status='open').all()],
           'group_id': self.group_id,
           'group_name': g.name if g else None,
           'notes': self.notes,
           'cp_manager': self.cp_manager,
           'cp_manager_phone': self.cp_manager_phone,
           'cp_manager_email': self.cp_manager_email,
           'area_manager': self.area_manager,
           'area_manager_phone': self.area_manager_phone,
           'area_manager_email': self.area_manager_email,
           'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat() if self.created_at else None,
           'ssh_port': self.ssh_port,
           'grafana_cred': self.grafana_cred,
           'anydesk_cred': self.anydesk_cred,
           'user_password': self.user_password,
           'tailscale_ip': self.tailscale_ip,
           'engine_mata_ver': self.engine_mata_ver,
           'abtool_ver': self.abtool_ver,
           'etl_ver': self.etl_ver,
           'nuc_received_date': self.nuc_received_date.isoformat() if self.nuc_received_date else None,
           'nuc_setup_date': self.nuc_setup_date.isoformat() if self.nuc_setup_date else None,
           'nuc_handover_date': self.nuc_handover_date.isoformat() if self.nuc_handover_date else None,
           'nuc_handover_details': self.nuc_handover_details,
           'nuc_handover_att': self.nuc_handover_att,
           'lpr_db_main_server': self.lpr_db_main_server,
           'lpr_db_name': self.lpr_db_name,
           'lpr_db_restore_status': self.lpr_db_restore_status,
           'parkee_redis_host': self.parkee_redis_host,
           'etl_activated_by': self.etl_activated_by,
           'etl_activated_at': self.etl_activated_at.isoformat() if self.etl_activated_at else None,
           'etl_attachment': self.etl_attachment,
           'etl_status': self.etl_status,
           'parkee_integration_by': self.parkee_integration_by,
           'parkee_integration_at': self.parkee_integration_at.isoformat() if self.parkee_integration_at else None,
           'parkee_integration_attachment': self.parkee_integration_attachment,
           'parkee_integration_status': self.parkee_integration_status,
           'devices': [d.to_dict() for d in self.devices.all()],
       }


class AssetDevice(db.Model):
   """Individual device (Server/NUC or Camera) under an asset location."""
   __tablename__ = 'asset_devices'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   location_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'), nullable=False)
   device_name   = db.Column(db.String(200))
   device_category = db.Column(db.String(30))  # server / camera
   device_type   = db.Column(db.String(100))   # NUC i5/i7, IP Camera, LPR Camera, etc
   hostname_role = db.Column(db.String(20))    # Utama / Sub
   ip_address    = db.Column(db.String(50))
   username      = db.Column(db.String(100))
   password      = db.Column(db.String(100))
   port          = db.Column(db.String(10))
   gate_name     = db.Column(db.String(100))
   assessment    = db.Column(db.Text)
   serial_number = db.Column(db.String(100))
   brand_model   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   status        = db.Column(db.String(30), default='active')
   # SOP Checklist & Technical Details
   ssh1_port     = db.Column(db.String(10))
   ssh2_port     = db.Column(db.String(10))
   tailscale_ip  = db.Column(db.String(50))
   anydesk_id    = db.Column(db.String(50))
   anydesk_pw    = db.Column(db.String(50))
   mata_version   = db.Column(db.String(50))
   alpr_version   = db.Column(db.String(50))
   docker_version = db.Column(db.String(50))
   has_abtool     = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=False)
   has_grafana    = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=False)
   grafana_user   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   grafana_pass   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   frame_rate     = db.Column(db.String(10))
    
   # Camera Deployment Details
   resolution     = db.Column(db.String(50))
   fps            = db.Column(db.String(20))
   bitrate        = db.Column(db.String(20))
   image_config   = db.Column(db.Text) # JSON string for brightness, contrast, etc.
   ws_lpr_port    = db.Column(db.String(20))
   anpr_port      = db.Column(db.String(20))
   grafana_role   = db.Column(db.String(50))
   
   notes         = db.Column(db.Text)
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

   location = db.relationship('AssetLocation', backref=db.backref('devices', lazy='dynamic', cascade='all,delete-orphan'))

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id':self.id,'location_id':self.location_id,
           'device_name':self.device_name,'device_category':self.device_category,
           'device_type':self.device_type,'hostname_role':self.hostname_role,
           'ip_address':self.ip_address, 'username':self.username, 'password':self.password, 'port':self.port,
           'gate_name':self.gate_name, 'assessment':self.assessment,
           'serial_number':self.serial_number,'brand_model':self.brand_model,
           'status':self.status,
           'ssh1_port':self.ssh1_port, 'ssh2_port':self.ssh2_port,
           'tailscale_ip':self.tailscale_ip, 'anydesk_id':self.anydesk_id, 'anydesk_pw':self.anydesk_pw,
           'mata_version':self.mata_version, 'alpr_version':self.alpr_version, 'docker_version':self.docker_version,
           'has_abtool':self.has_abtool, 'has_grafana':self.has_grafana,
           'grafana_user':self.grafana_user, 'grafana_pass':self.grafana_pass,
           'frame_rate': self.frame_rate,
           'resolution': self.resolution,
           'fps': self.fps,
           'bitrate': self.bitrate,
           'image_config': self.image_config,
           'ws_lpr_port': self.ws_lpr_port,
           'anpr_port': self.anpr_port,
           'grafana_role': self.grafana_role,
           'notes':self.notes,
       }


class AssetIssue(db.Model):
   """Damage report / maintenance record for an asset device."""
   __tablename__ = 'asset_issues'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   location_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'), nullable=False)
   device_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_devices.id'), nullable=True)
   issue_type    = db.Column(db.String(50))   # damage/maintenance/offline/resolved
   title         = db.Column(db.String(300), nullable=False)
   description   = db.Column(db.Text)
   severity      = db.Column(db.String(20), default='medium')  # critical/high/medium/low
   status        = db.Column(db.String(30), default='open')    # open/in_progress/resolved
   reported_by   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   resolved_by   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'), nullable=True)
   resolved_at   = db.Column(db.DateTime, nullable=True)
   resolution_note = db.Column(db.Text)
   attachment      = db.Column(db.String(255))
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

   location = db.relationship('AssetLocation', backref=db.backref('issues', lazy='dynamic', cascade='all,delete-orphan'))
   device   = db.relationship('AssetDevice', backref=db.backref('issues', lazy='dynamic', cascade='all,delete-orphan'))
   reporter = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[reported_by])
   resolver = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[resolved_by])
   
   # Relationship to Work Order
   work_orders    = db.relationship('WorkOrder', backref='issue', lazy=True, cascade="all, delete-orphan")

   @property
   def severity_color(self):
       return {'critical':'red','high':'amber','medium':'blue','low':'green'}.get(self.severity,'gray')

   def to_dict(self):
      return {
          'id':self.id,'location_id':self.location_id,'device_id':self.device_id,
          'issue_type':self.issue_type,'title':self.title,'description':self.description,
          'severity':self.severity,'status':self.status,
          'severity_color': self.severity_color,
          'reported_by':self.reported_by,
          'reporter_name': self.reporter.full_name if self.reporter else '—',
          'location_name': self.location.name if self.location else '—',
          'device_name': self.device.device_name if self.device else '—',
          'resolution_note': self.resolution_note or '',
          'root_cause': getattr(self, 'root_cause', ''),
          'mitigation': getattr(self, 'mitigation', ''),
          'ba_status': self.ba.status if (hasattr(self, 'ba') and self.ba) else None,
          'attachment': self.attachment,
          'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat() if self.created_at else None,
          'resolved_at': self.resolved_at.isoformat() if self.resolved_at else None,
          'work_orders': [wo.to_dict() for wo in self.work_orders] if hasattr(self, 'work_orders') else []
      }


class AssetIssueBA(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "asset_issue_ba"
    id              = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    issue_id        = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("asset_issues.id"), nullable=True)
    engineer_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    status          = db.Column(db.String(30), default="pending") # pending, approved
    approver_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    approved_at     = db.Column(db.DateTime, nullable=True)
    content_json    = db.Column(db.Text)
    pdf_filename    = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=True)
    ba_number       = db.Column(db.String(100))
    perihal         = db.Column(db.String(255))
    lokasi_text     = db.Column(db.String(255))
    device_gate     = db.Column(db.String(255))
    kronologi       = db.Column(db.Text)
    tindakan_dilakukan = db.Column(db.Text)
    analisa_rekomendasi = db.Column(db.Text)
    revision_reason     = db.Column(db.Text)
    intro_text          = db.Column(db.Text)
    ba_type         = db.Column(db.String(30), default="troubleshoot") # troubleshoot, live
    created_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=get_wib)


    issue    = db.relationship("AssetIssue", backref=db.backref("ba", uselist=False))
    engineer = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[engineer_id])
    approver = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[approver_id])

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "issue_id": self.issue_id,
            "status": self.status,
            "engineer_name": self.engineer.full_name if self.engineer else "Unknown",
            "approver_name": self.approver.full_name if self.approver else None,
            "approved_at": self.approved_at.isoformat() if self.approved_at else None,
            "pdf_url": f"/berita-acara/{self.ba_type}/{self.id}/print",
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "ba_number": self.ba_number,
            "perihal": self.perihal,
            "lokasi_text": self.lokasi_text,
            "device_gate": self.device_gate,
            "kronologi": self.kronologi,
            "tindakan_dilakukan": self.tindakan_dilakukan,
            "analisa_rekomendasi": self.analisa_rekomendasi,
            "ba_type": self.ba_type,
            "view_url": f"/berita-acara/{self.ba_type}/{self.id}"
        }


class WorkOrder(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'asset_work_orders'
   id             = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   issue_id       = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_issues.id'), nullable=False)
   assigned_to    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   status         = db.Column(db.String(20), default='open') # open, in_progress, done
   photo_before   = db.Column(db.String(255))
   photo_after    = db.Column(db.String(255))
   started_at     = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   completed_at   = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   notes          = db.Column(db.Text)
   created_at     = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   
   technician = db.relationship('User', backref='asset_work_orders')
   parts      = db.relationship('WorkOrderPart', backref='work_order', lazy=True, cascade="all, delete-orphan")

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id,
           'issue_id': self.issue_id,
           'assigned_to': self.technician.full_name if self.technician else 'Unassigned',
           'assigned_id': self.assigned_to,
           'status': self.status,
           'photo_before': self.photo_before,
           'photo_after': self.photo_after,
           'started_at': self.started_at.isoformat() if self.started_at else None,
           'completed_at': self.completed_at.isoformat() if self.completed_at else None,
           'notes': self.notes,
           'parts': [p.to_dict() for p in self.parts]
       }

class WorkOrderPart(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'asset_work_order_parts'
   id             = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   wo_id          = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_work_orders.id'), nullable=False)
   part_name      = db.Column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
   quantity       = db.Column(db.Integer, default=1)
   notes          = db.Column(db.String(255))

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'part_name': self.part_name, 'quantity': self.quantity, 'notes': self.notes
       }


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class Project(db.Model):
   """Core project model for Build & Large Maintenance."""
   __tablename__ = 'projects'
   id              = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   ticket_id       = db.Column(db.String(50))
   name            = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
   project_owner   = db.Column(db.String(255))
   system_name     = db.Column(db.String(200))
   location_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'))
   vendor_partner  = db.Column(db.String(200))
   priority        = db.Column(db.String(20), default='medium') # low, medium, high, critical
   deadline        = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   budget_code     = db.Column(db.String(50))
   status          = db.Column(db.String(20), default='created') # created, in_progress, completed, closed
   tech_spec_file  = db.Column(db.String(255))
   created_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=get_wib)
   created_by_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))

   location    = db.relationship('AssetLocation', backref='projects')
   creator     = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by_id])
   assignments = db.relationship('ProjectAssignment', backref='project', cascade="all, delete-orphan")
   tasks       = db.relationship('ProjectTask', backref='project', cascade="all, delete-orphan")
   activities  = db.relationship('ProjectActivity', backref='project', cascade="all, delete-orphan")
   signatures  = db.relationship('ProjectSignature', backref='project', cascade="all, delete-orphan")

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'ticket_id': getattr(self, 'ticket_id', None), 'name': self.name, 
           'project_owner': self.project_owner,
           'system_name': self.system_name,
           'location': self.location.to_dict() if self.location else None,
           'vendor_partner': self.vendor_partner, 'priority': self.priority,
           'deadline': self.deadline.isoformat() if self.deadline else None,
           'budget_code': self.budget_code, 'status': self.status,
           'tech_spec_file': self.tech_spec_file,
           'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat(),
           'assignments': [a.to_dict() for a in self.assignments],
           'tasks_count': len(self.tasks),
           'tasks_done': len([t for t in self.tasks if t.is_done]),
           'progress_pct': int(len([t for t in self.tasks if t.is_done]) / len(self.tasks) * 100) if self.tasks else 0,
           'attachments': [
               {'id': att.id, 'filename': att.filename, 'file_type': att.file_type} 
               for att in ProjectAttachment.query.filter_by(project_id=self.id).all()
           ],
           'signatures': [s.to_dict() for s in self.signatures] if hasattr(self, 'signatures') else []
       }

class ProjectAssignment(db.Model):
   """Team members assigned to a project."""
   __tablename__ = 'project_assignments'
   id         = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   project_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('projects.id'), nullable=False)
   user_id    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'), nullable=False)
   role       = db.Column(db.String(50), default='member') # lead, member

   user = db.relationship('User', backref='project_assignments')

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'user_id': self.user_id, 'full_name': self.user.full_name,
           'role': self.role, 'username': self.user.username
       }

class ProjectTask(db.Model):
   """Checklist items for a project (To-Do List)."""
   __tablename__ = 'project_tasks'
   id          = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   project_id  = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('projects.id'), nullable=False)
   title       = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
   is_done     = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=False)
   created_at  = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=get_wib)


class ProjectActivity(db.Model):
   """Daily/periodic progress reports submitted by engineers."""
   __tablename__ = 'project_activities'
   id             = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   project_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('projects.id'), nullable=False)
   engineer_id    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   personnel_log  = db.Column(db.Text) # JSON or Comma separated names
   vendor_partner = db.Column(db.String(200)) # Pendamping vendor
   description    = db.Column(db.Text) # Work Description
   tasks_checked  = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]') # JSON list of task IDs
   status         = db.Column(db.String(20), default='pending') # pending, approved, revised
   submitted_at   = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=get_wib)
   approved_by_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   approved_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   manager_notes  = db.Column(db.Text)
   progress_snap  = db.Column(db.Integer, default=0) # Snapshot of progress %
   revisions      = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')

   engineer = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[engineer_id])
   approver = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[approved_by_id])
   attachments = db.relationship('ProjectAttachment', backref='activity', cascade="all, delete-orphan")

   def to_dict(self):
       try:
           rev_data = json.loads(self.revisions) if (hasattr(self, 'revisions') and self.revisions) else []
       except:
           rev_data = []
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'project_id': self.project_id,
           'engineer_name': self.engineer.full_name if self.engineer else 'Unknown',
           'engineer_position': self.engineer.job_position if self.engineer and self.engineer.job_position else '',
           'personnel_log': self.personnel_log, 'description': self.description,
           'status': self.status, 'submitted_at': self.submitted_at.isoformat(),
           'vendor_partner': self.vendor_partner,
           'approved_at': self.approved_at.isoformat() if self.approved_at else None,
           'manager_notes': self.manager_notes,
           'progress_snap': self.progress_snap,
           'attachments': [a.to_dict() for a in self.attachments],
           'revisions': rev_data
       }

class AssetMovement(db.Model):
   """Tracking asset RMA and site-to-site movements/loans."""
   __tablename__ = 'asset_movements'
   id              = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   rma_number      = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)
   movement_type   = db.Column(db.String(50), default='RMA') # RMA, Pinjam Pakai, Transfer
   asset_name      = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
   serial_number   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   from_location   = db.Column(db.String(255))
   to_location     = db.Column(db.String(255))
   reason          = db.Column(db.String(255))
   status          = db.Column(db.String(30), default='Requested') 
   priority        = db.Column(db.String(20), default='Medium')
   description     = db.Column(db.Text)
   
   # Dates for "Pinjam Pakai"
   start_date      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   return_date     = db.Column(db.DateTime) # Estimated Return
   actual_return   = db.Column(db.DateTime) # When it actually came back
   
   created_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
   created_by_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   
   creator         = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by_id])

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'rma_number': self.rma_number, 'asset_name': self.asset_name,
           'movement_type': self.movement_type,
           'serial_number': self.serial_number, 'from_location': self.from_location,
           'to_location': self.to_location, 'reason': self.reason, 'status': self.status,
           'priority': self.priority, 'description': self.description,
           'start_date': self.start_date.isoformat() if self.start_date else None,
           'return_date': self.return_date.isoformat() if self.return_date else None,
           'actual_return': self.actual_return.isoformat() if self.actual_return else None,
           'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat(), 'created_by': self.creator.full_name if self.creator else 'Unknown'
       }

class HandoverTracker(db.Model):
   __tablename__   = 'handover_tracker'
   id              = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   location_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'), nullable=False)
   project_id      = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('projects.id'))
   survey_id       = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('surveys.id'))
   historical_log  = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   
   server_details  = db.Column(db.Text)
   camera_details  = db.Column(db.Text)
   
   checklist_progress = db.Column(db.Text, default='{}')
   physics_data       = db.Column(db.Text, default='{}')
   nuc_setup_details  = db.Column(db.Text, default='{}')
   deployment_details = db.Column(db.Text, default='{}')
   run_assigned_to_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   
   status          = db.Column(db.String(30), default='Draft')
   notes           = db.Column(db.Text)
   
   created_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
   created_by_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))

   build_approved_by_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   run_approved_by_id     = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   manager_approved_by_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   director_approved_by_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   build_approved_at      = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   run_approved_at        = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   manager_approved_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   director_approved_at   = db.Column(db.DateTime)

   location = db.relationship('AssetLocation', backref='handovers')
   project  = db.relationship('Project', backref=db.backref('handover', uselist=False))
   survey   = db.relationship('Survey', backref=db.backref('handover', uselist=False))
   build_approver   = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[build_approved_by_id])
   run_approver     = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[run_approved_by_id])
   manager_approver = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[manager_approved_by_id])
   director_approver = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[director_approved_by_id])
   run_assigned_to   = db.relationship("User", foreign_keys=[run_assigned_to_id])

   def to_dict(self):
      import json
      return {
         'id': self.id,
         'location_name': self.location.name if self.location else 'Unknown',
         'project_name': self.project.name if self.project else 'N/A',
         'server_details': json.loads(self.server_details or '[]'),
         'camera_details': json.loads(self.camera_details or '[]'),
         'checklist': json.loads(self.checklist_progress or '{}'),
         'physics': json.loads(self.physics_data or '{}'),
         'status': self.status,
         'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat(),
         'build_approved_by': self.build_approver.full_name if self.build_approver else None,
         'run_approved_by': self.run_approver.full_name if self.run_approver else None,
         'manager_approved_by': self.manager_approver.full_name if self.manager_approver else None,
      }

class ProjectAttachment(db.Model):
   """Files related to projects or activities."""
   __tablename__ = 'project_attachments'

   def to_dict(self):
       return {
           'id': self.id, 'filename': self.filename, 'file_type': self.file_type,
           'url': f'/static/uploads/{self.filename}', 'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat()
       }

   """Digital e-signatures for project closing (BAST)."""

       return {
           'id': self.id, 'full_name': self.user.full_name if self.user else 'Unknown',
           'job_position': self.user.job_position if self.user else '',
           'role': self.signer_role, 'signed_at': self.signed_at.isoformat()
       }

   uploader = db.relationship('User')

       return {
           'id': self.id, 'location_id': self.location_id,
           'filename': self.filename, 'original_name': self.original_name,
           'file_type': self.file_type, 'uploader': self.uploader.full_name if self.uploader else 'Unknown',
           'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat(),
           'url': f'/static/uploads/{self.filename}'
       }

class PreventiveScheduler(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'preventive_scheduler'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   title         = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
   task_type     = db.Column(db.String(50), default='PM') # PM, Survey, POC, Deployment, etc.
   description   = db.Column(db.Text)
   start_date    = db.Column(db.DateTime, nullable=False)
   end_date      = db.Column(db.DateTime)
   location_id   = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('asset_locations.id'), nullable=True)
   assigned_to   = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]') # JSON list of user IDs
   created_by    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   status        = db.Column(db.String(30), default='Scheduled') # Scheduled, In Progress, Completed, Cancelled
   system_type   = db.Column(db.String(100))
   color         = db.Column(db.String(20), default='#3b82f6')
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

   creator  = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by])
   location = db.relationship('AssetLocation')

   @property
   def assigned_users(self):
      try:
         val = self.assigned_to or '[]'
         if val.startswith('['):
            uids = json.loads(val)
         else:
            uids = [int(val)] if val.isdigit() else []
         return User.query.filter(User.id.in_(uids)).all()
      except: return []

   @property
   def assignee_names_display(self):
      users = self.assigned_users
      if not users: return 'Unassigned'
      return ', '.join([u.full_name or u.username for u in users])

class Survey(db.Model):
   __tablename__ = 'surveys'
   id            = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
   ssr_number    = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)
   version       = db.Column(db.String(10), default='1.0')
   survey_type   = db.Column(db.String(50), default='LPR') # LPR, General
   project_type  = db.Column(db.String(100))
   project_name  = db.Column(db.String(200))
   project_code  = db.Column(db.String(50))
   project_id    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('projects.id'))
   client_name   = db.Column(db.String(200))
   client_pic    = db.Column(db.String(200))
   client_phone  = db.Column(db.String(50))
   client_email  = db.Column(db.String(100))
   company_logo  = db.Column(db.String(500))
   client_logo   = db.Column(db.String(500))
   site_name     = db.Column(db.String(200))
   site_address  = db.Column(db.Text)
   site_city     = db.Column(db.String(100))
   site_gps      = db.Column(db.String(100))
   survey_date   = db.Column(db.Date)
   surveyor_name = db.Column(db.String(200))
   surveyor_role = db.Column(db.String(100))
   status        = db.Column(db.String(50), default='Draft')
   readiness     = db.Column(db.String(20), default='hold')
   form_data     = db.Column(db.Text, default='{}')
   created_by    = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('users.id'))
   created_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
   updated_at    = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
   additional_surveyors = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   attachment_files     = db.Column(db.Text, default='[]')
   
   creator          = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[created_by])
   project          = db.relationship('Project', backref='surveys')
   timeline_entries = db.relationship('SSRTimeline', backref='survey',
                                      lazy='dynamic', cascade='all, delete-orphan',
                                      order_by='SSRTimeline.created_at')
   versions = db.relationship('SurveyVersion', backref='survey',
                              lazy='dynamic', cascade='all, delete-orphan',
                              order_by='SurveyVersion.created_at')

   @property
   def created_wib(self):
       return self.created_at + timedelta(hours=7)

   @property
   def surveyor_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.additional_surveyors or '[]')
       except: return []

   @property
   def attachment_list(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.attachment_files or '[]')
       except: return []

   @property
   def form_data_dict(self):
       try: return json.loads(self.form_data or '{}')
       except: return {}

   @property
   def status_color(self):
       return {'Draft':'gray','In Review':'blue','Approved':'green','Rejected':'red'}.get(self.status,'gray')

   @property
   def readiness_label(self):
       return {'go':'✅ Go','hold':'⚠️ Hold','nogo':'🚫 No-Go'}.get(self.readiness,'—')

       except: return []

       ids = self.assigned_ids
       return User.query.filter(User.id.in_(ids)).all() if ids else []

   def priority_color(self):
       return {'critical':'red','high':'amber','medium':'blue','low':'green'}.get(self.priority,'gray')

   @property
   def status_label(self):
       return {'open':'Open','assigned':'Assigned','in_progress':'In Progress',
               'ssr_created':'SSR Created','closed':'Closed'}.get(self.status, self.status)

@login_manager.user_loader
def load_user(uid): return db.session.get(User, int(uid))

def get_settings():
   s = AppSettings.query.first()
   if not s:
       s = AppSettings(); db.session.add(s); db.session.commit()
   return s

def get_sla_limits():
   s = get_settings()
   return {'critical':s.sla_critical,'high':s.sla_high,'medium':s.sla_medium,'low':s.sla_low}

def gen_ticket_id():
   s = get_settings()
   s.ticket_counter += 1
   db.session.commit()
   now = datetime.utcnow()
   return f"{s.ticket_prefix}-{now.strftime('%Y%m')}-{s.ticket_counter:04d}"

def gen_project_ticket_id():
   s = get_settings()
   s.project_counter = getattr(s, 'project_counter', 0) + 1
   db.session.commit()
   now = datetime.utcnow()
   return f"TKT-BUILD-{now.strftime('%Y%m')}-{s.project_counter:04d}"

def gen_survey_project_code():
   s = get_settings()
   s.survey_counter = getattr(s, 'survey_counter', 0) + 1
   db.session.commit()
   now = datetime.utcnow()
   return f"ASN-PRJ-{now.strftime('%Y%m')}-{s.survey_counter:04d}"

def allowed_file(fn):
   return '.' in fn and fn.rsplit('.',1)[1].lower() in ALLOWED_EXT

def save_file(file, prefix='att'):
   if file and file.filename and allowed_file(file.filename):
       ext  = file.filename.rsplit('.',1)[1].lower()
       name = f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}.{ext}"
       file.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], name))
       return name
   return None

def _save_logo(file):
   if file and file.filename:
       ext = file.filename.rsplit('.',1)[1].lower() if '.' in file.filename else 'png'
       if ext in {'png','jpg','jpeg','svg','webp','gif'}:
           name = f"logo_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.{ext}"
           file.save(os.path.join(UPLOAD_DIR, name))
           return name
   return None

def role_required(*roles):
   def decorator(f):
       @wraps(f)
       def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
           if not current_user.is_authenticated or current_user.role not in roles:
               abort(403)
           return f(*args, **kwargs)
       return decorated
   return decorator

def to_wib(dt):
   """Convert UTC datetime to WIB (UTC+7) aware datetime."""
   if dt is None: return None
   if dt.tzinfo is None:
       dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   return dt.astimezone(WIB)

def build_form_data(req):
    def get_json(key, default='[]'):
        val = req.form.get(key, default)
        try:
            return json.loads(val if val else default)
        except:
            return json.loads(default)

    data = {
        'site_info': {
            'operation_hours':  req.form.get('op_hours',''),
            'visitor_capacity': req.form.get('visitor_cap',''),
            'install_schedule': req.form.get('install_schedule',''),
            'access_notes':     req.form.get('access_notes',''),
        },
        'power': {
            'source':              req.form.get('power_source',''),
            'capacity_va':         req.form.get('power_capacity',''),
            'voltage':             req.form.get('power_voltage',''),
            'distance_from_panel': req.form.get('power_distance',''),
            'cable_condition':     req.form.get('cable_condition',''),
            'ups_available':       req.form.get('ups_available',''),
            'notes':               req.form.get('power_notes',''),
        },
        'network': {
            'connection_type':  req.form.get('net_type',''),
            'isp':              req.form.get('net_isp',''),
            'bandwidth':        req.form.get('net_bandwidth',''),
            'lan_exists':       req.form.get('lan_exists',''),
            'switch_available': req.form.get('switch_avail',''),
            'lte_signal':       req.form.get('lte_signal',''),
            'notes':            req.form.get('net_notes',''),
        },
        'installation_points': get_json('installation_points_json'),
        'environmental': {
            'avg_temp':     req.form.get('env_temp',''),
            'humidity':     req.form.get('env_humidity',''),
            'rain_exposure':req.form.get('rain_exposure',''),
            'dust_level':   req.form.get('dust_level',''),
            'notes':        req.form.get('env_notes',''),
        },
        'civil': {
            'floor_type':      req.form.get('floor_type',''),
            'floor_thickness': req.form.get('floor_thick',''),
            'wall_material':   req.form.get('wall_material',''),
            'drilling_needed': req.form.get('drilling',''),
            'ducting_needed':  req.form.get('ducting',''),
            'notes':           req.form.get('civil_notes',''),
        },
        'safety': {
            'fire_ext':   'fire_ext'    in req.form,
            'signage':    'signage'     in req.form,
            'grounding':  'grounding'   in req.form,
            'safe_area':  'safe_area'   in req.form,
            'permits':    'permits'     in req.form,
            'height_work':'height_work' in req.form,
            'notes':       req.form.get('safety_notes',''),
        },
        'findings': {
            'main_findings':        req.form.get('findings_main',''),
            'risks':                req.form.get('findings_risks',''),
            'recommendations':      req.form.get('findings_recs',''),
            'additional_materials': req.form.get('add_materials',''),
            'prerequisites':        req.form.get('prerequisites',''),
        },
        'lpr_assessment': {
            'gates':               get_json('lpr_gates_json'),
            'infra_elec':          req.form.get('lpr_infra_elec',''),
            'infra_net':           req.form.get('lpr_infra_net',''),
            'infra_sipil':         req.form.get('lpr_infra_sipil',''),
            'env_conditions':      req.form.get('lpr_env_conditions',''),
            'safety_check':        req.form.get('lpr_safety_check',''),
            'findings':            req.form.get('lpr_findings',''),
            'risks':               req.form.get('lpr_risks',''),
            'recommendations':     req.form.get('lpr_recommendations',''),
        },
        'additional_surveyors': get_json('additional_surveyors_json'),
        'signoff': {
            'surveyor': {'name':req.form.get('sign_surveyor_name',''),'role':req.form.get('sign_surveyor_role',''),'date':req.form.get('sign_surveyor_date',''),'signature':req.form.get('sign_surveyor_signature','')},
            'client':   {'name':req.form.get('sign_client_name',''),  'role':req.form.get('sign_client_role',''),  'date':req.form.get('sign_client_date',''),  'signature':req.form.get('sign_client_signature','')},
            'pm':       {'name':req.form.get('sign_pm_name',''),      'role':req.form.get('sign_pm_role',''),      'date':req.form.get('sign_pm_date',''),      'signature':req.form.get('sign_pm_signature','')},
            'reviewer': {'name':req.form.get('sign_rev_name',''),     'role':req.form.get('sign_rev_role',''),     'date':req.form.get('sign_rev_date',''),     'signature':req.form.get('sign_rev_signature','')},
        },
    }
    # Capture all dynamic fields (tech_*)
    for key in req.form:
        if key.startswith('tech_') or key.startswith('lpr_'):
            data[key] = req.form.get(key)
    return data

def fmt_wib(dt, fmt='%d/%m/%Y %H:%M'):
   """Format UTC datetime as WIB string."""
   if dt is None: return '—'
   return to_wib(dt).strftime(fmt)

def gen_ssr():
   s = get_settings()
   s.ssr_counter += 1
   db.session.commit()
   return f"{s.ssr_prefix}/{datetime.utcnow().year}/{s.ssr_counter:04d}"

def save_attachment(file, prefix='att'):
   if file and file.filename:
       ext = file.filename.rsplit('.',1)[1].lower() if '.' in file.filename else ''
       if ext in {'png','jpg','jpeg','gif','webp','pdf'}:
           name = f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.{ext}"
           file.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], name))
           return name, ext
   return None, None


def sync_survey_to_assets(s):
    """Integrate SSR data into Asset Monitoring (AssetLocation & AssetDevice)."""
    try:
        # 1. Update AssetLocation info
        loc = s.project.location if (s.project and s.project.location) else None
        if not loc:
            # Try to find by site_name if not linked to project
            loc = AssetLocation.query.filter_by(name=s.site_name).first()
        
        if loc:
            if s.site_address: loc.address = s.site_address
            if s.site_city: loc.city = s.site_city
            if s.site_gps:
                try:
                    lat, lng = s.site_gps.split(',')
                    loc.lat = float(lat.strip())
                    loc.lng = float(lng.strip())
                except: pass
        
        # 2. Sync Devices from installation_points
        form_data = s.form_data_dict
        points = form_data.get('installation_points', [])
        
        # We only sync if there's a valid location
        if loc and points:
            # To prevent duplicates
            for p in points:
                if not p.get('ip'): continue
                
                existing = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=loc.id, ip_address=p.get('ip')).first()
                if not existing:
                    dev = AssetDevice(
                        location_id=loc.id,
                        device_name=p.get('point_name') or f"{p.get('category','Device')} Point",
                        device_category=p.get('category','other').lower(),
                        device_type=p.get('category','Other'),
                        ip_address=p.get('ip'),
                        username=p.get('username'),
                        password=p.get('password'),
                        notes=f"Synced from SSR {s.ssr_number}. Spec: {p.get('spec')} | Assessment: {p.get('assessment')}"
                    )
                    db.session.add(dev)
                else:
                    # Update existing
                    existing.device_name = p.get('point_name') or existing.device_name
                    existing.username = p.get('username') or existing.username
                    existing.password = p.get('password') or existing.password
                    existing.notes = f"Updated from SSR {s.ssr_number}. Spec: {p.get('spec')} | Assessment: {p.get('assessment')}"
            
            # Update counts
            db.session.flush()
            loc.device_count = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=loc.id).count()
            loc.nuc_count = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=loc.id, device_category='server').count()
            loc.camera_count = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=loc.id, device_category='camera').count()
            
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Sync Error: {e}")
        return False

def sync_survey_to_handover(s):
   """Sync SSR technical details to Handover Tracker."""
   try:
      ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(survey_id=s.id).first()
      if not ho and s.project_id:
         ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(project_id=s.project_id).first()
      if not ho:
          # Create missing tracker
          loc_id = None
          if s.project_id:
             proj = Project.query.get(s.project_id)
             if proj: loc_id = proj.location_id
          
          ho = HandoverTracker(
             project_id=s.project_id,
             survey_id=s.id,
             location_id=loc_id,
             status="Draft",
             checklist_progress="{}"
          )
          db.session.add(ho)
          db.session.flush()
      
      ho.survey_id = s.id
      form_data = s.form_data_dict
      points = form_data.get('installation_points', [])
      
      servers = []
      cameras = []
      for p in points:
         cat = (p.get('device_type') or p.get('category') or '').lower()
         if 'server' in cat or 'nuc' in cat or 'pc' in cat:
            servers.append({
               'ip': p.get('ip'),
               'hostname': p.get('point_name') or p.get('id'),
               'type': p.get('device_type') or p.get('category'),
               'spec': p.get('spec') or p.get('notes'),
               'username': p.get('username'),
               'password': p.get('password')
            })
         elif 'camera' in cat or 'cam' in cat or 'lpr' in cat or 'kamera' in cat:
            cameras.append({
               'gate_name': p.get('point_name') or p.get('location_desc'),
               'ip': p.get('ip'),
               'brand': p.get('brand') or p.get('spec') or p.get('device_type'),
               'fps': '25',
               'username': p.get('username'),
               'password': p.get('password'),
               'assessment': {'note': p.get('notes')},
               'physics': {
                  'cam_to_manless': p.get('cam_to_manless'),
                  'manless_to_boomgate': p.get('manless_to_boomgate')
               }
            })
      
      if servers: ho.server_details = json.dumps(servers)
      if cameras: ho.camera_details = json.dumps(cameras)
      
      db.session.commit()
      return True
   except Exception as e:
      print(f"HO Sync Error: {e}")
      return False

def add_timeline(survey, phase, action, status_from=None, status_to=None, notes=None):
   entry = SSRTimeline(
       survey_id=survey.id, phase=phase, action=action,
       actor_id=current_user.id if current_user.is_authenticated else None,
       actor_name=current_user.full_name or current_user.username if current_user.is_authenticated else 'System',
       status_from=status_from, status_to=status_to, notes=notes
   )
   db.session.add(entry)

def save_survey_version(survey, phase, notes=None):
   """Saves a snapshot of current survey form_data as a new version."""
   count = SurveyVersion.query.filter_by(survey_id=survey.id).count()
   ver_label = f"v{count + 1}"
   
   ver = SurveyVersion(
       survey_id=survey.id,
       version_label=ver_label,
       phase=phase,
       actor_name=current_user.full_name or current_user.username if current_user.is_authenticated else 'System',
       status_at=survey.status,
       review_notes=notes,
       form_snapshot=survey.form_data
   )
   db.session.add(ver)
   return ver

def calc_sla(survey):
   s = get_settings()
   entries = list(survey.timeline_entries)
   def find_first(phases):
       for e in entries:
           if e.phase in phases: return e.created_at
       return None
   def hours_diff(a, b):
       if a and b: return round((b - a).total_seconds() / 3600, 1)
       return None
   created_t = find_first(['create']); submit_t = find_first(['submit'])
   review_t = find_first(['revision','approved','rejected','resubmit'])
   rev_t = find_first(['revision']); resubmit_t = find_first(['resubmit']); done_t = find_first(['approved','rejected'])
   phases = [
       {'name': 'Create → Submit', 'hours': hours_diff(created_t, submit_t), 'target': 24, 'icon': '📤'},
       {'name': 'Submit → Review', 'hours': hours_diff(submit_t, review_t), 'target': 48, 'icon': '🔍'},
       {'name': 'Revisi → Resubmit', 'hours': hours_diff(rev_t, resubmit_t), 'target': 24, 'icon': '📨'}
   ]
   total_h = hours_diff(created_t, done_t)
   return {'phases': phases, 'total_hours': total_h, 'on_time': total_h <= 120 if total_h else None}

def add_notification(user_id, message, link=None):
   if not user_id: return
   db.session.add(Notification(user_id=user_id, message=message, link=link))

def notify_admins(message, link=None):
   for a in User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['admin','lead_engineer'])).all():
       add_notification(a.id, message, link)

def detect_mentions(text):
   """Parse @username from text and return unique user IDs."""
   if not text: return []
   import re
   matches = re.findall(r'@(\w+)', text)
   if not matches: return []
   users = User.query.filter(User.username.in_(matches)).all()
   return [u.id for u in users]

def notify_mentions(text, message, link=None):
   """Notify users mentioned in text."""
   uids = detect_mentions(text)
   for uid in uids:
       if uid != current_user.id: # Don't notify self
           add_notification(uid, message, link)

def telegram_notify_ssr(survey, event_type, notes=None):
   st = get_settings()
   if not st.telegram_enabled or not st.tg_survey_enabled: return
   icons  = {'create':'📝','submit':'📤','revision':'🔄','resubmit':'📨','approved':'✅','rejected':'❌'}
   label = event_type.capitalize()
   msg = f"{icons.get(event_type,'📌')} <b>SSR {label}</b>\n\nNo: {survey.ssr_number}\nSite: {html.escape(survey.site_name or '—')}\nStatus: {survey.status}"
   if notes: msg += f"\nNotes: {html.escape(notes)}"
   send_telegram(msg)

def telegram_notify_scheduler(task, event_type="created"):
    st = get_settings()
    if not st.telegram_enabled or not st.tg_scheduler_enabled: return
    
    icons = {'created': '📅', 'updated': '🔄', 'deleted': '🗑️'}
    status_icons = {'Scheduled': '🟡', 'In Progress': '🔵', 'Completed': '🟢', 'Cancelled': '🔴'}
    title = f"SCHEDULER TASK {event_type.upper()}"
    
    msg = f"<b>{icons.get(event_type, '📌')} {title}</b>\n"
    msg += f"----------------------------\n"
    msg += f"📝 <b>Title:</b> {html.escape(task.title or '-')}\n"
    msg += f"🔧 <b>Type:</b> {task.task_type} ({task.system_type or '-'})\n"
    msg += f"📍 <b>Site:</b> {html.escape(task.location.name if task.location else '-')}\n"
    msg += f"⏰ <b>Date:</b> {task.start_date.strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M')}\n"
    msg += f"🚦 <b>Status:</b> {status_icons.get(task.status, '⚪')} {task.status}\n"
    
    assignees = task.assignee_names_display
    msg += f"👤 <b>Assigned to:</b> {html.escape(assignees or '-')}\n"
    msg += f"----------------------------\n"
    msg += f"🔗 Buka Scheduler: <a href='{url_for('scheduler_view', _external=True)}'>Klik Di Sini</a>\n"
    msg += f"Sistem: <b>ALFABETA ODMS</b>"
    
    send_telegram(msg)

def send_telegram(message):
   """Send message to Telegram group/channel with detailed logging."""
   s = get_settings()
   
   if not s.telegram_token or not s.telegram_chat_id:
       app.logger.warning("[TELEGRAM] DIBATALKAN: Token atau Chat ID belum diisi di Settings!")
       return False
       
   # --- TAMBAHKAN .strip() DI SINI UNTUK MEMBERSIHKAN SPASI TERSEMBUNYI ---
   clean_token = s.telegram_token.strip()
   clean_chat_id = s.telegram_chat_id.strip()
   
   try:
       # Gunakan clean_token dan clean_chat_id
       url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{clean_token}/sendMessage"
       app.logger.info("[TELEGRAM] Menghubungi API Telegram...")
       
       resp = requests.post(url, json={
           'chat_id': clean_chat_id,
           'text': message,
           'parse_mode': 'HTML',
       }, timeout=5)
       
       if resp.status_code != 200:
           app.logger.error(f"[TELEGRAM] GAGAL (Kode {resp.status_code}): {resp.text}")
           return False
           
       app.logger.info("[TELEGRAM] SUKSES: Pesan terkirim!")
       return True
       
   except requests.exceptions.Timeout:
       app.logger.error("[TELEGRAM] GAGAL: Koneksi Timeout.")
       return False
   except Exception as e:
       app.logger.error(f"[TELEGRAM] ERROR FATAL: {str(e)}")
       return False


def send_telegram_to_user(phone_number, message):
   """Send Telegram message directly to a user's phone number (via username/chat_id)."""
   s = get_settings()
   if not s.telegram_token:
       app.logger.warning("[TELEGRAM-USER] Token belum diisi!")
       return False
   # phone_number can be numeric chat_id or @username
   target = phone_number.strip()
   if not target:
       return False
   # If it's a phone number without +, add +
   if target.lstrip('+').isdigit() and not target.startswith('+'):
       target = '+' + target
   clean_token = s.telegram_token.strip()
   try:
       url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{clean_token}/sendMessage"
       resp = requests.post(url, json={
           'chat_id': target,
           'text': message,
           'parse_mode': 'HTML',
       }, timeout=5)
       if resp.status_code != 200:
           app.logger.error(f"[TELEGRAM-USER] GAGAL ke {target}: {resp.text}")
           return False
       app.logger.info(f"[TELEGRAM-USER] SUKSES ke {target}")
       return True
   except Exception as e:
       app.logger.error(f"[TELEGRAM-USER] ERROR: {str(e)}")
       return False

# Jinja2 template globals
@app.context_processor
def inject_globals():
   def dropdown_id(category, value):
       opt = DropdownOption.query.filter_by(category=category, value=value).first()
       return opt.id if opt else 0
   return {'dropdown_id': dropdown_id}

# Jinja2 filters
@app.template_filter('wib')
def wib_filter(dt, fmt='%d/%m/%Y %H:%M WIB'):
   return fmt_wib(dt, fmt) if dt else '—'

@app.template_filter('wib_full')
def wib_full_filter(dt):
   return fmt_wib(dt, '%A, %d %B %Y %H:%M WIB') if dt else '—'

@app.template_filter('duration')
def duration_filter(minutes):
   if minutes is None: return '—'
   if minutes < 60: return f'{minutes}m'
   h = minutes // 60; m = minutes % 60
   return f'{h}j {m}m' if m else f'{h}j'

#  AUTH ROUTES
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.route('/login', methods=['GET','POST'])
def login():
   if current_user.is_authenticated:
       if request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest' or request.is_json:
           return jsonify({'success': True, 'redirect': url_for('dashboard')})
       return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
   
   error = None
   if request.method == 'POST':
       if request.is_json:
           data = request.json
           un = data.get('username', '').strip()
           pw = data.get('password', '')
           remember = data.get('remember', False)
       else:
           un = request.form.get('username','').strip()
           pw = request.form.get('password','')
           remember = 'remember' in request.form

       user = User.query.filter((User.username == un) | (User.email == un)).first()
       if user and user.is_active_acc and user.check_password(pw):
           user.last_login = datetime.utcnow()
           db.session.commit()
           session.permanent = True 
           login_user(user, remember=remember)
           
           if request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest' or request.is_json:
               return jsonify({'success': True, 'redirect': request.args.get('next') or url_for('dashboard')})
           return redirect(request.args.get('next') or url_for('dashboard'))
       
       error = 'Username atau password salah.'
       if request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest' or request.is_json:
           return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': error})

   return render_template('login.html', error=error)

@app.route('/logout')
@login_required
def logout():
   logout_user(); return redirect(url_for('login'))

@app.route('/health')
def health(): return jsonify({'status':'ok','time':fmt_wib(datetime.utcnow())})

#  DASHBOARD
