@app.route('/')
@login_required
def dashboard():
   settings = get_settings()
   now      = datetime.utcnow()
   today    = to_wib(now).date()

   total      = Ticket.query.count()
   open_t     = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='open').count()
   inprog_t   = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='in_progress').count()
   monitor_t  = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='monitoring').count()
   resolved_t = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='resolved').count()
   closed_t   = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='closed').count()

   # Today count in WIB
   today_start_utc = datetime.combine(today, datetime.min.time()) - timedelta(hours=7)
   today_count = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.created_at >= today_start_utc).count()

   critical_open   = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='open', priority='critical').count()
   active_tickets  = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.status.notin_(['closed'])).all()
   breached        = [t for t in active_tickets if t.sla_breached]
   sla_ok          = len(active_tickets) - len(breached)
   sla_pct         = round((sla_ok / max(len(active_tickets),1)) * 100)

   from sqlalchemy import func
   prio_data = db.session.query(Ticket.priority, func.count(Ticket.id)).group_by(Ticket.priority).all()
   site_data = db.session.query(Ticket.site, func.count(Ticket.id)).group_by(Ticket.site)\
       .order_by(func.count(Ticket.id).desc()).limit(10).all()

   # ── Trend Filtering ──
   trend_type = request.args.get('trend_type', 'week')
   trend_from = request.args.get('trend_from', '')
   trend_to   = request.args.get('trend_to', '')
   trend      = []
   trend_lbl  = "7 Hari"

   if trend_type == 'day':
       trend_lbl = "24 Jam Terakhir"
       from datetime import datetime as dt_class
       now_utc = dt_class.utcnow()
       for i in range(23, -1, -1):
           h_start = now_utc - timedelta(hours=i+1)
           h_end   = now_utc - timedelta(hours=i)
           cnt = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.created_at.between(h_start, h_end)).count()
           h_wib = (to_wib(h_end)).strftime('%H:00')
           trend.append({'date': h_wib, 'count': cnt})
   elif trend_type == 'month':
       trend_lbl = "30 Hari Terakhir"
       for i in range(29, -1, -1):
           d = today - timedelta(days=i)
           ds = datetime.combine(d, datetime.min.time()) - timedelta(hours=7)
           de = ds + timedelta(days=1)
           cnt = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.created_at.between(ds, de)).count()
           trend.append({'date': d.strftime('%d/%m'), 'count': cnt})
   elif trend_type == 'range' and trend_from and trend_to:
       try:
           df = datetime.strptime(trend_from, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
           dt = datetime.strptime(trend_to, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
           days_diff = (dt - df).days
           trend_lbl = f"{df.strftime('%d/%m')} - {dt.strftime('%d/%m')}"
           if days_diff > 90: days_diff = 90
           for i in range(days_diff + 1):
               d = df + timedelta(days=i)
               ds = datetime.combine(d, datetime.min.time()) - timedelta(hours=7)
               de = ds + timedelta(days=1)
               cnt = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.created_at.between(ds, de)).count()
               trend.append({'date': d.strftime('%d/%m'), 'count': cnt})
       except:
           trend_type = 'week'
   
   if trend_type == 'week' or not trend:
       trend_lbl = "7 Hari Terakhir"
       for i in range(6, -1, -1):
           d = today - timedelta(days=i)
           ds = datetime.combine(d, datetime.min.time()) - timedelta(hours=7)
           de = ds + timedelta(days=1)
           cnt = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.created_at.between(ds, de)).count()
           trend.append({'date': d.strftime('%d/%m'), 'count': cnt})

   recent    = Ticket.query.order_by(Ticket.created_at.desc()).limit(10).all()
   pending_val = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='resolved').count() if (current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer) else 0

   # ── Outstanding Tasks Collection ──
   outstanding = []
    
   # 1. Tickets assigned to me that need progress
   if current_user.role in ['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer']:
       my_tasks = Ticket.query.filter(
           Ticket.assigned_to == current_user.id,
           Ticket.status.in_(['open', 'reopened'])
       ).all()
       for t in my_tasks:
           outstanding.append({
               'type': 'Ticket Assign',
               'title': f"Tiket {t.ticket_id}: {t.issue_desc[:40]}...",
               'link': url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=t.id),
               'status': t.status.upper()
           })

   # 2. Tickets waiting for validation (Admin/Lead)
   if current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer:
       v_tasks = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='resolved').all()
       for t in v_tasks:
           outstanding.append({
               'type': 'Waiting Validate',
               'title': f"Tiket {t.ticket_id} (Resolved) - {t.site}",
               'link': url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=t.id),
               'status': 'RESOLVED'
           })

   # 3. BA signatures/approval
   if current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer:
       ba_tasks = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(status='pending').all()
       for ba in ba_tasks:
           # Dynamic route based on BA type
           ba_route = 'ba_troubleshoot_detail'
           if ba.ba_type == 'optimasi': ba_route = 'ba_optimasi_detail'
           elif ba.ba_type == 'live': ba_route = 'ba_live_detail'
           
           outstanding.append({
               'type': 'BA Approval',
               'title': f"BA {ba.ba_number}: {ba.perihal}",
               'link': url_for(ba_route, baid=ba.id),
               'status': 'PENDING'
           })

   # 4. Survey Submissions (SSR)
   if current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer:
       # SSR status for approval is 'In Review'
       sv_tasks = Survey.query.filter_by(status='In Review').all()
       for sv in sv_tasks:
           outstanding.append({
               'type': 'SSR Approval',
               'title': f"SSR {sv.ssr_number if hasattr(sv, 'ssr_number') else sv.id}: {sv.site_name}",
               'link': url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=sv.id),
               'status': 'IN REVIEW'
           })
            
   # 5. Mentions (Unread notifications with 'mention' in message)
   mentions = Notification.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id, is_read=False)\
               .filter(Notification.message.like('%mention%')).all()
   for m in mentions:
       outstanding.append({
           'type': 'Mention',
           'title': m.message,
           'link': m.link,
           'status': 'UNREAD'
       })

   # 6. Preventive Maintenance Schedules (Assigned to me)
   if current_user.role in ['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer']:
       pm_tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter_by(status='Scheduled').all()
       for pm in pm_tasks:
           try:
               assignees = json.loads(pm.assigned_to or '[]')
               if current_user.id in assignees:
                   outstanding.append({
                       'type': 'PM Schedule',
                       'title': f"PM: {pm.title}",
                       'link': url_for('scheduler_view'), # Link to the calendar/list
                       'status': 'SCHEDULED'
                   })
           except: pass

   # 7. Project Assignments (Assigned to me)
   if current_user.role in ['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer']:
       # Fetch projects where I am assigned and status is not closed/completed
       proj_assigns = ProjectAssignment.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id).all()
       for pa in proj_assigns:
           proj = pa.project
           if proj and proj.status not in ['closed', 'completed']:
               outstanding.append({
                   'type': 'Project Assign',
                   'title': f"Project: {proj.name}",
                   'link': url_for('project_detail', pid=proj.id),
                   'status': proj.status.upper()
               })

   my_tickets = []
   if current_user.is_run:
       my_tickets = Ticket.query.filter(
           Ticket.assigned_to == current_user.id,
           Ticket.status.notin_(['closed'])
       ).order_by(Ticket.priority.desc(), Ticket.created_at).limit(5).all()

   engineers = []
   if current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer:
       ops = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer'])).all()
       for eng in ops:
           closed = Ticket.query.filter_by(assigned_to=eng.id, status='closed').all()
           if closed:
               avg = sum(t.mttr_minutes or 0 for t in closed) / len(closed)
               engineers.append({'name': eng.full_name or eng.username,
                                 'closed': len(closed), 'avg_mttr': round(avg)})
       engineers.sort(key=lambda x: x['avg_mttr'])

   # ── Analytics Summary (filterable by date range) ──────
   from collections import Counter
   ana_date_from = request.args.get('ana_date_from', '')
   ana_date_to   = request.args.get('ana_date_to', '')
   ana_qry = Ticket.query
   if ana_date_from:
       try:
           df = datetime.strptime(ana_date_from, '%Y-%m-%d') - timedelta(hours=7)
           ana_qry = ana_qry.filter(Ticket.created_at >= df)
       except ValueError:
           pass
   if ana_date_to:
       try:
           dt2 = datetime.strptime(ana_date_to, '%Y-%m-%d') + timedelta(days=1) - timedelta(hours=7)
           ana_qry = ana_qry.filter(Ticket.created_at < dt2)
       except ValueError:
           pass
   all_tickets_summary = ana_qry.all()
   ticket_ids_filtered = [t.id for t in all_tickets_summary]
   all_updates_summary = TicketUpdate.query.filter(
       TicketUpdate.ticket_id_fk.in_(ticket_ids_filtered)
   ).all() if ticket_ids_filtered else []

   # Dampak Masalah
   impact_counter = Counter()
   for t in all_tickets_summary:
       for imp in t.impact_list:
           if imp: impact_counter[imp] += 1
   top_impacts = impact_counter.most_common(10)

   # Root Symptom
   symptom_counter = Counter()
   for upd in all_updates_summary:
       for sym in upd.root_symptoms_list:
           if sym: symptom_counter[sym] += 1
   top_symptoms = symptom_counter.most_common(10)

   # Tindakan
   action_counter = Counter()
   for upd in all_updates_summary:
       for act in upd.actions_list:
           if act: action_counter[act] += 1
   top_actions = action_counter.most_common(10)

   return render_template('dashboard.html',
       settings=settings, now_wib=to_wib(now),
       total=total, open_t=open_t, inprog_t=inprog_t,
       monitor_t=monitor_t, resolved_t=resolved_t, closed_t=closed_t,
       today_count=today_count, critical_open=critical_open,
       breached_count=len(breached), sla_pct=sla_pct,
       prio_data=json.dumps([{'label':p[0],'count':p[1]} for p in prio_data]),
       site_data=json.dumps([{'label':s[0] or 'Unknown','count':s[1]} for s in site_data]),
       trend_data=json.dumps(trend),
       recent=recent, my_tickets=my_tickets,
       pending_val=pending_val, engineers=engineers,
       top_impacts=top_impacts, top_symptoms=top_symptoms, top_actions=top_actions,
       ana_date_from=ana_date_from, ana_date_to=ana_date_to,
       ana_ticket_count=len(all_tickets_summary),
       outstanding=outstanding)


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  TICKET ROUTES
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.route('/tickets')
@login_required
def tickets():
   settings = get_settings()
   # Basic filters
   q        = request.args.get('q','')
   status_f = request.args.get('status','')
   prio_f   = request.args.get('priority','')
   site_f   = request.args.get('site','')
   mine_f   = request.args.get('mine','')
   # Column-level filters
   tid_f    = request.args.get('f_tid','')
   device_f = request.args.get('f_device','')
   eng_f    = request.args.get('f_eng','')
   # Date range (WIB input → convert to UTC)
   date_from = request.args.get('date_from','')
   date_to   = request.args.get('date_to','')

   qry = Ticket.query
   if current_user.is_user_role:
       # User hanya melihat tiket dari lokasi (site) miliknya sendiri
       if current_user.site:
           qry = qry.filter(Ticket.site == current_user.site)
       else:
           qry = qry.filter_by(created_by=current_user.id)
   if q:
       like = f'%{q}%'
       qry  = qry.filter(db.or_(Ticket.ticket_id.ilike(like), Ticket.site.ilike(like),
                                 Ticket.issue_desc.ilike(like), Ticket.nama_pelapor.ilike(like)))
   if status_f: qry = qry.filter_by(status=status_f)
   if prio_f:   qry = qry.filter_by(priority=prio_f)
   if site_f:   qry = qry.filter_by(site=site_f)
   if tid_f:    qry = qry.filter(Ticket.ticket_id.ilike(f'%{tid_f}%'))
   if device_f: qry = qry.filter(Ticket.device_type.ilike(f'%{device_f}%'))
   if eng_f:
       qry = qry.join(Ticket.assignee, isouter=True).filter(User.full_name.ilike(f'%{eng_f}%'))
   
   if date_from:
       try:
           from datetime import datetime, timedelta
           df_utc = datetime.strptime(date_from, '%Y-%m-%d') - timedelta(hours=7)
           qry = qry.filter(Ticket.created_at >= df_utc)
       except: pass
   if date_to:
       try:
           from datetime import datetime, timedelta
           dt_utc = datetime.strptime(date_to, '%Y-%m-%d') - timedelta(hours=7) + timedelta(days=1)
           qry = qry.filter(Ticket.created_at < dt_utc)
       except: pass

   if mine_f and current_user.is_run:
       qry = qry.filter_by(assigned_to=current_user.id)

   all_tickets = qry.order_by(
       db.case({'critical':0,'high':1,'medium':2,'low':3}, value=Ticket.priority),
       Ticket.created_at.desc()
   ).all()

   total_count = Ticket.query.count() # Definisikan total_count
   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'lead_engineer']), User.is_active_acc==True).all()
   sites     = DropdownOption.get('sites')
   return render_template('tickets.html',
       settings=settings, 
       tickets=all_tickets,
       total_count=total_count, # Kirim ke template
       q=q, status_f=status_f, prio_f=prio_f, site_f=site_f, mine_f=mine_f,
       tid_f=tid_f, device_f=device_f, eng_f=eng_f, # Variabel baru v4
       date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,         # Variabel baru v4
       urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS, 
       sites=sites, 
       engineers=engineers)

@app.route('/tickets/new', methods=['GET','POST'])
@login_required
def ticket_new():
   settings     = get_settings()
   sites        = DropdownOption.get('sites')
   device_types = DropdownOption.get('device_types')
   impacts      = DropdownOption.get('impacts')
   divisions    = DropdownOption.get('divisions')

   if request.method == 'POST':
       urgency      = request.form.get('urgency','medium')
       sla_lim      = get_sla_limits()
       sla_m        = sla_lim.get(urgency, 480)

       device_type  = request.form.get('device_type','')
       if device_type == 'Other':
           device_type = 'Other: ' + request.form.get('device_type_other','')

       gate_sel = request.form.get('gate_area', '')  # plain text now

       impacts_sel  = request.form.getlist('impact')
       impact_other = request.form.get('impact_other','')
       if impact_other:
           impacts_sel.append('Other: ' + impact_other)

       files = request.files.getlist('attachments')
       saved = [fn for fn in (save_file(f,'tkt') for f in files) if fn]
       
       # Handle pasted images sent as base64 hidden inputs
       import base64 as _b64
       for key, val in request.form.items():
           if key.startswith('pasted_image_') and val.startswith('data:image/'):
               try:
                   header, data = val.split(',', 1)
                   ext = header.split(';')[0].split('/')[-1] or 'png'
                   raw = _b64.b64decode(data)
                   fname = f"paste_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}.{ext}"
                   fpath = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname)
                   with open(fpath, 'wb') as fout:
                       fout.write(raw)
                   saved.append(fname)
               except Exception:
                   pass
       
       created_at_val = request.form.get('created_at')
       if created_at_val:
           try:
               dt_local = datetime.strptime(created_at_val, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M')
               created_at_utc = dt_local - timedelta(hours=7)
           except:
               created_at_utc = datetime.utcnow()
       else:
           created_at_utc = datetime.utcnow()

       # Automatic Location Association
       site_name = request.form.get('site','')
       loc = AssetLocation.query.filter_by(name=site_name).first()
       loc_id = loc.id if loc else None

       ticket = Ticket(
           ticket_id        = gen_ticket_id(),
           nama_pelapor     = request.form.get('nama_pelapor',''),
           divisi_pelapor   = request.form.get('divisi_pelapor',''),
           site             = site_name,
           location_id      = loc_id,
           gate_area        = gate_sel,  # plain text
           device_type      = device_type,
           issue_desc       = request.form.get('issue_desc',''),
           impact           = json.dumps(impacts_sel),
           impact_other     = impact_other,
           urgency          = urgency,
           priority         = urgency,
           status           = 'open',
           created_by       = current_user.id,
           created_at       = created_at_utc,
           sla_deadline     = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=sla_m),
           attachment_files = json.dumps(saved),
       )
       db.session.add(ticket)
       db.session.flush()  # get ticket.id

       upd = TicketUpdate(
           ticket_id_fk = ticket.id, engineer_id = current_user.id,
           old_status=None, new_status='open',
           note=f'Tiket dibuat oleh {current_user.full_name or current_user.username}'
                + (f' ({ticket.nama_pelapor})' if ticket.nama_pelapor else ''),
       )
       db.session.add(upd)
       db.session.commit()
       notify_mentions(ticket.issue_desc, f"Anda dimention dalam tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for("ticket_detail", ticket_id=ticket.id))
       db.session.commit()

       # Telegram notification
       if settings.telegram_enabled and settings.tg_run_enabled:
           msg = (
               f"🎫 <b>Tiket Baru Masuk!</b>\n"
               f"🆔 <b>{ticket.ticket_id}</b>\n"
               f"📍 <b>Site:</b> {html.escape(ticket.site or '—')} — {html.escape(ticket.gate_area_display)}\n"
               f"⚙️ <b>Device:</b> {html.escape(ticket.device_type or '—')}\n"
               f"🔴 <b>Urgency:</b> {ticket.urgency.upper()}\n"
               f"📝 <b>Issue:</b> {html.escape(ticket.issue_desc[:120])}{'...' if len(ticket.issue_desc)>120 else ''}\n"
               f"👤 <b>Reporter:</b> {html.escape(ticket.nama_pelapor or current_user.full_name or '—')}\n"
               f"🕐 <b>SLA Deadline:</b> {fmt_wib(ticket.sla_deadline)}\n"
               f"🔗 Segera cek dashboard ODMS!"
           )
           send_telegram(msg)

       return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))

   default_site = current_user.site if current_user.is_user_role else ''
   return render_template('ticket_form.html',
       settings=settings, mode='new', ticket=None,
       device_types=device_types, impacts=impacts,
       urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS, sites=sites,
       divisions=divisions,
       default_site=default_site,
       default_nama=current_user.full_name or '',
       default_divisi=current_user.divisi or '')


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>')
@login_required
def ticket_detail(ticket_id):
   settings  = get_settings()
   ticket    = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   if current_user.is_user_role:
       # Allow access if creator OR if operation created it for user's site
       op_ids = [u.id for u in User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'lead_engineer'])).all()]
       is_own = ticket.created_by == current_user.id
       is_site_ticket = (ticket.site == current_user.site and ticket.created_by in op_ids)
       if not (is_own or is_site_ticket):
           abort(403)
   updates   = ticket.updates.order_by(TicketUpdate.created_at.asc()).all()
   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'lead_engineer']), User.is_active_acc==True).all()

   # Build allowed next statuses based on current status + role
   allowed_statuses = _allowed_statuses(ticket.status, current_user.role)

   return render_template('ticket_detail.html',
       settings=settings, ticket=ticket, updates=updates,
       engineers=engineers, root_symptoms=ROOT_SYMPTOMS,
       action_checks=ACTION_CHECKS, allowed_statuses=allowed_statuses,
       urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS)


def _allowed_statuses(current_status, role):
   """Enforce sequential flow. Closed only allowed after Resolved."""
   if role in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
       # Admin can do anything
       return [s for s in STATUS_FLOW]
   # Operation
   flow_map = {
       'open':        ['open','in_progress'],
       'in_progress': ['in_progress','monitoring','resolved'],
       'monitoring':  ['monitoring','in_progress','resolved'],
       'resolved':    ['resolved'],     # only admin can close
       'reopened':    ['reopened','in_progress'],
   }
   return flow_map.get(current_status, [current_status])


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/update', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
@role_required('run','admin','lead_engineer')
def ticket_update(ticket_id):
   ticket     = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   old_status = ticket.status
   new_status = request.form.get('status', ticket.status)

   # ── Validate solution mandatory for Resolved ──
   if new_status == 'resolved':
       st = request.form.get('short_term_sol','').strip()
       lt = request.form.get('long_term_sol','').strip()
       if not st or not lt:
           # Return back with error
           engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'lead_engineer']), User.is_active_acc==True).all()
           allowed   = _allowed_statuses(old_status, current_user.role)
           from flask import get_flashed_messages
           return render_template('ticket_detail.html',
               settings=get_settings(), ticket=ticket,
               updates=ticket.updates.order_by(TicketUpdate.created_at.asc()).all(),
               engineers=engineers, root_symptoms=ROOT_SYMPTOMS,
               action_checks=ACTION_CHECKS, allowed_statuses=allowed,
               urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS,
               sol_error='Short Term Solution dan Long Term Solution wajib diisi sebelum mengubah status ke Resolved.',
               form_data=request.form), 400

   # Validate status transition
   allowed = _allowed_statuses(old_status, current_user.role)
   if new_status not in allowed:
       new_status = old_status  # reject invalid

   # Urgency correction (operation can change)
   new_urgency = request.form.get('urgency_corrected','').strip()
   urgency_changed = False
   if new_urgency and new_urgency in URGENCY_LEVELS and new_urgency != ticket.priority:
       urgency_changed = True
       ticket.urgency  = new_urgency
       ticket.priority = new_urgency
       # Recalculate SLA if ticket not yet responded
       if old_status in ('open',):
           sla_m = get_sla_limits().get(new_urgency, 480)
           ticket.sla_deadline = ticket.created_at + timedelta(minutes=sla_m)

   assign_id = request.form.get('assigned_to','')
   if assign_id:
       new_assignee = int(assign_id)
       if new_assignee != ticket.assigned_to:
           ticket.assigned_to = new_assignee
           add_notification(new_assignee, f"Anda ditugaskan pada tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
       if not ticket.response_at:
           ticket.response_at = datetime.utcnow()

   files = request.files.getlist('attachments')
   saved = [fn for fn in (save_file(f,'upd') for f in files) if fn]

   note = request.form.get('note','')
   if urgency_changed:
       note = f'[Urgency dikoreksi: {old_status} → {new_urgency.upper()}] ' + note

   upd = TicketUpdate(
       ticket_id_fk       = ticket.id,
       engineer_id        = current_user.id,
       old_status         = old_status,
       new_status         = new_status,
       urgency_corrected  = new_urgency if urgency_changed else None,
       root_symptoms      = json.dumps(request.form.getlist('root_symptoms')),
       technical_analysis = request.form.get('technical_analysis',''),
       actions_taken      = json.dumps(request.form.getlist('actions_taken')),
       actions_detail     = request.form.get('actions_detail',''),
       short_term_sol     = request.form.get('short_term_sol',''),
       long_term_sol      = request.form.get('long_term_sol',''),
       mitigation_plan    = request.form.get('mitigation_plan',''),
       attachments        = json.dumps(saved),
       note               = note,
   )
   db.session.add(upd)

   # Mentions
   notify_mentions(upd.technical_analysis, f"Anda dimention dalam update tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
   notify_mentions(upd.actions_detail, f"Anda dimention dalam update tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
   notify_mentions(upd.note, f"Anda dimention dalam update tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))

   ticket.status     = new_status
   ticket.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()

   if new_status in ('in_progress',) and not ticket.response_at:
       ticket.response_at = datetime.utcnow()
   if new_status in ('resolved',) and not ticket.resolved_at:
       ticket.resolved_at = datetime.utcnow()
       notify_admins(f"📥 Tiket {ticket.ticket_id} telah di-Resolve dan menunggu validasi", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))

   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/validate', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def ticket_validate(ticket_id):
   ticket = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   action = request.form.get('action')
   note   = request.form.get('validation_note','')

   if action == 'approve':
       ticket.status       = 'closed'
       ticket.closed_at    = datetime.utcnow()
       ticket.validated_by = current_user.id
       ticket.validation_note = note
       upd_note = f'✅ Disetujui oleh {current_user.full_name or current_user.username}. {note}'
       add_notification(ticket.assigned_to, f"Tiket {ticket.ticket_id} telah disetujui (Closed)", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
       add_notification(ticket.created_by, f"Tiket {ticket.ticket_id} yang Anda lapor telah disetujui (Closed)", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
   elif action == 'revise':
       ticket.status          = 'in_progress'
       ticket.validated_by    = current_user.id
       ticket.validation_note = note
       ticket.resolved_at     = None
       upd_note = f'🔄 Perlu Revisi: {note}'
       add_notification(ticket.assigned_to, f"Tiket {ticket.ticket_id} perlu revisi: {note}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
   elif action == 'reopen':
       ticket.status       = 'reopened'
       ticket.resolved_at  = None
       ticket.closed_at    = None
       ticket.created_at   = datetime.utcnow()
       sla_m = get_sla_limits().get(ticket.priority, 480)
       ticket.sla_deadline = ticket.created_at + timedelta(minutes=sla_m)
       upd_note = f'🔄 Dibuka kembali oleh {current_user.full_name or current_user.username}. {note}'
   else:
       return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))

   db.session.add(TicketUpdate(
       ticket_id_fk=ticket.id, engineer_id=current_user.id,
       old_status=ticket.status, new_status=ticket.status, note=upd_note))
   
   # Notifikasi mention di dalam catatan validasi
   notify_mentions(note, f"Anda dimention dalam validasi tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
   
   db.session.commit()

   # ── Notifikasi Telegram ke group saat tiket di-close (approve) ──
   if action == 'approve':
       st = get_settings()
       if st.telegram_enabled:
           engineer_name = ticket.assignee.full_name if ticket.assignee else '—'
           base_url = st.base_url.rstrip('/') if st.base_url else f"{request.scheme}://{request.host}"
           pdf_url = f"{base_url}{url_for('ticket_pdf', ticket_id=ticket.id)}"
           closed_msg = (
               f"✅ <b>Tiket Telah Ditutup (Closed)</b>\n\n"
               f"🆔 <b>No. Tiket:</b> {html.escape(ticket.ticket_id)}\n"
               f"📍 <b>Site:</b> {html.escape(ticket.site or '—')}\n"
               f"⚙️ <b>Masalah:</b> {html.escape(ticket.issue_desc[:120])}{'...' if len(ticket.issue_desc or '')>120 else ''}\n"
               f"👷 <b>Engineer:</b> {html.escape(engineer_name)}\n"
               f"🕐 <b>Closed:</b> {fmt_wib(ticket.closed_at)}\n"
               f"📄 <b>Laporan PDF:</b> {pdf_url}"
           )
           send_telegram(closed_msg)

   return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/assign', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
@role_required('run','admin','lead_engineer')
def ticket_assign(ticket_id):
   ticket = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   eng_id = request.form.get('engineer_id')
   if eng_id:
       ticket.assigned_to = int(eng_id)
       ticket.status = 'in_progress'
       add_notification(ticket.assigned_to, f"Anda ditugaskan pada tiket {ticket.ticket_id}", url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))
       if not ticket.response_at:
           ticket.response_at = datetime.utcnow()
       eng = db.session.get(User, int(eng_id))
       db.session.add(TicketUpdate(
           ticket_id_fk=ticket.id, engineer_id=current_user.id,
           old_status='open', new_status='in_progress',
           note=f'Assigned to {eng.full_name or eng.username}'))
       db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/escalate', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def ticket_escalate(ticket_id):
   ticket = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   note   = request.form.get('note','')
   prio_order = ['low','medium','high','critical']
   idx    = prio_order.index(ticket.priority) if ticket.priority in prio_order else 1
   if idx < len(prio_order)-1:
       ticket.priority = prio_order[idx+1]
       ticket.urgency  = ticket.priority
       sla_m = get_sla_limits().get(ticket.priority, 480)
       ticket.sla_deadline = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=sla_m)
   db.session.add(TicketUpdate(
       ticket_id_fk=ticket.id, engineer_id=current_user.id,
       note=f'🚨 ESCALATED to {ticket.priority.upper()}. {note}'))
   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('ticket_detail', ticket_id=ticket.id))

def ticket_pdf(ticket_id):
   settings = get_settings()
   ticket   = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   if current_user.is_user_role:
       # Allow access if creator OR if operation created it for user's site

       is_own = ticket.created_by == current_user.id
       is_site_ticket = (ticket.site == current_user.site and ticket.created_by in op_ids)

                          fmt_wib=fmt_wib)


@app.route('/kpi-team')
@login_required
@role_required('admin', 'lead_engineer')
def kpi_team():
   """KPI Team — comprehensive RUN Team performance metrics."""
   from sqlalchemy import func
   settings = get_settings()
   now      = datetime.utcnow()
   today    = to_wib(now).date()

   # Date range filter (default: last 30 days)
   period   = request.args.get('period', '30')
   try:    days_back = int(period)
   except: days_back = 30

   since_utc = now - timedelta(days=days_back)

   # All engineers (operation role) termasuk Lead Engineer
   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer'])).all()

   kpi_data = []
   for eng in engineers:
       # Tickets handled in period
       assigned = Ticket.query.filter(
           Ticket.assigned_to == eng.id,
           Ticket.created_at >= since_utc
       ).all()

       total_assigned = len(assigned)
       closed_t = [t for t in assigned if t.status == 'closed']
       resolved_t = [t for t in assigned if t.status in ('resolved','closed')]

       # MTTR — only from closed tickets with both created_at & resolved_at
       mttr_values = [t.mttr_minutes for t in closed_t if t.mttr_minutes is not None]
       avg_mttr = round(sum(mttr_values) / len(mttr_values)) if mttr_values else None
       min_mttr = min(mttr_values) if mttr_values else None
       max_mttr = max(mttr_values) if mttr_values else None

       # SLA compliance (closed tickets on time)
       on_time  = sum(1 for t in closed_t if not t.sla_breached)
       sla_pct  = round((on_time / len(closed_t)) * 100) if closed_t else 0

       # Response time (time from ticket created to first response)
       resp_times = []
       for t in assigned:
           if t.response_at and t.created_at:
               rt = (t.response_at - t.created_at).total_seconds() / 60
               resp_times.append(rt)
       avg_response = round(sum(resp_times) / len(resp_times)) if resp_times else None

       # Priority breakdown
       prio_breakdown = {'critical':0,'high':0,'medium':0,'low':0}
       for t in assigned:
           prio_breakdown[t.priority or 'medium'] = prio_breakdown.get(t.priority or 'medium', 0) + 1

       # SLA breach count
       breached = sum(1 for t in closed_t if t.sla_breached)

       # Monthly trend (last 4 weeks)
       monthly = []
       for w in range(3,-1,-1):
           w_start = since_utc + timedelta(days=(days_back//4)*w)
           w_end   = w_start + timedelta(days=days_back//4)
           wc = Ticket.query.filter(
               Ticket.assigned_to == eng.id,
               Ticket.status == 'closed',
               Ticket.closed_at.between(w_start, w_end)
           ).count()
           monthly.append(wc)

       kpi_data.append({
           'engineer':       eng,
           'total_assigned': total_assigned,
           'closed':         len(closed_t),
           'resolved':       len(resolved_t),
           'avg_mttr':       avg_mttr,
           'min_mttr':       min_mttr,
           'max_mttr':       max_mttr,
           'sla_pct':        sla_pct,
           'on_time':        on_time,
           'breached':       breached,
           'avg_response':   avg_response,
           'prio_breakdown': prio_breakdown,
           'monthly':        monthly,
       })

   # Sort by closed tickets desc
   kpi_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['closed'], reverse=True)

   # Team overall
   all_closed = Ticket.query.filter(
       Ticket.status == 'closed',
       Ticket.created_at >= since_utc
   ).all()
   team_mttr_vals = [t.mttr_minutes for t in all_closed if t.mttr_minutes is not None]
   team_avg_mttr  = round(sum(team_mttr_vals)/len(team_mttr_vals)) if team_mttr_vals else None
   team_sla_on    = sum(1 for t in all_closed if not t.sla_breached)
   team_sla_pct   = round((team_sla_on / len(all_closed)) * 100) if all_closed else 0

   # Trend last 14 days (team total closed)
   team_trend = []
   for i in range(13,-1,-1):
       d   = today - timedelta(days=i)
       ds  = datetime.combine(d, datetime.min.time()) - timedelta(hours=7)
       de  = ds + timedelta(days=1)
       cnt = Ticket.query.filter(
           Ticket.status == 'closed',
           Ticket.closed_at.between(ds, de)
       ).count()
       team_trend.append({'date': d.strftime('%d/%m'), 'count': cnt})

   return render_template('kpi_team.html',
       settings=settings, now_wib=to_wib(now),
       period=days_back, kpi_data=kpi_data,
       team_avg_mttr=team_avg_mttr, team_sla_pct=team_sla_pct,
       total_closed=len(all_closed), team_trend=json.dumps(team_trend))


@app.route('/api/notifications')
@login_required
def api_get_notifications():
   """Return all unread notifications for the user."""
   notifs = Notification.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id, is_read=False).order_by(Notification.created_at.desc()).limit(10).all()
   return jsonify({
       'count': Notification.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id, is_read=False).count(),
       'notifications': [{
           'id': n.id,
           'message': n.message,
           'link': n.link,
           'created_at': fmt_wib(n.created_at)
       } for n in notifs]
   })

@app.route('/api/notifications/mark-read', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_mark_notifications_read():
   """Mark notifications as read."""
   nid = request.json.get('id') if request.is_json else request.form.get('id')
   if nid:
       n = Notification.query.filter_by(id=nid, user_id=current_user.id).first()
       if n: n.is_read = True
   else:
       # Mark all as read
       Notification.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id, is_read=False).update({'is_read': True})
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route('/settings', methods=['GET','POST'])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def settings_page():
   s      = get_settings()
   users  = User.query.order_by(User.created_at.desc()).all()
   from sqlalchemy import func
   raw    = db.session.query(Ticket.created_by, func.count(Ticket.id)).group_by(Ticket.created_by).all()
   tcounts = {r[0]:r[1] for r in raw}
   user_error = None

   # Dropdown data for display
   dd_categories = {
       'sites':        ('Lokasi / Site',        DropdownOption.get('sites')),
       'device_types': ('Device Type',           DropdownOption.get('device_types')),
       'impacts':      ('Dampak Masalah',         DropdownOption.get('impacts')),
       'divisions':    ('Divisi',                 DropdownOption.get('divisions')),
       'job_positions': ('Jabatan / Job Position', DropdownOption.get('job_positions')),
   }

   if request.method == 'POST':
       tab    = request.form.get('tab','config')
       action = request.form.get('action','')

       # ── CONFIG ──────────────────────────────────────
       if tab == 'config':
           s.company_name    = request.form.get('company_name', s.company_name)
           s.ticket_prefix   = request.form.get('ticket_prefix', s.ticket_prefix)
           s.sla_critical    = int(request.form.get('sla_critical', s.sla_critical) or s.sla_critical)
           s.sla_high        = int(request.form.get('sla_high', s.sla_high) or s.sla_high)
           s.sla_medium      = int(request.form.get('sla_medium', s.sla_medium) or s.sla_medium)
           s.sla_low         = int(request.form.get('sla_low', s.sla_low) or s.sla_low)
           s.telegram_token  = request.form.get('telegram_token', s.telegram_token or '')
           s.telegram_chat_id= request.form.get('telegram_chat_id', s.telegram_chat_id or '')
           s.base_url        = request.form.get('base_url', s.base_url or 'http://ssh.alfabeta.co.id:64575')
           s.telegram_enabled = 'telegram_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_run_enabled   = 'tg_run_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_build_enabled = 'tg_build_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_ba_enabled    = 'tg_ba_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_wo_enabled    = 'tg_wo_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_survey_enabled = 'tg_survey_enabled' in request.form
           s.tg_scheduler_enabled = 'tg_scheduler_enabled' in request.form
           logo = _save_logo(request.files.get('company_logo'))
           if logo: s.company_logo = logo
           db.session.commit()
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page'))

       # ── ADD USER ────────────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'add_user':
           un,em,fn,ro,pw,pw2 = (request.form.get(k,'').strip() for k in
               ['username','email','full_name','role','password','password2'])
           site = request.form.get('site','')
           divisi = request.form.get('divisi','')
           job_pos = request.form.get('job_position','')
           if not un or not em or not pw:
               user_error = 'Username, email, dan password wajib diisi.'
           elif User.query.filter_by(username=un).first():
               user_error = f'Username "{un}" sudah ada.'
           elif User.query.filter_by(email=em).first():
               user_error = f'Email "{em}" sudah terdaftar.'
           elif len(pw) < 6:
               user_error = 'Password minimal 6 karakter.'
           elif pw != pw2:
               user_error = 'Konfirmasi password tidak cocok.'
           else:
               u = User(username=un, email=em, full_name=fn, role=ro, site=site, divisi=divisi, job_position=job_pos)
               u.set_password(pw); db.session.add(u); db.session.commit()
               return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + '?tab=users')

       # ── EDIT USER ────────────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'edit_user':
           uid = request.form.get('uid')
           u   = db.session.get(User, int(uid)) if uid else None
           if u:
               u.full_name     = request.form.get('full_name', u.full_name)
               u.email         = request.form.get('email', u.email)
               u.role          = request.form.get('role', u.role)
               u.site          = request.form.get('site', u.site)
               u.divisi        = request.form.get('divisi', u.divisi)
               u.job_position  = request.form.get('job_position', u.job_position)
               u.is_active_acc = request.form.get('is_active','0') == '1'
               pw = request.form.get('password','')
               if pw and len(pw) >= 6: u.set_password(pw)
               db.session.commit()
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + '?tab=users')

       # ── DELETE USER ──────────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'del_user':
           uid = request.form.get('uid')
           u   = db.session.get(User, int(uid)) if uid else None
           if u and u.id != current_user.id:
               db.session.delete(u); db.session.commit()
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + '?tab=users')

       # ── ADD DROPDOWN ─────────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'add_dropdown':
           cat = request.form.get('category','')
           val = request.form.get('value','').strip()
           if cat and val:
               exists = DropdownOption.query.filter_by(category=cat, value=val).first()
               if not exists:
                   max_order = db.session.query(db.func.max(DropdownOption.sort_order))\
                       .filter_by(category=cat).scalar() or 0
                   db.session.add(DropdownOption(category=cat, value=val, sort_order=max_order+1))
                   db.session.commit()
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + '?tab=dropdowns')

       # ── DELETE DROPDOWN ──────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'del_dropdown':
           did = request.form.get('did')
           opt = db.session.get(DropdownOption, int(did)) if did else None
           if opt: db.session.delete(opt); db.session.commit()
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + '?tab=dropdowns')

       # ── TEST TELEGRAM ────────────────────────────────
       elif action == 'test_telegram':
           ok = send_telegram('🧪 Test notifikasi ODMS berhasil! Dashboard aktif.')
           msg = 'Telegram berhasil!' if ok else 'Gagal kirim Telegram. Cek token dan chat ID.'
           return redirect(url_for('settings_page') + f'?tab=config&tmsg={msg}')

   tmsg = request.args.get('tmsg','')
   return render_template('settings.html',
       settings=s, users=users, tcounts=tcounts, user_error=user_error,
       dd_categories=dd_categories,
       sites=DropdownOption.get('sites'),
       divisions=DropdownOption.get('divisions'),
       job_positions=DropdownOption.get('job_positions'),
       tmsg=tmsg)

@app.route('/profile', methods=['GET','POST'])
@login_required
def profile():
   settings = get_settings()
   error = success = None
   if request.method == 'POST':
       current_user.full_name = request.form.get('full_name','').strip()
       current_user.email     = request.form.get('email','').strip()
       current_user.divisi    = request.form.get('divisi','').strip()
       current_user.job_position = request.form.get('job_position','').strip()
       np, cp = request.form.get('new_password',''), request.form.get('current_password','')
       if np:
           if not current_user.check_password(cp): error = 'Password saat ini salah.'
           elif len(np) < 6: error = 'Password baru minimal 6 karakter.'
           else: current_user.set_password(np)
       if not error:
           db.session.commit(); success = 'Profil berhasil diperbarui.'
   my_tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(created_by=current_user.id)\
       .order_by(Ticket.created_at.desc()).limit(10).all()
   return render_template('profile.html', settings=settings,
       error=error, success=success, my_tickets=my_tickets,
       divisions=DropdownOption.get('divisions'),
       job_positions=DropdownOption.get('job_positions'))

@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/delete', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def ticket_delete(ticket_id):
   ticket = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   # Delete related updates first
   TicketUpdate.query.filter_by(ticket_id_fk=ticket_id).delete()
   db.session.delete(ticket)
   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('tickets'))


@app.route('/tickets/<int:ticket_id>/sign', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def ticket_sign(ticket_id):
   ticket = Ticket.query.get_or_404(ticket_id)
   if ticket.status != 'closed':
       return jsonify({'error': 'Can only sign closed tickets'}), 400
   role   = current_user.role
   sig_data = request.json.get('signature','')
   if role == 'user':
       ticket.sig_reporter = sig_data
   elif role == 'run':
       ticket.sig_engineer = sig_data
   elif role == 'admin':
       ticket.sig_admin = sig_data
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({'ok': True})


@app.route('/export/tickets.csv')
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def export_tickets_csv():
   from flask import Response
   import csv, io
   tickets = Ticket.query.order_by(Ticket.created_at.desc()).all()
   output = io.StringIO()
   w = csv.writer(output)
   w.writerow(['Ticket ID','Site','Gate/Area','Device','Issue','Urgency','Priority','Status',
               'Pelapor','Divisi','Engineer','Created (WIB)','Resolved (WIB)','MTTR (min)','SLA Breached'])
   for t in tickets:
       w.writerow([
           t.ticket_id, t.site or '', t.gate_area_display, t.device_type or '',
           t.issue_desc or '', t.urgency or '', t.priority or '', t.status,
           t.nama_pelapor or '', t.divisi_pelapor or '',
           t.assignee.full_name if t.assignee else '',
           fmt_wib(t.created_at), fmt_wib(t.resolved_at) if t.resolved_at else '',
           t.mttr_minutes or '', 'Yes' if t.sla_breached else 'No'
       ])
   output.seek(0)
   return Response(output.getvalue(), mimetype='text/csv',
                   headers={'Content-Disposition':'attachment;filename=odms_tickets.csv'})



@app.route('/api/tickets')
@login_required
def api_tickets():
   qry = Ticket.query
   if current_user.is_user_role:
       # User hanya melihat tiket dari lokasi (site) miliknya sendiri
       if current_user.site:
           qry = qry.filter(Ticket.site == current_user.site)
       else:
           qry = qry.filter_by(created_by=current_user.id)
   return jsonify([t.to_dict() for t in qry.order_by(Ticket.created_at.desc()).limit(50).all()])

   return jsonify([{
       'ticket_id': t.ticket_id, 'priority': t.priority,
       'sla_remaining': t.sla_remaining_seconds, 'sla_breached': t.sla_breached
   } for t in active])

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  DB MIGRATION + SEED
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def migrate_db():
   from sqlalchemy import text, inspect
   with db.engine.connect() as conn:
       inspector = inspect(db.engine)
       # Tickets
       ticket_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('tickets')]
       new_cols = {
           'nama_pelapor':    'VARCHAR(200)',
           'divisi_pelapor':  'VARCHAR(100)',
           'phone_telegram':  'VARCHAR(50)',
           'device_type_other': 'VARCHAR(200)',
           'impact_other':    'VARCHAR(500)',
           'urgency_corrected': 'VARCHAR(20)',
           'sig_reporter':    'TEXT',
           'sig_engineer':    'TEXT',
           'sig_admin':       'TEXT',
           'location_id':     'INTEGER REFERENCES asset_locations(id)',
       }
       for col, typ in new_cols.items():
           if col not in ticket_cols:
               conn.execute(text(f'ALTER TABLE tickets ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}'))
       # Link existing tickets by name
       conn.execute(text("""
           UPDATE tickets 
           SET location_id = (SELECT id FROM asset_locations WHERE name = tickets.site LIMIT 1)
           WHERE location_id IS NULL AND site IS NOT NULL
       """))
       # TicketUpdate
       if inspector.has_table('ticket_updates'):
           upd_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('ticket_updates')]
           if 'urgency_corrected' not in upd_cols:
               conn.execute(text('ALTER TABLE ticket_updates ADD COLUMN urgency_corrected VARCHAR(20)'))
       # Users
       if inspector.has_table('users'):
           user_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('users')]
           if 'divisi' not in user_cols:
               conn.execute(text('ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN divisi VARCHAR(100)'))
           if 'user_signature' not in user_cols:
               conn.execute(text('ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN user_signature TEXT'))
       # AppSettings
       if inspector.has_table('app_settings'):
           set_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('app_settings')]
           for col in ['telegram_token','telegram_chat_id', 'telegram_enabled']:
               if col not in set_cols:
                   typ = 'BOOLEAN' if col == 'telegram_enabled' else 'VARCHAR(200)'
                   conn.execute(text(f'ALTER TABLE app_settings ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}'))

       # AssetDevice - Critical Migration
       if inspector.has_table('asset_devices'):
           dev_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('asset_devices')]
           new_dev_cols = {
               'hostname_role': 'VARCHAR(50)',
               'ssh1_port':     'VARCHAR(10)',
               'ssh2_port':     'VARCHAR(10)',
               'tailscale_ip':  'VARCHAR(50)',
               'anydesk_id':    'VARCHAR(50)',
               'anydesk_pw':    'VARCHAR(50)',
               'mata_version':  'VARCHAR(50)',
               'alpr_version':  'VARCHAR(50)',
               'docker_version':'VARCHAR(50)',
               'has_abtool':    'BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0',
               'has_grafana':   'BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0',
               'frame_rate':    'VARCHAR(10)',
               'resolution':    'VARCHAR(50)',
               'bitrate':       'VARCHAR(20)'
           }
           for col, typ in new_dev_cols.items():
               if col not in dev_cols:
                   conn.execute(text(f'ALTER TABLE asset_devices ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}'))

       # AssetLocation Migration
       if inspector.has_table('asset_locations'):
           loc_cols = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('asset_locations')]
           if 'group_id' not in loc_cols:
               conn.execute(text('ALTER TABLE asset_locations ADD COLUMN group_id INTEGER REFERENCES location_groups(id)'))
           
           new_loc_cols = {
               'cp_manager': 'VARCHAR(200)',
               'cp_manager_phone': 'VARCHAR(50)',
               'cp_manager_email': 'VARCHAR(120)',
               'area_manager': 'VARCHAR(200)',
               'area_manager_phone': 'VARCHAR(50)',
               'area_manager_email': 'VARCHAR(120)'
           }
           for col, typ in new_loc_cols.items():
               if col not in loc_cols:
                   conn.execute(text(f'ALTER TABLE asset_locations ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}'))

       # PreventiveScheduler Migration
       if inspector.has_table('preventive_scheduler'):
           col_info = next((c for c in inspector.get_columns('preventive_scheduler') if c['name'] == 'assigned_to'), None)
           if col_info and 'INTEGER' in str(col_info['type']).upper():
               conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE preventive_scheduler RENAME TO sch_old"))
               conn.execute(text("""
                   CREATE TABLE preventive_scheduler (
                       id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                       title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
                       task_type VARCHAR(50),
                       description TEXT,
                       start_date DATETIME NOT NULL,
                       end_date DATETIME,
                       location_id INTEGER REFERENCES asset_locations(id),
                       assigned_to TEXT,
                       created_by INTEGER REFERENCES users(id),
                       status VARCHAR(30),
                       color VARCHAR(20),
                       created_at DATETIME
                   )
               """))
               conn.execute(text("""
                   INSERT INTO preventive_scheduler (id, title, task_type, description, start_date, end_date, location_id, assigned_to, created_by, status, color, created_at)
                   SELECT id, title, task_type, description, start_date, end_date, location_id, 
                          CASE WHEN assigned_to IS NULL THEN '[]' ELSE '[' || assigned_to || ']' END, 
                          created_by, status, color, created_at FROM sch_old
               """))
               conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE sch_old"))
           
           sch_cols2 = [c['name'] for c in inspector.get_columns('preventive_scheduler')]
           if 'system_type' not in sch_cols2:
               conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE preventive_scheduler ADD COLUMN system_type VARCHAR(100)"))

       conn.commit()
def seed_dropdowns():
   seeds = {
       'sites':        DEFAULT_SITES,
       'device_types': DEFAULT_DEVICE_TYPES,
       'impacts':      DEFAULT_IMPACTS,
       'gates':        DEFAULT_GATES,
       'divisions':    DEFAULT_DIVISIONS,
   }
   for cat, values in seeds.items():
       existing = {o.value for o in DropdownOption.query.filter_by(category=cat).all()}
       for i, val in enumerate(values):
           if val not in existing:
               db.session.add(DropdownOption(category=cat, value=val, sort_order=i))
   db.session.commit()

def seed_users():
   get_settings()
   defaults = []
   for un, em, fn, ro, site, div in defaults:
       if not User.query.filter_by(username=un).first():
           u = User(username=un, email=em, full_name=fn, role=ro, site=site, divisi=div)
           u.set_password(f'{un}123')
           db.session.add(u)
   db.session.commit()

with app.app_context():
   db.create_all()
   from sqlalchemy import text
   for col in ["tg_run_enabled", "tg_build_enabled", "tg_ba_enabled", "tg_wo_enabled", "tg_survey_enabled", "tg_scheduler_enabled"]:
       try:
           db.session.execute(text(f"ALTER TABLE app_settings ADD COLUMN {col} BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1"))
           db.session.commit()
       except: db.session.rollback()
   migrate_db()
   seed_dropdowns()
   seed_users()
   # Seed Location Groups
   initial_groups = ["Premium", "Non-Premium", "Parkee", "AP1", "AP2", "Ezitama"]
   for gname in initial_groups:
       if not LocationGroup.query.filter_by(name=gname).first():
           db.session.add(LocationGroup(name=gname))
   db.session.commit()


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  ASSET MONITORING ROUTES
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

@app.route("/assets")
@app.route("/assets/sites/<int:target_lid>")
@login_required
def asset_map(target_lid=None):
   settings = get_settings()
   locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name).all()

   # Summary Statistics
   total_locations = AssetLocation.query.count()
   total_servers = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(device_category='server').count()
   total_cameras = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(device_category='camera').count()
   open_issues = AssetIssue.query.filter(AssetIssue.status != 'resolved').count()
   return render_template(
       "asset_map.html",
       locations=locations,
       total_locations=total_locations,
       total_servers=total_servers,
       total_cameras=total_cameras,
       open_issues=open_issues,
       asset_types=ASSET_TYPES,
       server_types=SERVER_TYPES,
       camera_types=CAMERA_TYPES,
       can_edit=current_user.role in ("admin", "run"),
       settings=settings,
       device_types=DropdownOption.get('device_types') or DEFAULT_DROPDOWNS['device_types'],
       target_lid=target_lid
   )

@app.route("/api/assets")
@login_required
def api_assets():
   return jsonify([l.to_dict() for l in AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()])

@app.route("/api/engineers")
@login_required
def api_engineers():
   role = request.args.get('role')
   query = User.query.filter(User.role != 'admin') # Always exclude admin
   
   if role == 'run_engineer':
       query = query.filter((User.divisi == 'RUN') | (User.role == 'run'))
   elif role == 'build_engineer':
       query = query.filter((User.divisi == 'BUILD') | (User.role == 'build'))
   elif role == 'lead_engineer':
       query = query.filter(User.role == 'lead_engineer')
   else:
       # Default technical roles excluding admin
       query = query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'build', 'lead_engineer', 'operation']))
       
   users = query.all()
   return jsonify([{'id': u.id, 'name': u.full_name or u.username} for u in users])

@app.route("/api/assets", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_asset_create():
   data = request.json or {}
   def pc(v):
       try: return float(v) if v not in (None, "") else None
       except: return None
   nuc = int(data.get("nuc_count", 0) or 0)
   cam = int(data.get("camera_count", 0) or 0)
   loc = AssetLocation(
       name=data.get("name", "").strip(),
       location_code=data.get("location_code", "").strip(),
       address=data.get("address", "").strip(),
       city=data.get("city", "").strip(),
       lat=pc(data.get("lat")),
       lng=pc(data.get("lng")),
       asset_type=data.get("asset_type", ""),
       group_id=data.get("group_id"),
       device_count=nuc + cam,
       nuc_count=nuc,
       camera_count=cam,
       status=data.get("status", "active"),
       notes=data.get("notes", "").strip(),
       cp_manager=data.get("cp_manager", "").strip(),
       cp_manager_phone=data.get("cp_manager_phone", "").strip(),
       cp_manager_email=data.get("cp_manager_email", "").strip(),
       area_manager=data.get("area_manager", "").strip(),
       area_manager_phone=data.get("area_manager_phone", "").strip(),
       area_manager_email=data.get("area_manager_email", "").strip(),
       created_by=current_user.id,
   )
   db.session.add(loc)
   try:
       db.session.commit()
       return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": loc.id, **loc.to_dict()})
   except Exception as e:
       db.session.rollback()
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}), 500

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>", methods=["PUT"])
@login_required
def api_asset_update(lid):
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get_or_404(lid)
   data = request.json or {}
   for f in ("name","location_code","address","city","asset_type","status","notes",
             "cp_manager","cp_manager_phone","cp_manager_email",
             "area_manager","area_manager_phone","area_manager_email"):
       if f in data: setattr(loc, f, data[f])
   if "group_id" in data: loc.group_id = data["group_id"]
   if "lat" in data:
       try: loc.lat = float(data["lat"]) if data["lat"] not in (None, "") else None
       except: loc.lat = None
   if "lng" in data:
       try: loc.lng = float(data["lng"]) if data["lng"] not in (None, "") else None
       except: loc.lng = None
   if "nuc_count" in data: loc.nuc_count = int(data["nuc_count"] or 0)
   if "camera_count" in data: loc.camera_count = int(data["camera_count"] or 0)
   loc.device_count = (loc.nuc_count or 0) + (loc.camera_count or 0)
   loc.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True, **loc.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>", methods=["DELETE"])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def api_asset_delete(lid):
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get_or_404(lid)
   db.session.delete(loc)
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/devices")

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/devices", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_device_create(lid):
   AssetLocation.query.get_or_404(lid)
   data = request.json or {}
   device_category = data.get("device_category", "server")
   device_name = data.get("device_name", "").strip()
   gate_name = data.get("gate_name", "").strip()

   if not device_name and device_category == 'camera' and gate_name:
       device_name = gate_name

   if not device_name:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Nama Perangkat / Gate Name wajib diisi"}), 400

   dev = AssetDevice(
       location_id=lid,
       device_name=device_name,
       device_category=device_category,
       device_type=data.get("device_type", "").strip(),
       hostname_role=data.get("hostname_role", "").strip(),
       ip_address=data.get("ip_address", "").strip(),
       username=data.get("username", "").strip(),
       password=data.get("password", "").strip(),
       port=data.get("port", "").strip(),
       gate_name=gate_name,
       assessment=data.get("assessment", "").strip(),
       serial_number=data.get("serial_number", "").strip(),
       brand_model=data.get("brand_model", "").strip(),
       status=data.get("status", "active"),
       ssh1_port=data.get("ssh1_port", "").strip(),
       ssh2_port=data.get("ssh2_port", "").strip(),
       tailscale_ip=data.get("tailscale_ip", "").strip(),
       anydesk_id=data.get("anydesk_id", "").strip(),
       anydesk_pw=data.get("anydesk_pw", "").strip(),
       mata_version=data.get("mata_version", "").strip(),
       alpr_version=data.get("alpr_version", "").strip(),
       docker_version=data.get("docker_version", "").strip(),
       has_abtool=bool(data.get("has_abtool")),
       has_grafana=bool(data.get("has_grafana")),
       frame_rate=data.get("frame_rate", "").strip(),
       notes=data.get("notes", "").strip(),
   )
   db.session.add(dev)
   db.session.flush()
   _sync_loc_counts(lid)
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True, **dev.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/devices/<int:did>", methods=["PUT"])
@login_required
def api_device_update(did):
   dev = AssetDevice.query.get_or_404(did)
   data = request.json or {}
   if "device_name" in data:
       name = data["device_name"].strip()
       if not name and data.get("device_category") == 'camera' and data.get("gate_name"):
           name = data["gate_name"].strip()
       if not name:
           return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Nama Perangkat tidak boleh kosong"}), 400
       dev.device_name = name

   fields = ("device_category","device_type","hostname_role","ip_address",
             "username","password","port","gate_name","assessment","serial_number",
             "brand_model","status","notes","ssh1_port","ssh2_port","tailscale_ip",
             "anydesk_id","anydesk_pw","mata_version","alpr_version","docker_version", "frame_rate")
   for f in fields:
       if f in data: setattr(dev, f, data[f])
   if "has_abtool" in data: dev.has_abtool = bool(data["has_abtool"])
   if "has_grafana" in data: dev.has_grafana = bool(data["has_grafana"])
   
   dev.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
   dev.location.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
   db.session.flush()
   _sync_loc_counts(dev.location_id)
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True, **dev.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/devices/<int:did>", methods=["DELETE"])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def api_device_delete(did):
   dev = AssetDevice.query.get_or_404(did)
   lid = dev.location_id
   db.session.delete(dev)
   db.session.flush()
   _sync_loc_counts(lid)
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/issues")
@login_required
def api_issues(lid):
    # Get both AssetIssues and Tickets
    issues = AssetIssue.query.filter_by(location_id=lid).order_by(AssetIssue.created_at.desc()).all()
    tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(location_id=lid).order_by(Ticket.created_at.desc()).all()
    
    results = []
    for i in issues:
        d = i.to_dict()
        d['is_ticket'] = False
        results.append(d)
    
    for t in tickets:
        results.append({
            'id': t.id,
            'ticket_id': t.ticket_id,
            'title': f"Ticket: {t.ticket_id}",
            'description': t.issue_desc,
            'severity': t.priority or 'medium',
            'status': t.status,
            'is_ticket': True,
            'created_at': t.created_at.isoformat(),
            'reported_by_name': t.creator.full_name if t.creator else t.nama_pelapor
        })
    
    # Sort unified results by date
    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['created_at'], reverse=True)
    return jsonify(results)

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/issues", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_issue_create(lid):
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get_or_404(lid)
   # Handle both JSON and Form data (for attachments)
   data = request.form.to_dict() if request.form else (request.json or {})
   
   issue = AssetIssue(
       location_id=lid,
       device_id=data.get("device_id") or None,
       issue_type=data.get("issue_type", "damage"),
       title=data.get("title", "").strip(),
       description=data.get("description", "").strip(),
       severity=data.get("severity", "medium"),
       status="open",
       reported_by=current_user.id,
   )
   
   # Handle Attachment
   if 'attachment' in request.files:
       f = request.files['attachment']
       if f and f.filename:
           ext = f.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[-1].lower()
           fname = f"issue_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.{ext}"
           f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
           issue.attachment = fname

   db.session.add(issue)
   if issue.severity in ("critical", "high") and loc.status == "active":
       loc.status = "maintenance"
   db.session.flush() # Get issue.id
   
   # --- AUTOMATIC WORK ORDER ---
   assigned_id = data.get("assigned_to")
   wo = None
   if assigned_id and assigned_id != "":
       wo = WorkOrder(
           issue_id=issue.id,
           assigned_to=int(assigned_id),
           status='open',
           created_at=datetime.utcnow()
       )
       issue.status = 'in_progress'
       db.session.add(wo)
       add_notification(wo.assigned_to, f"Ada Work Order baru untuk Anda di {loc.name}: {issue.title}", url_for('asset_map'))
   
   db.session.commit()
   
   # Telegram Notification
   st = get_settings()
   if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_run_enabled:
       tech_name = "Unassigned"
       if wo and wo.assigned_to:
           tu = db.session.get(User, wo.assigned_to)
           if tu: tech_name = tu.full_name or tu.username

       msg = (
           f"⚠️ <b>NEW ISSUE & WORK ORDER</b>\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<b>Site:</b> {html.escape(loc.name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Issue:</b> {html.escape(issue.title or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Severity:</b> {issue.severity.upper()}\n"
           f"<b>Assigned To:</b> <b>{html.escape(tech_name)}</b>\n"
           f"<b>By:</b> {html.escape(current_user.full_name or current_user.username)}\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"{'📎 <b>Attachment:</b> Attached' if issue.attachment else ''}"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)

   return jsonify({"ok": True, **issue.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/issues/<int:iid>")
@login_required
def api_get_issue(iid):
    issue = AssetIssue.query.get_or_404(iid)
    return jsonify(issue.to_dict())

@app.route("/api/issues/<int:iid>/resolve", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_issue_resolve(iid):
   issue = AssetIssue.query.get_or_404(iid)
   data = request.json or {}
   issue.status = "resolved"
   issue.resolved_by = current_user.id
   issue.resolved_at = datetime.utcnow()
   issue.resolution_note = data.get("resolution_note", "").strip()
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get(issue.location_id)
   if loc and loc.status == "maintenance":
       rem = AssetIssue.query.filter_by(location_id=loc.id, status="open")\
           .filter(AssetIssue.severity.in_(["critical", "high"])).count()
       if rem == 0: loc.status = "active"
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True, **issue.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/bas")
@login_required
def api_asset_bas(lid):
    """Fetch all Berita Acara (BA) related to a location."""
    loc = AssetLocation.query.get_or_404(lid)
    
    # 1. BAs linked via AssetIssue
    bas_via_issue = AssetIssueBA.query.join(AssetIssue).filter(AssetIssue.location_id == lid).all()
    
    # 2. BAs linked via lokasi_text (for manual entries)
    bas_via_text = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(lokasi_text=loc.name).all()
    
    # Combine and unique by ID
    all_bas_map = {b.id: b for b in (bas_via_issue + bas_via_text)}
    all_bas = list(all_bas_map.values())
    
    # Sort by created_at desc
    all_bas.sort(key=lambda x: x.created_at if x.created_at else datetime.min, reverse=True)
    
    return jsonify([b.to_dict() for b in all_bas])

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/attachments")
@login_required
def api_asset_attachments(lid):
    """Fetch all general attachments for a location."""
    atts = AssetLocationAttachment.query.filter_by(location_id=lid).all()
    return jsonify([a.to_dict() for a in atts])

@app.route("/api/assets/<int:lid>/attachments", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_asset_add_attachment(lid):
    """Upload a new attachment for a location."""
    file = request.files.get('file')
    if not file: return jsonify({'ok':False, 'msg':'No file uploaded'})
    
    filename, ext = save_attachment(file, prefix='asset_att')
    if not filename: return jsonify({'ok':False, 'msg':'Invalid file type'})
    
    new_att = AssetLocationAttachment(
        location_id=lid,
        filename=filename,
        original_name=file.filename,
        file_type=ext,
        uploaded_by=current_user.id
    )
    db.session.add(new_att)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'ok':True})

    return jsonify({'ok':True})

@app.route("/api/location-groups", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def api_location_groups():
    if request.method == "GET":
        groups = LocationGroup.query.order_by(LocationGroup.name).all()
        return jsonify([g.to_dict() for g in groups])
    
    if current_user.role != 'admin': abort(403)
    data = request.json
    if not data or 'name' not in data: return jsonify({'ok':False, 'msg':'Name required'}), 400
    
    if LocationGroup.query.filter_by(name=data['name']).first():
        return jsonify({'ok':False, 'msg':'Group already exists'}), 400
        
    new_group = LocationGroup(name=data['name'])
    db.session.add(new_group)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'ok':True, 'id':new_group.id})

@app.route("/api/location-groups/<int:gid>", methods=["DELETE"])
@login_required
@role_required('admin')
def api_delete_location_group(gid):
    group = LocationGroup.query.get_or_404(gid)
    # Optional: check if any locations are using this group
    if AssetLocation.query.filter_by(group_id=gid).first():
        return jsonify({'ok':False, 'msg':'Group is in use and cannot be deleted'}), 400
        
    db.session.delete(group)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'ok':True})

@app.route("/api/issues/<int:iid>/generate-wo", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_generate_wo(iid):
   issue = AssetIssue.query.get_or_404(iid)
   # Check if WO already exists and is open
   existing = WorkOrder.query.filter_by(issue_id=iid).filter(WorkOrder.status != 'done').first()
   if existing:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Work Order active sudah ada"}), 400
   
   wo = WorkOrder(
       issue_id=iid,
       status='open',
       created_at=datetime.utcnow()
   )
   issue.status = 'in_progress'
   db.session.add(wo)
   db.session.commit()

   # Telegram Notification
   st = get_settings()
   if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_wo_enabled:
       loc = AssetLocation.query.get(issue.location_id)
       msg = (
           f"🛠️ <b>NEW WORK ORDER</b>\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<b>Site:</b> {html.escape(loc.name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Issue:</b> {html.escape(issue.title or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Severity:</b> {issue.severity.upper()}\n"
           f"<b>Status:</b> {wo.status.upper()}\n"
           f"<b>Reported By:</b> {html.escape(current_user.full_name or current_user.username)}\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<i>Silakan cek dashboard untuk detail pengerjaan.</i>"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)

   return jsonify({"ok": True, "wo": wo.to_dict()})

@app.route("/api/wo/<int:wid>", methods=["PUT"])
@login_required
def api_wo_update(wid):
   wo = WorkOrder.query.get_or_404(wid)
   issue = AssetIssue.query.get(wo.issue_id)
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get(issue.location_id)
   
   # Handle both JSON and Form data (for photos)
   data = request.form.to_dict() if request.form else (request.json or {})
   
   old_status = wo.status
   old_tech = wo.assigned_to

   if 'assigned_to' in data:
       wo.assigned_to = int(data['assigned_to']) if data['assigned_to'] and data['assigned_to'] != 'null' else None
       if wo.assigned_to:
           add_notification(wo.assigned_to, f"Anda ditugaskan pada Work Order di {loc.name}", url_for('project_work_queue'))
   
   if 'status' in data:
       new_status = data['status']
       if new_status == 'in_progress' and wo.status == 'open':
           wo.started_at = datetime.utcnow()
       elif new_status == 'done' and wo.status != 'done':
           wo.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
       wo.status = new_status
       
   if 'notes' in data:
       wo.notes = data['notes'].strip()
       
   # Handle Photos
   if 'photo_before' in request.files:
       f = request.files['photo_before']
       if f and f.filename:
           fname = f"wo_{wid}_before_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.jpg"
           f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
           wo.photo_before = fname
           
   if 'photo_after' in request.files:
       f = request.files['photo_after']
       if f and f.filename:
           fname = f"wo_{wid}_after_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.jpg"
           f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
           wo.photo_after = fname

   db.session.commit()
   notify_mentions(wo.notes, f"Anda dimention dalam catatan Work Order di {loc.name}", url_for("project_work_queue"))
   db.session.commit()

   # Telegram Updates
   try:
       st = get_settings()
       if st.telegram_enabled:
           notify = False
           update_type = ""
           if old_status != wo.status:
               notify = True
               update_type = f"Status updated to <b>{wo.status.upper()}</b>"
           elif old_tech != wo.assigned_to:
               notify = True
               tech_name = User.query.get(wo.assigned_to).full_name if wo.assigned_to else "Unassigned"
               update_type = f"Assigned to <b>{tech_name}</b>"
               
           st = get_settings()
           if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_wo_enabled:
               msg = (
                   f"📝 <b>WORK ORDER UPDATE</b>\n"
                   f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
                   f"<b>Site:</b> {html.escape(loc.name or '—')}\n"
                   f"<b>Issue:</b> {html.escape(issue.title or '—')}\n"
                   f"<b>Update:</b> {html.escape(update_type or '—')}\n"
                   f"<b>By:</b> {html.escape(current_user.full_name or current_user.username)}\n"
                   f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
               )
               send_telegram(msg)
   except: pass

   return jsonify({"ok": True, **wo.to_dict()})
