@app.route('/api/scheduler/import', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def import_scheduler():
   if 'file' not in request.files:
       return jsonify({'error': 'No file uploaded'}), 400
   
   file = request.files['file']
   if not file.filename:
       return jsonify({'error': 'Empty filename'}), 400

   try:
       import pandas as pd
       import datetime
       
       # Read Excel
       df = pd.read_excel(file, header=None)
       
       def to_day(val):
           try:
               v = str(val).strip().split('.')[0]
               if v.isdigit():
                   d = int(v)
                   if 1 <= d <= 31: return d
           except: pass
           return None

       # 1. Detect Month/Year from "PERIODE:" cell
       period_str = ""
       for r in range(min(20, df.shape[0])):
           for c in range(min(25, df.shape[1])):
               val = str(df.iloc[r, c])
               if "PERIODE:" in val.upper():
                   period_str = val.split(":")[-1].strip()
                   break
           if period_str: break
       
       target_year = datetime.datetime.now().year
       target_month = datetime.datetime.now().month
       
       month_map = {
           'JANUARI': 1, 'FEBRUARI': 2, 'MARET': 3, 'APRIL': 4, 'MEI': 5, 'JUNI': 6,
           'JULI': 7, 'AGUSTUS': 8, 'SEPTEMBER': 9, 'OKTOBER': 10, 'NOVEMBER': 11, 'DESEMBER': 12,
           'JANUARY': 1, 'FEBRUARY': 2, 'MARCH': 3, 'MAY': 5, 'JUNE': 6, 'JULY': 7, 'AUGUST': 8, 'OCTOBER': 10, 'DECEMBER': 12
       }
       
       if period_str:
           parts = period_str.split()
           if len(parts) >= 1:
               m_str = parts[0].upper()
               if m_str in month_map:
                   target_month = month_map[m_str]
               if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1].isdigit():
                   target_year = int(parts[1])

       # 2. Find Header Row (dates 1, 2, 3...)
       header_row = -1
       for r in range(df.shape[0]):
           row_days = [to_day(x) for x in df.iloc[r] if to_day(x) is not None]
           if 1 in row_days and 2 in row_days and 3 in row_days:
               header_row = r
               break
       
       if header_row == -1:
           return jsonify({'error': 'Invalid format: Could not find date headers (1, 2, 3...)'}), 400
       
       # 3. Map columns
       name_col = -1
       date_cols = {}
       for c in range(df.shape[1]):
           val = df.iloc[header_row, c]
           day = to_day(val)
           if day:
               date_cols[day] = c
           
           # Check for "NAMA" in current or previous 2-3 rows
           for offset in [0, -1, -2, -3]:
               check_r = header_row + offset
               if 0 <= check_r < df.shape[0]:
                   if "NAMA" in str(df.iloc[check_r, c]).upper():
                       name_col = c
                       break
       
       if name_col == -1: name_col = 1 # Fallback to col 1
       
       # 4. Process Rows
       created_count = 0
       users = User.query.all()
       
       for r in range(header_row + 1, df.shape[0]):
           raw_name = str(df.iloc[r, name_col]).strip()
           if not raw_name or raw_name == 'nan' or raw_name == 'None' or "No" == raw_name or raw_name.isdigit(): 
               continue
           
           # Fuzzy match user
           target_user = None
           for u in users:
               if u.full_name and (raw_name.lower() in u.full_name.lower() or u.full_name.lower() in raw_name.lower()):
                   target_user = u
                   break
           
           if not target_user: continue
           
           for day, col in date_cols.items():
               code = str(df.iloc[r, col]).strip().upper()
               if code in ['R', 'SB', 'CT']:
                   title = ""
                   t_type = "Other"
                   color = "#3b82f6"
                   
                   if code == 'R':
                       title = f"Shift Reguler - {target_user.full_name}"
                   elif code == 'SB':
                       title = f"Stand By - {target_user.full_name}"
                       t_type = "PM"
                       color = "#f59e0b"
                   elif code == 'CT':
                       title = f"Cuti - {target_user.full_name}"
                       color = "#ef4444"
                   
                   try:
                       start_dt = datetime.datetime(target_year, target_month, day, 8, 0)
                       end_dt = datetime.datetime(target_year, target_month, day, 17, 0)
                       
                       task = PreventiveScheduler(
                           title=title,
                           task_type=t_type,
                           description=f"Automated Import: {code}",
                           start_date=start_dt,
                           end_date=end_dt,
                           assigned_to=target_user.id,
                           status='Scheduled',
                           color=color,
                           created_by=current_user.id
                       )
                       db.session.add(task)
                       created_count += 1
                   except Exception: 
                       continue
       
       db.session.commit()
       return jsonify({'message': f'Successfully imported {created_count} entries for {period_str or target_month}'}), 200

   except Exception as e:
       return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

@app.route('/api/scheduler/clear', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def clear_scheduler():
   data = request.json or {}
   month = data.get('month')
   year = data.get('year')
   
   if not month or not year:
       return jsonify({'error': 'Month and year required'}), 400
   
   try:
       import datetime
       start_date = datetime.datetime(int(year), int(month), 1)
       if int(month) == 12:
           end_date = datetime.datetime(int(year) + 1, 1, 1)
       else:
           end_date = datetime.datetime(int(year), int(month) + 1, 1)
           
       # Only delete tasks with "Automated Import" in description
       tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter(
           PreventiveScheduler.start_date >= start_date,
           PreventiveScheduler.start_date < end_date,
           PreventiveScheduler.description.like('%Automated Import%')
       ).all()
       
       count = len(tasks)
       for t in tasks:
           db.session.delete(t)
           
       db.session.commit()
       return jsonify({'message': f'Cleared {count} imported entries for {month}/{year}'})
   except Exception as e:
       return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500

@app.route("/api/assets/global-summary")
@login_required
def api_assets_global_summary():
   from sqlalchemy import func
   locs = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
   open_issues = AssetIssue.query.filter_by(status="open").all()
   def eff(loc):
       if loc.status == 'offline': return 'offline'
       if any(i.severity in ("critical", "high") for i in open_issues if i.location_id == loc.id) and loc.status == "active":
           return "maintenance"
       return loc.status
   total_nuc = db.session.query(func.count(AssetDevice.id)).filter_by(device_category="server").scalar() or sum(l.nuc_count or 0 for l in locs)
   total_cam = db.session.query(func.count(AssetDevice.id)).filter_by(device_category="camera").scalar() or sum(l.camera_count or 0 for l in locs)
   return jsonify({
       "total_sites": len(locs),
       "active": sum(1 for l in locs if eff(l) == "active"),
       "maintenance": sum(1 for l in locs if eff(l) == "maintenance"),
       "offline": sum(1 for l in locs if eff(l) == "offline"),
       "total_nuc": total_nuc,
       "total_camera": total_cam,
       "open_issues": len(open_issues),
   })

def _sync_loc_counts(lid):
   loc = AssetLocation.query.get(lid)
   if not loc: return
   loc.nuc_count = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=lid, device_category='server').count()
   loc.camera_count = AssetDevice.query.filter_by(location_id=lid, device_category='camera').count()
   loc.device_count = loc.nuc_count + loc.camera_count
   db.session.commit()

@app.route("/projects/new", methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def project_new():
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
       return redirect(url_for('project_work_queue'))
       
   if request.method == 'POST':
       name = request.form.get('name')
       system_name = request.form.get('system_name')
       location_id = request.form.get('location_id')
       vendor_partner = request.form.get('vendor_partner')
       project_owner = request.form.get('project_owner')
       priority = request.form.get('priority', 'medium')
       deadline_str = request.form.get('deadline')
       budget_code = request.form.get('budget_code')
       
       deadline = None
       if deadline_str:
           try:
               deadline = datetime.strptime(deadline_str, '%Y-%m-%d')
           except ValueError: pass
           
       # Handle New Location creation
       new_loc_name = request.form.get('new_location_name')
       if new_loc_name:
           new_loc = AssetLocation(
               name=new_loc_name,
               city=request.form.get('new_location_city'),
               lat=float(request.form.get('new_location_lat')) if request.form.get('new_location_lat') else None,
               lng=float(request.form.get('new_location_lng')) if request.form.get('new_location_lng') else None,
               asset_type=system_name,
               status='active'
           )
           db.session.add(new_loc)
           db.session.flush() # Get the ID
           location_id = new_loc.id
           
       proj = Project(
           ticket_id=gen_project_ticket_id(),
           name=name,
           project_owner=project_owner,
           system_name=system_name,
           location_id=int(location_id) if location_id else None,
           vendor_partner=vendor_partner,
           priority=priority,
           deadline=deadline,
           budget_code=budget_code,
           created_by_id=current_user.id,
           status='created'
       )
       db.session.add(proj)
       db.session.flush() # Get the ID

       # Initialize Handover Tracker
       new_ho = HandoverTracker(
           project_id=proj.id,
           location_id=proj.location_id,
           status='Draft',
           created_by_id=current_user.id,
           historical_log=json.dumps([{
               'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
               'actor': current_user.full_name or current_user.username,
               'action': 'Project Created',
               'note': f'Project assigned to Build team. Handover tracker initialized.'
           }])
       )
       db.session.add(new_ho)
       
       # Lead assignment
       lead_id = request.form.get('lead_id')
       if lead_id:
           lead_asm = ProjectAssignment(project_id=proj.id, user_id=int(lead_id), role='lead')
           db.session.add(lead_asm)
           add_notification(int(lead_id), f"Anda ditugaskan pada project {proj.name}", url_for('project_dispatcher'))
           
       # Member assignments
       member_ids = request.form.getlist('member_ids')
       for mid in member_ids:
           if mid and mid != lead_id:
               asm = ProjectAssignment(project_id=proj.id, user_id=int(mid), role='member')
               db.session.add(asm)
               add_notification(int(mid), f"Anda ditugaskan pada project {proj.name}", url_for('project_dispatcher'))
               
       # Attachments (Technical Spec)
       files = request.files.getlist('attachments')
       for f in files:
           fname = save_file(f, 'proj')
           if fname:
               att = ProjectAttachment(project_id=proj.id, filename=fname, file_type='tech_spec')
               db.session.add(att)
               
       # Tasks (Checklist)
       tasks = request.form.getlist('tasks')
       for t_title in tasks:
           if t_title.strip():
               db.session.add(ProjectTask(project_id=proj.id, title=t_title.strip()))
               
       # Gather team names for personnel log
       team_names = []
       if lead_id:
           lu = User.query.get(int(lead_id))
           if lu: team_names.append(lu.full_name or lu.username)
       for mid in member_ids:
           if mid and mid != lead_id:
               mu = User.query.get(int(mid))
               if mu: team_names.append(mu.full_name or mu.username)
               
       # Initial Activity - Assigned to LEAD
       act = ProjectActivity(
           project_id=proj.id,
           engineer_id=int(lead_id) if lead_id else current_user.id,
           personnel_log=", ".join(team_names) if team_names else "Team Assigned",
           description="Task Created: Project initialized and team assigned.",
           status='approved',
           submitted_at=get_wib(),
           progress_snap=0
       )
       db.session.add(act)
       
       db.session.commit()
       
       # Telegram Notification
       settings = get_settings()
       if settings.telegram_enabled and settings.tg_build_enabled:
           loc_name = "No Site"
           if proj.location:
               loc_name = proj.location.name
               
           # Get lead user for notification
           lead_user_name = "—"
           if lead_id:
               lu = db.session.get(User, int(lead_id))
               if lu:
                   lead_user_name = lu.full_name or lu.username
               
           base_url = settings.base_url.rstrip('/') if settings.base_url else f"{request.scheme}://{request.host}"
           project_url = f"{base_url}{url_for('project_detail', pid=proj.id)}"
           
           msg = (
               f"🏗️ <b>Project Baru Dibuat!</b>\n"
               f"📂 <b>Project:</b> {html.escape(proj.name or '—')}\n"
               f"⚙️ <b>System:</b> {html.escape(proj.system_name or '—')}\n"
               f"📍 <b>Lokasi:</b> {html.escape(loc_name)}\n"
               f"🔴 <b>Priority:</b> {proj.priority.upper()}\n"
               f"📅 <b>Deadline:</b> {proj.deadline.strftime('%d/%m/%Y') if proj.deadline else '—'}\n"
               f"👤 <b>Pemberi Task:</b> {html.escape(current_user.full_name or current_user.username)}\n"
               f"👷 <b>Penerima Task:</b> {html.escape(lead_user_name)}\n"
               f"\n"
               f"👷 <b>Team assigned.</b> Cek dashboard Project Dispatcher!\n"
               f"🔗 <b>Project Detail:</b> {project_url}"
           )
           send_telegram(msg)
           
       flash(f"Project berhasil dibuat! Notifikasi Telegram terkirim ke Lead.")
       
       return redirect(url_for('project_work_queue'))

   sites = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name).all()
   # Users for assignment (Build and Run)
   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['build', 'run', 'admin', 'lead_engineer'])).all()
   device_types = DropdownOption.get('device_types') or DEFAULT_DEVICE_TYPES
   settings = get_settings()
   return render_template('create_project.html', 
       sites=sites, 
       engineers=engineers, 
       device_types=device_types,
       urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS,
       settings=settings)


@app.route("/projects/<int:pid>")
@login_required
def project_detail(pid):
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   # Check if user has access (assigned or admin)
   is_assigned = ProjectAssignment.query.filter_by(project_id=pid, user_id=current_user.id).first()
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'build'] and not is_assigned and proj.created_by_id != current_user.id:
       return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
   
   settings = get_settings()
   linked_survey = Survey.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).first()
   return render_template('project_detail.html', project=proj, settings=settings, linked_survey=linked_survey)

@app.route("/projects/<int:pid>/edit", methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def project_edit(pid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
       abort(403)
       
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   
   if request.method == 'POST':
       proj.name = request.form.get('name')
       proj.project_owner = request.form.get('project_owner')
       proj.system_name = request.form.get('system_name')
       proj.location_id = int(request.form.get('location_id')) if request.form.get('location_id') else None
       proj.vendor_partner = request.form.get('vendor_partner')
       proj.priority = request.form.get('priority', 'medium')
       proj.budget_code = request.form.get('budget_code')
       
       deadline_str = request.form.get('deadline')
       if deadline_str:
           try:
               proj.deadline = datetime.strptime(deadline_str, '%Y-%m-%d')
           except ValueError: pass
           
       db.session.commit()
       flash(f"Project '{proj.name}' berhasil diperbarui.")
       return redirect(url_for('project_detail', pid=pid))

   sites = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name).all()
   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['build', 'run', 'admin', 'lead_engineer'])).all()
   device_types = DropdownOption.get('device_types') or DEFAULT_DEVICE_TYPES
   settings = get_settings()
   
   return render_template('edit_project.html', 
       project=proj,
       sites=sites, 
       engineers=engineers, 
       device_types=device_types,
       urgency_levels=URGENCY_LEVELS,
       settings=settings)

@app.route("/projects/<int:pid>/add-progress", methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def project_add_progress(pid):
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   
   if request.method == 'POST':
       description = request.form.get('description')
       vendor_partner = request.form.get('vendor_partner')
       personnel_log = request.form.get('personnel_log')
       tasks_checked = request.form.getlist('tasks_checked') # List of task IDs
       
       files = request.files.getlist('attachments')
       saved_files = []
       for f in files:
           if f and f.filename:
               fname = save_file(f, 'proj')
               if fname:
                   saved_files.append(fname)
       
       # Mark tasks as done
       for tid in tasks_checked:
           task = ProjectTask.query.get(tid)
           if task and task.project_id == pid:
               task.is_done = True
       
       db.session.flush() # Ensure tasks are updated before calculation
       
       # Calculate Progress Snapshot
       total_tasks = len(proj.tasks)
       done_tasks = len([t for t in proj.tasks if t.is_done])
       snap_pct = int(done_tasks / total_tasks * 100) if total_tasks > 0 else 0

       # Create Activity
       activity = ProjectActivity(
           project_id=pid,
           engineer_id=current_user.id,
           personnel_log=personnel_log,
           vendor_partner=vendor_partner,
           description=description,
           tasks_checked=json.dumps(tasks_checked),
           status='pending',
           progress_snap=snap_pct
       )
       db.session.add(activity)
       db.session.flush()
       
       # Add Attachments
       for fname in saved_files:
           att = ProjectAttachment(
               project_id=pid,
               activity_id=activity.id,
               filename=fname,
               file_type='progress_report'
           )
           db.session.add(att)
            
       # Update Handover Tracker Historical Log
       ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).first()
       if ho:
           log = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
           log.append({
               'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
               'actor': current_user.full_name or current_user.username,
               'action': 'Progress Update',
               'note': f'Activity submitted: {description[:100]}... (Progress: {snap_pct}%)'
           })
           ho.historical_log = json.dumps(log)
           if snap_pct >= 100:
               ho.status = 'In Review'

       db.session.commit()
       
       # Notify Lead/Admin
       settings = get_settings()
       if settings.telegram_enabled:
           msg = (
               f"🏗️ <b>Progress Report Baru!</b>\n"
               f"📂 <b>Project:</b> {html.escape(proj.name)}\n"
               f"👤 <b>Engineer:</b> {current_user.full_name or current_user.username}\n"
               f"📝 <b>Status:</b> Menunggu Review"
           )
           send_telegram(msg)
       
       flash('Progress report submitted and awaiting review.')
       return redirect(url_for('project_detail', pid=pid))

   engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['build', 'run', 'admin', 'lead_engineer'])).all()
   settings = get_settings()
   return render_template('project_add_progress.html', project=proj, engineers=engineers, settings=settings, now=datetime.utcnow())


@app.route('/projects/<int:pid>/progress/lpr', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def project_progress_lpr(pid):
    proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
    
    # Update AssetLocation
    if proj.location:
        loc = proj.location
        loc.cp_manager = request.form.get('cp_manager')
        loc.cp_manager_phone = request.form.get('cp_manager_phone')
        loc.cp_manager_email = request.form.get('cp_manager_email')
        loc.area_manager = request.form.get('area_manager')
        loc.area_manager_phone = request.form.get('area_manager_phone')
        loc.area_manager_email = request.form.get('area_manager_email')
        
        # NUC Timeline
        from datetime import datetime
        def parse_date(d): return datetime.strptime(d, '%Y-%m-%d').date() if d else None
        
        loc.nuc_received_date = parse_date(request.form.get('nuc_received_date'))
        loc.nuc_setup_date = parse_date(request.form.get('nuc_setup_date'))
        loc.nuc_handover_date = parse_date(request.form.get('nuc_handover_date'))
        loc.nuc_handover_details = request.form.get('nuc_handover_details')
        
        # Attachment
        f = request.files.get('nuc_handover_att')
        if f and f.filename:
            from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
            import uuid
            ext = f.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() if '.' in f.filename else ''
            fname = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{ext}"
            f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            loc.nuc_handover_att = fname
        
        # Process Multi-Device (Servers)
        server_ids = request.form.getlist('server_id[]')
        server_hostnames = request.form.getlist('server_hostname[]')
        server_device_names = request.form.getlist('server_device_name[]')
        server_ips = request.form.getlist('server_ip[]')
        server_types = request.form.getlist('server_type[]')
        server_ssh1s = request.form.getlist('server_ssh1[]')
        server_ssh2s = request.form.getlist('server_ssh2[]')
        server_anydesks = request.form.getlist('server_anydesk_id[]')
        server_pws = request.form.getlist('server_password[]')
        server_tails = request.form.getlist('server_tailscale[]')
        server_matas = request.form.getlist('server_mata_ver[]')
        server_alprs = request.form.getlist('server_alpr_ver[]')
        server_abtools = request.form.getlist('server_abtool[]')
        server_grafanas = request.form.getlist('server_grafana[]')
        
        for i in range(len(server_hostnames)):
            sid = server_ids[i] if i < len(server_ids) else None
            dev = None
            if sid:
                dev = AssetDevice.query.get(sid)
            
            if not dev:
                dev = AssetDevice(location_id=loc.id, device_category='server')
                db.session.add(dev)
            
            dev.hostname_role = server_hostnames[i]
            dev.device_name = server_device_names[i] if i < len(server_device_names) else None
            dev.ip_address = server_ips[i] if i < len(server_ips) else None
            dev.device_type = server_types[i] if i < len(server_types) else None
            dev.ssh1_port = server_ssh1s[i] if i < len(server_ssh1s) else None
            dev.ssh2_port = server_ssh2s[i] if i < len(server_ssh2s) else None
            dev.anydesk_id = server_anydesks[i] if i < len(server_anydesks) else None
            dev.password = server_pws[i] if i < len(server_pws) else None
            dev.tailscale_ip = server_tails[i] if i < len(server_tails) else None
            dev.mata_version = server_matas[i] if i < len(server_matas) else None
            dev.alpr_version = server_alprs[i] if i < len(server_alprs) else None
            dev.has_abtool = (server_abtools[i] == '1') if i < len(server_abtools) else False
            dev.has_grafana = (server_grafanas[i] == '1') if i < len(server_grafanas) else False
            
            if not dev.device_name:
                dev.device_name = f"Server {dev.hostname_role}" if dev.hostname_role else "New Server"

        db.session.add(loc)
        db.session.flush()
        _sync_loc_counts(loc.id)

    # Mark Task as Done
    tid = request.form.get('task_id')
    task = ProjectTask.query.get(tid)
    if task and task.project_id == pid:
        task.is_done = True
    
    # Create or Append Activity
    total_tasks = len(proj.tasks)
    done_tasks = len([t for t in proj.tasks if t.is_done])
    snap_pct = int(done_tasks / total_tasks * 100) if total_tasks > 0 else 0
    
    # Check for existing activity for this task
    activities = ProjectActivity.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).order_by(ProjectActivity.submitted_at.desc()).all()
    existing = next((a for a in activities if tid in (json.loads(a.tasks_checked) if a.tasks_checked else [])), None)

    if existing:
        # Append revision
        revs = json.loads(existing.revisions) if (hasattr(existing, 'revisions') and existing.revisions) else []
        revs.append({
            'submitted_at': get_wib().isoformat(),
            'engineer_name': current_user.full_name,
            'description': 'Instal Engine LPR: data updated & synchronized',
            'progress_snap': snap_pct
        })
        existing.revisions = json.dumps(revs)
        existing.progress_snap = snap_pct
    else:
        activity = ProjectActivity(
            project_id=pid,
            engineer_id=current_user.id,
            description=f'Instal Engine LPR: {len(server_hostnames)} devices updated & synchronized to Asset Monitoring.',
            tasks_checked=json.dumps([tid]) if tid else '[]',
            status='approved',
            progress_snap=snap_pct
        )
        db.session.add(activity)
    
    db.session.commit()
    
    return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route('/projects/<int:pid>/progress/deployment', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def project_progress_deployment(pid):
    proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
    
    if proj.location:
        loc = proj.location
        loc.lpr_db_main_server = request.form.get('lpr_db_main_server')
        loc.lpr_db_name = request.form.get('lpr_db_name')
        loc.lpr_db_restore_status = (request.form.get('lpr_db_restore_status') == '1')
        loc.parkee_redis_host = request.form.get('parkee_redis_host')
        
        from datetime import datetime
        def parse_date(d): return datetime.strptime(d, '%Y-%m-%d').date() if d else None
        
        # ETL Activation
        loc.etl_activated_by = request.form.get('etl_activated_by')
        loc.etl_activated_at = parse_date(request.form.get('etl_activated_at'))
        loc.etl_status = request.form.get('etl_status')
        
        f_etl = request.files.get('etl_attachment')
        if f_etl and f_etl.filename:
            import uuid
            ext = f_etl.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() if '.' in f_etl.filename else ''
            fname = f"etl_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{ext}"
            f_etl.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            loc.etl_attachment = fname
            
        # Parkee Integration
        loc.parkee_integration_by = request.form.get('parkee_integration_by')
        loc.parkee_integration_at = parse_date(request.form.get('parkee_integration_at'))
        loc.parkee_integration_status = request.form.get('parkee_integration_status')
        
        f_int = request.files.get('parkee_integration_attachment')
        if f_int and f_int.filename:
            import uuid
            ext = f_int.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() if '.' in f_int.filename else ''
            fname = f"int_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{ext}"
            f_int.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            loc.parkee_integration_attachment = fname

        # Device/Camera Credentials
        dev_ids = request.form.getlist('dev_id[]')
        dev_names = request.form.getlist('dev_gate_name[]')
        dev_ips = request.form.getlist('dev_ip[]')
        dev_users = request.form.getlist('dev_username[]')
        dev_pws = request.form.getlist('dev_password[]')
        dev_ws_ports = request.form.getlist('dev_ws_port[]')
        dev_anpr_ports = request.form.getlist('dev_anpr_port[]')
        dev_mapping = request.form.getlist('dev_mapping[]')
        
        # Camera Specific Configs (Dropdowns)
        dev_resolutions = request.form.getlist('dev_resolution[]')
        dev_fps_list = request.form.getlist('dev_fps[]')
        dev_bitrates = request.form.getlist('dev_bitrate[]')
        dev_brands = request.form.getlist('dev_brand[]')
        
        for i in range(len(dev_ips)):
            did = dev_ids[i] if i < len(dev_ids) else None
            dev = None
            if did:
                dev = AssetDevice.query.get(did)
            
            if not dev:
                dev = AssetDevice(location_id=loc.id, device_category='camera')
                db.session.add(dev)
            
            dev.gate_name = dev_names[i] if i < len(dev_names) else None
            dev.ip_address = dev_ips[i] if i < len(dev_ips) else None
            dev.username = dev_users[i] if i < len(dev_users) else None
            dev.password = dev_pws[i] if i < len(dev_pws) else None
            dev.ws_lpr_port = dev_ws_ports[i] if i < len(dev_ws_ports) else None
            dev.anpr_port = dev_anpr_ports[i] if i < len(dev_anpr_ports) else None
            dev.notes = dev_mapping[i] if i < len(dev_mapping) else None # Using notes for server mapping
            
            dev.resolution = dev_resolutions[i] if i < len(dev_resolutions) else None
            dev.frame_rate = dev_fps_list[i] if i < len(dev_fps_list) else None # Using frame_rate field (sync with assets)
            dev.bitrate = dev_bitrates[i] if i < len(dev_bitrates) else None
            dev.brand_model = dev_brands[i] if i < len(dev_brands) else None

        db.session.add(loc)
        db.session.commit()

        if request.form.get('is_auto_save') == '1':
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "message": "Auto-saved"})

    # Mark Task as Done
    tid = request.form.get('task_id')
    task = ProjectTask.query.get(tid)
    if task and task.project_id == pid:
        task.is_done = True
    
    # Create or Append Activity
    total_tasks = len(proj.tasks)
    done_tasks = len([t for t in proj.tasks if t.is_done])
    snap_pct = int(done_tasks / total_tasks * 100) if total_tasks > 0 else 0
    
    # Check for existing activity for this task
    activities = ProjectActivity.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).order_by(ProjectActivity.submitted_at.desc()).all()
    existing = next((a for a in activities if tid in (json.loads(a.tasks_checked) if a.tasks_checked else [])), None)

    if existing:
        # Append revision
        revs = json.loads(existing.revisions) if (hasattr(existing, 'revisions') and existing.revisions) else []
        revs.append({
            'submitted_at': get_wib().isoformat(),
            'engineer_name': current_user.full_name,
            'description': 'Deployment Checklist Updated/Revised',
            'progress_snap': snap_pct
        })
        existing.revisions = json.dumps(revs)
        existing.progress_snap = snap_pct
    else:
        activity = ProjectActivity(
            project_id=pid,
            engineer_id=current_user.id,
            description=f'Deployment Checklist Completed: Database, Environment, Camera Config, and Activations synchronized.',
            tasks_checked=json.dumps([tid]) if tid else '[]',
            status='approved',
            progress_snap=snap_pct
        )
        db.session.add(activity)
    
    db.session.commit()
    
    return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route("/projects/activity/<int:aid>/review", methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def project_activity_review(aid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead']:
       abort(403)
       
   activity = ProjectActivity.query.get_or_404(aid)
   action = request.form.get('action') # approve / revise
   notes = request.form.get('manager_notes')
   
   activity.status = 'approved' if action == 'approve' else 'revised'
   activity.manager_notes = notes
   activity.approved_by_id = current_user.id
   activity.approved_at = datetime.utcnow()
   
   if action == 'approve':
       # Mark tasks as done
       try:
           tasks_done = json.loads(activity.tasks_checked or '[]')
           for tid in tasks_done:
               task = ProjectTask.query.get(int(tid))
               if task and task.project_id == activity.project_id:
                   task.is_done = True
       except: pass
       
       # Update project status if needed
       proj = Project.query.get(activity.project_id)
       if proj and proj.status == 'created':
           proj.status = 'in_progress'
           
   db.session.commit()
   
   # Notify Engineer
   settings = get_settings()
   if settings.telegram_enabled:
       status_text = "DISETUJUI" if action == 'approve' else "PERLU REVISI"
       msg = (
           f"✅ <b>Progress Report {status_text}</b>\n"
           f"📂 <b>Project:</b> {html.escape(activity.project.name)}\n"
           f"💬 <b>Catatan:</b> {html.escape(notes or '—')}"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)
   
   flash(f'Activity report has been {activity.status}.')
   return redirect(url_for('project_detail', pid=activity.project_id))

@app.route("/api/projects", methods=["GET"])
@login_required
def api_projects_list():
   role = current_user.role
   q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
   status = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
   date_from = request.args.get('date_from', '')
   date_to = request.args.get('date_to', '')

   if role in ['admin', 'build']:
       query = Project.query
   else:
       # Show projects assigned to user or created by user (e.g. for run role if they have minimal access)
       query = Project.query.outerjoin(ProjectAssignment).filter(
           (ProjectAssignment.user_id == current_user.id) | (Project.created_by_id == current_user.id)
       ).distinct()

   if q:
       query = query.filter(Project.name.ilike(f'%{q}%'))
   
   if status == 'pending_approval':
       # Projects with any activity in 'pending' status
       query = query.join(ProjectActivity).filter(ProjectActivity.status == 'pending')
   elif status:
       query = query.filter(Project.status == status)

   if date_from:
       try:
           df = datetime.strptime(date_from, '%Y-%m-%d')
           query = query.filter(Project.created_at >= df)
       except ValueError: pass
   if date_to:
       try:
           dt = datetime.strptime(date_to, '%Y-%m-%d') + timedelta(days=1)
           query = query.filter(Project.created_at < dt)
       except ValueError: pass

   projects = query.order_by(Project.created_at.desc()).all()
   
   # Calculate summary counts for the user
   # (Re-run base query for counts without status filter)
   if role in ['admin', 'build']:
       base_cnt = Project.query
   else:
       base_cnt = Project.query.outerjoin(ProjectAssignment).filter(
           (ProjectAssignment.user_id == current_user.id) | (Project.created_by_id == current_user.id)
       ).distinct()
   
   # Simple count logic
   res = []
   for p in projects:
       d = p.to_dict()
       res.append(d)

   summary = {
       'total': base_cnt.count(),
       'new': base_cnt.filter(Project.status == 'created').count(),
       'in_progress': base_cnt.filter(Project.status == 'in_progress').count(),
       'pending_approval': base_cnt.join(ProjectActivity).filter(ProjectActivity.status == 'pending').distinct().count(),
       'completed': base_cnt.filter(Project.status.in_(['completed', 'closed'])).count(),
   }

   return jsonify({'projects': res, 'summary': summary})

@app.route("/api/projects", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_project_create():
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   # Handle both JSON and Form Data
   if request.is_json:
       data = request.json
   else:
       data = request.form

   name = data.get('name')
   if not name: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Project name is required"}), 400
   
   # Handle Tech Spec File
   tech_spec_filename = None
   if 'tech_spec_file' in request.files:
       file = request.files['tech_spec_file']
       if file and file.filename:
           tech_spec_filename = save_file(file, prefix='spec')

   # Create Project
   new_proj = Project(
       ticket_id=gen_project_ticket_id(),
       name=name,
       project_owner=data.get('project_owner'),
       system_name=data.get('system_name'),
       location_id=data.get('location_id'),
       vendor_partner=data.get('vendor_partner'),
       priority=data.get('priority', 'medium'),
       deadline=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get('deadline')) if data.get('deadline') else None,
       budget_code=data.get('budget_code'),
       tech_spec_file=tech_spec_filename,
       created_by_id=current_user.id
   )
   db.session.add(new_proj)
   db.session.flush() # Get ID
   
   # Add Checklist Tasks
   tasks_raw = data.get('tasks', '[]')
   try:
       tasks = json.loads(tasks_raw) if isinstance(tasks_raw, str) else tasks_raw
   except:
       tasks = []
       
   for t_title in tasks:
       if t_title.strip():
           db.session.add(ProjectTask(project_id=new_proj.id, title=t_title.strip()))
           
   # Initial Assignment (Lead & Member)
   lead_id = data.get('lead_id')
   member_id = data.get('member_id')
   team_names = []
   
   if lead_id:
       try:
           l_id = int(lead_id)
           db.session.add(ProjectAssignment(project_id=new_proj.id, user_id=l_id, role='lead'))
           
           lu = User.query.get(l_id)
           if lu: 
               team_names.append(lu.full_name or lu.username)
               add_notification(l_id, f"🚀 NEW PROJECT: {new_proj.name}. Silakan mulai pengerjaan SSR.", f"/projects/{new_proj.id}")
       except (ValueError, TypeError): pass
       
   if member_id:
       try:
           m_id = int(member_id)
           if m_id != int(lead_id or 0): # Avoid duplicate if same person selected
               db.session.add(ProjectAssignment(project_id=new_proj.id, user_id=m_id, role='member'))
               mu = User.query.get(m_id)
               if mu: 
                   team_names.append(mu.full_name or mu.username)
                   add_notification(m_id, f"🚀 NEW PROJECT: {new_proj.name}. Anda ditugaskan sebagai MEMBER.", f"/projects/{new_proj.id}")
       except (ValueError, TypeError): pass
       
   # Initial Activity
   act = ProjectActivity(
       project_id=new_proj.id,
       engineer_id=int(lead_id) if lead_id else current_user.id,
       personnel_log=", ".join(team_names) if team_names else "Team Assigned",
       description=f"Project Initialized. Technical Specs: {'Attached' if tech_spec_filename else 'None'}",
       status='approved',
       submitted_at=get_wib(),
       progress_snap=0
   )
   db.session.add(act)
   db.session.commit()
   
   # Telegram Notification
   st = get_settings()
   if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_build_enabled:
       msg = (
           f"🚀 <b>NEW PROJECT DISPATCHED</b>\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<b>Project:</b> {html.escape(name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>System:</b> {html.escape(new_proj.system_name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Priority:</b> {new_proj.priority.upper()}\n"
           f"<b>Lead:</b> {team_names[0] if team_names else '—'}\n"
           f"<b>Spec:</b> {'✅ Attached' if tech_spec_filename else '❌ None'}\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)
   
   return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": new_proj.id})

@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>", methods=["GET"])
@login_required
def api_project_get(pid):
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   survey = Survey.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).order_by(Survey.created_at.desc()).first()
   
   data = proj.to_dict()
   
   # Retroactive Sync: If SSR is approved, ensure task is marked done and activity exists
   if survey and survey.status == 'Approved':
      ssr_task = next((t for t in proj.tasks if 'Site Survey Report' in t.title or 'SSR' in t.title), None)
      
      # If task doesn't exist, create it as done
      if not ssr_task:
         ssr_task = ProjectTask(
            project_id=pid,
            title='Site Survey Report (SSR)',
            is_done=True
         )
         db.session.add(ssr_task)
         db.session.commit()
         proj = Project.query.get(pid) # Refresh tasks

      # Check if an activity for this SSR approval already exists
      ssr_activity_exists = ProjectActivity.query.filter_by(
         project_id=pid,
         status='approved'
      ).filter(ProjectActivity.description.like(f'%SSR {survey.ssr_number}%')).first()

      if not ssr_task.is_done or not ssr_activity_exists:
         ssr_task.is_done = True
         if not ssr_activity_exists:
            db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
               project_id=pid,
               engineer_id=current_user.id,
               description=f'Otomatis: Task "{ssr_task.title}" selesai karena SSR {survey.ssr_number} sudah Approved.',
               progress_snap=int(len([t for t in proj.tasks if t.is_done]) / len(proj.tasks) * 100) if proj.tasks else 0,
               status='approved'
            ))
         db.session.commit()
         data = proj.to_dict() # Refresh data

   # Retroactive Sync for BA Live
   if proj.location:
      ba_live = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(
         lokasi_text=proj.location.name,
         ba_type='live'
      ).order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).first()
      
      if ba_live:
         ba_live_task = next((t for t in proj.tasks if 'BA Live' in t.title), None)
         
         # If task doesn't exist, create it as done
         if not ba_live_task:
            ba_live_task = ProjectTask(
               project_id=pid,
               title='Create BA Live',
               is_done=True
            )
            db.session.add(ba_live_task)
            db.session.commit()
            proj = Project.query.get(pid) # Refresh tasks
            ba_live_task = next((t for t in proj.tasks if 'BA Live' in t.title), None)

         if ba_live_task and not ba_live_task.is_done:
            ba_live_task.is_done = True
            db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
               project_id=pid,
               engineer_id=current_user.id,
               personnel_log=f"Completed: {ba_live_task.title}",
               description=f'Otomatis: Task "{ba_live_task.title}" selesai karena BA Live {ba_live.ba_number} sudah tersedia.',
               progress_snap=100,
               status='approved'
            ))
            db.session.commit()
            data = proj.to_dict()

   # Retroactive Sync for BA Optimasi
   if proj.location:
      ba_opt = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(
         lokasi_text=proj.location.name,
         ba_type='optimasi'
      ).order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).first()
      
      if ba_opt:
         ba_opt_task = next((t for t in proj.tasks if 'BA Optimasi' in t.title), None)
         
         # If task doesn't exist, create it as done
         if not ba_opt_task:
            ba_opt_task = ProjectTask(
               project_id=pid,
               title='Create BA Optimasi',
               is_done=True
            )
            db.session.add(ba_opt_task)
            db.session.commit()
            proj = Project.query.get(pid) # Refresh tasks
            ba_opt_task = next((t for t in proj.tasks if 'BA Optimasi' in t.title), None)

         if ba_opt_task and not ba_opt_task.is_done:
            ba_opt_task.is_done = True
            db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
               project_id=pid,
               engineer_id=current_user.id,
               personnel_log=f"Completed: {ba_opt_task.title}",
               description=f'Otomatis: Task "{ba_opt_task.title}" selesai karena BA Optimasi {ba_opt.ba_number} sudah tersedia.',
               progress_snap=100,
               status='approved'
            ))
            db.session.commit()
            data = proj.to_dict()
            
   data['activities'] = [a.to_dict() for a in proj.activities]
   data['tasks'] = [{'id': t.id, 'title': t.title, 'is_done': t.is_done} for t in proj.tasks]
   data['linked_survey'] = {
      'id': survey.id,
      'ssr_number': survey.ssr_number,
      'status': survey.status
   } if survey else None
   
   return jsonify(data)
@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/tasks", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_project_add_task(pid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'build']:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   data = request.json
   title = data.get('title')
   if not title: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Task title is required"}), 400
   
   task = ProjectTask(project_id=pid, title=title)
   db.session.add(task)
   db.session.commit()
   
   return jsonify({"ok": True, "id": task.id})


@app.route("/api/tasks/<int:tid>", methods=["PUT", "DELETE"])
@login_required
def api_task_crud(tid):
    if current_user.role != 'admin':
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
    
    task = ProjectTask.query.get_or_404(tid)
    
    if request.method == "DELETE":
        db.session.delete(task)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({"ok": True})
    
    if request.method == "PUT":
        data = request.json
        task.title = data.get('title', task.title)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/assign", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_project_assign(pid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'build']:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   data = request.json
   user_id = data.get('user_id')
   role = data.get('role', 'member')
   
   if not user_id: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "User ID is required"}), 400
   
   # Check if already assigned
   existing = ProjectAssignment.query.filter_by(project_id=pid, user_id=user_id).first()
   if existing:
       existing.role = role
   else:
       db.session.add(ProjectAssignment(project_id=pid, user_id=user_id, role=role))
       
   db.session.commit()
   p_name = Project.query.get(pid).name
   add_notification(user_id, f"Anda ditugaskan pada project {p_name}", url_for('project_dispatcher'))
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/activities", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_project_submit_activity(pid):
   # Only assigned team members can submit
   is_assigned = ProjectAssignment.query.filter_by(project_id=pid, user_id=current_user.id).first()
   if not is_assigned and current_user.role != 'admin':
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   desc = request.form.get('description')
   if not desc: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Description is required"}), 400
   
   activity = ProjectActivity(
       project_id=pid,
       engineer_id=current_user.id,
       personnel_log=request.form.get('personnel_log'),
       description=desc,
       status='pending'
   )
   db.session.add(activity)
   db.session.flush()
   
   # Handle Attachments
   files = request.files.getlist('attachments')
   for f in files:
       if f and f.filename:
           fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}_{f.filename}")
           f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
           db.session.add(ProjectAttachment(
               project_id=pid, activity_id=activity.id, filename=fname, file_type='report'
           ))
           
   # Update Task Checklist
   task_ids = request.form.getlist('tasks_done') # IDs of completed tasks
   for tid in task_ids:
       t = ProjectTask.query.get(tid)
       if t and t.project_id == pid:
           t.is_done = True
           
   db.session.commit()
   notify_mentions(desc, f"Anda dimention dalam activity project {Project.query.get(pid).name}", url_for("project_dispatcher"))
   db.session.commit()
   
   # Notify Manager
   st = get_settings()
   if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_build_enabled:
       proj = db.session.get(Project, pid)
       msg = (
           f"📝 <b>PROGRESS SUBMITTED</b>\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<b>Project:</b> {html.escape(proj.name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Engineer:</b> {html.escape(current_user.full_name or current_user.username)}\n"
           f"<b>Status:</b> Pending Review\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)
   
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/activities/<int:aid>/review", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_activity_review(aid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'build']:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   data = request.json
   status = data.get('status') # approved, revised
   activity = ProjectActivity.query.get_or_404(aid)
   
   activity.status = status
   activity.approved_by_id = current_user.id
   activity.approved_at = datetime.utcnow()
   activity.manager_notes = data.get('notes')
   
   db.session.commit()
   
   # Notify Engineer
   st = get_settings()
   if st.telegram_enabled and st.tg_build_enabled:
       status_text = 'APPROVED' if status=='approved' else 'REVISION REQUIRED'
       msg = (
           f"{'✅' if status=='approved' else '⚠️'} <b>ACTIVITY {status_text}</b>\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
           f"<b>Project:</b> {html.escape(activity.project.name or '—')}\n"
           f"<b>Notes:</b> {html.escape(activity.manager_notes or '-')}\n"
           f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
       )
       send_telegram(msg)
   
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/close", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def api_project_close(pid):
   if current_user.role not in ['admin', 'build']:
       return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
       
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   data = request.json
   
   # Capture Signatures
   sigs = data.get('signatures', [])
   for s in sigs:
       db.session.add(ProjectSignature(
           project_id=pid, user_id=s['user_id'], 
           signature_data=s['data'], signer_role=s['role']
       ))
       
   proj.status = 'closed'
   db.session.commit()
   
   return jsonify({"ok": True})

from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
from reportlab.lib.utils import ImageReader
import io

@app.route("/api/projects/<int:pid>/pdf")
@login_required
def api_project_pdf(pid):
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   
   buffer = io.BytesIO()
   c = canvas.Canvas(buffer, pagesize=A4)
   w, h = A4
   
   # Cover
   c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 24)
   c.drawCentredString(w/2, h-100, "PROJECT COMPLETION REPORT")
   c.setFont("Helvetica", 18)
   c.drawCentredString(w/2, h-140, proj.name)
   c.drawCentredString(w/2, h-170, f"System: {proj.system_name}")
   
   c.setFont("Helvetica", 12)
   c.drawString(50, h-250, f"Location: {proj.location.name if proj.location else '-'}")
   c.drawString(50, h-270, f"Vendor: {proj.vendor_partner or '-'}")
   c.drawString(50, h-290, f"Date: {datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y')}")
   
   # Timeline Summary
   c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 14)
   c.drawString(50, h-350, "ACTIVITY TIMELINE")
   y = h-380
   c.setFont("Helvetica", 10)
   for act in proj.activities:
       if act.status == 'approved':
           c.drawString(70, y, f"• {act.submitted_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}: {act.description[:100]}...")
           y -= 15
           if y < 50: c.showPage(); y = h-50
           
   # Signatures
   if y < 200: c.showPage(); y = h-50
   c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 14)
   c.drawString(50, y-50, "APPROVAL SIGN-OFF")
   
   sx = 50
   for sig in proj.signatures:
       c.setFont("Helvetica", 8)
       c.drawString(sx, y-180, f"{sig.signer_role.upper()}")
       c.drawString(sx, y-195, f"{sig.user.full_name}")
       if sig.user.job_position:
           c.drawString(sx, y-205, f"{sig.user.job_position}")
       sx += 180
       
   c.save()
   buffer.seek(0)
   return send_file(buffer, as_attachment=True, download_name=f"Report_{proj.name.replace(' ','_')}.pdf", mimetype='application/pdf')

@app.template_filter('fromjson')
def fromjson_filter(s):
   try: return json.loads(s or '[]')
   except: return []

@app.route('/surveys')
@login_required
def surveys():
   settings  = get_settings()
   q         = request.args.get('q','')
   status_f  = request.args.get('status','')
   type_f    = request.args.get('type','')
   date_from = request.args.get('date_from','')
   date_to   = request.args.get('date_to','')
   qry = Survey.query
   if q:
       like = f'%{q}%'
       qry  = qry.filter(db.or_(Survey.project_name.ilike(like), Survey.ssr_number.ilike(like),
                                 Survey.client_name.ilike(like), Survey.site_name.ilike(like)))
   if status_f: qry = qry.filter_by(status=status_f)
   if type_f:   qry = qry.filter_by(project_type=type_f)
   if date_from:
       try: qry = qry.filter(Survey.created_at >= datetime.strptime(date_from,'%Y-%m-%d'))
       except: pass
   if date_to:
       try: qry = qry.filter(Survey.created_at <= datetime.strptime(date_to,'%Y-%m-%d') + timedelta(days=1))
       except: pass
   return render_template('surveys.html', settings=settings,
       surveys=qry.order_by(Survey.created_at.desc()).all(),
       q=q, status_f=status_f, type_f=type_f,
       date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
       status_list=STATUS_LIST, project_types=PROJECT_TYPES)

@app.route('/surveys/new', methods=['GET','POST'])
@login_required
def survey_new():
   settings = get_settings()
   project_id = request.args.get('project_id')
   ticket_id  = request.args.get('ticket_id')
   
   prefill_project = None
   prefill_ticket  = None
   
   if project_id:
       prefill_project = Project.query.get(project_id)
   elif ticket_id:
       prefill_ticket = AssetIssue.query.get(ticket_id)

   if request.method == 'POST':
       ssr          = gen_ssr()
       company_logo = save_file(request.files.get('company_logo_upload'),'company') \
                      or request.form.get('company_logo_existing') or settings.company_logo
       client_logo  = save_file(request.files.get('client_logo_upload'),'client') \
                      or request.form.get('client_logo_existing')
       sd             = request.form.get('survey_date')
       initial_status = 'In Review' if prefill_project and 'LPR' in (prefill_project.system_name or '') else request.form.get('status','Draft')
       s_type = 'LPR' if prefill_project and 'LPR' in (prefill_project.system_name or '') else request.form.get('survey_type', 'General')
       s = Survey(
           ssr_number=ssr, version=request.form.get('version','1.0'),
           survey_type=s_type,
           project_type=request.form.get('project_type',''), project_name=request.form.get('project_name',''),
           project_code=gen_survey_project_code(), client_name=request.form.get('client_name',''),
           client_pic=request.form.get('client_pic',''), client_phone=request.form.get('client_phone',''),
           client_email=request.form.get('client_email',''), company_logo=company_logo,
           client_logo=client_logo, site_name=request.form.get('site_name',''),
           site_address=request.form.get('site_address',''), site_city=request.form.get('site_city',''),
           site_gps=request.form.get('site_gps',''),
           survey_date=datetime.strptime(sd,'%Y-%m-%d').date() if sd else date.today(),
           surveyor_name=request.form.get('surveyor_name',''), surveyor_role=request.form.get('surveyor_role',''),
           status=initial_status, readiness=request.form.get('readiness','hold'),
           form_data=json.dumps(build_form_data(request)), created_by=current_user.id,
           additional_surveyors=request.form.get('additional_surveyors_json','[]'))
       db.session.add(s)
       db.session.flush()

       # Link to Project & Update Handover Tracker
       p_id = request.form.get('project_id') or project_id
       if p_id:
           s.project_id = int(p_id)
           ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(project_id=int(p_id)).first()
           if ho:
               ho.survey_id = s.id
               log = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
               log.append({
                   'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
                   'actor': current_user.full_name or current_user.username,
                   'action': 'SSR Created',
                   'note': f'SSR {s.ssr_number} has been initialized.'
               })
               ho.historical_log = json.dumps(log)

       # Handle attachments
       for file in request.files.getlist('attachment_files'):
           fname, ftype = save_attachment(file, 'att')
           if fname:
               att = SurveyAttachment(
                   survey_id=s.id, filename=fname,
                   original_name=file.filename,
                   file_type='pdf' if ftype=='pdf' else 'image',
                   section=request.form.get('attachment_section','findings'),
                   caption=request.form.get('attachment_caption',''),
                   uploaded_by=current_user.id)
               db.session.add(att)

       add_timeline(s, 'create', f'SSR {ssr} dibuat', status_to=initial_status)
       if initial_status == 'In Review':
           add_timeline(s, 'submit', 'Disubmit untuk review SPV/Manager',
                        status_from='Draft', status_to='In Review')
           notify_admins(f'📤 SSR {ssr} disubmit oleh {current_user.full_name}',
                         link=url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=s.id, _external=False))
           telegram_notify_ssr(s, 'submit')
       else:
           telegram_notify_ssr(s, 'create')

       db.session.commit()
       add_notification(current_user.id, f"SSR {ssr} berhasil dibuat", url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=s.id))
       try:
           surveyors = json.loads(s.additional_surveyors or '[]')
           for sid in surveyors:
               add_notification(int(sid), f"Anda ditugaskan pada SSR {ssr} di {s.site_name}", url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=s.id))
       except: pass
       return redirect(url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=s.id))
   
   all_engineers = User.query.filter(User.is_active_acc==True).order_by(User.full_name).all()
   return render_template('survey_form.html', settings=settings, project_types=PROJECT_TYPES,
       status_list=STATUS_LIST,
       ssr_preview=f"{settings.ssr_prefix}/{datetime.utcnow().year}/{settings.ssr_counter+1:04d}",
       prj_code_preview=f"ASN-PRJ-{datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y%m')}-{settings.survey_counter+1:04d}",
       survey=None, mode='new', all_engineers=all_engineers,
       prefill_project=prefill_project, prefill_ticket=prefill_ticket)

@app.route('/surveys/<int:survey_id>')
@login_required
def survey_detail(survey_id):
   sv  = Survey.query.get_or_404(survey_id)
   sla = calc_sla(sv)
   tl  = list(sv.timeline_entries)
   attachments = SurveyAttachment.query.filter_by(survey_id=survey_id) \
       .order_by(SurveyAttachment.created_at.desc()).all()
   versions = sv.versions.order_by(SurveyVersion.created_at.desc()).all()
   return render_template('survey_detail.html', settings=get_settings(),
                          survey=sv, sla=sla, timeline=tl, attachments=attachments,
                          versions=versions)

@app.route('/surveys/<int:survey_id>/version/<int:ver_id>')
@login_required
def survey_version(survey_id, ver_id):
    sv = Survey.query.get_or_404(survey_id)
    ver = SurveyVersion.query.get_or_404(ver_id)
    if ver.survey_id != survey_id: abort(404)
    return render_template('survey_version.html', settings=get_settings(),
                           survey=sv, version=ver)


@app.route("/surveys/<int:survey_id>/edit", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def survey_edit(survey_id):
    settings = get_settings()
    sv = Survey.query.get_or_404(survey_id)
    if not current_user.is_admin and sv.created_by != current_user.id:
        abort(403)
    if request.method == "POST":
        old_status = sv.status
        nco = save_file(request.files.get("company_logo_upload"), "company")
        ncl = save_file(request.files.get("client_logo_upload"), "client")
        if nco:
            sv.company_logo = nco
        if ncl:
            sv.client_logo = ncl
        sd = request.form.get("survey_date")
        if sd:
            sv.survey_date = datetime.strptime(sd, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        for attr, key in [
            ("version", "version"),
            ("project_type", "project_type"),
            ("project_name", "project_name"),
            ("project_code", "project_code"),
            ("client_name", "client_name"),
            ("client_pic", "client_pic"),
            ("client_phone", "client_phone"),
            ("client_email", "client_email"),
            ("site_name", "site_name"),
            ("site_address", "site_address"),
            ("site_city", "site_city"),
            ("site_gps", "site_gps"),
            ("surveyor_name", "surveyor_name"),
            ("surveyor_role", "surveyor_role"),
            ("survey_type", "survey_type"),
            ("status", "status"),
            ("readiness", "readiness"),
        ]:
            val = request.form.get(key)
            if val is not None:
                setattr(sv, attr, val)
        sv.form_data = json.dumps(build_form_data(request))
        sv.additional_surveyors = request.form.get(
            "additional_surveyors_json", sv.additional_surveyors or "[]"
        )
        sv.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
        # New attachments
        for file in request.files.getlist("attachment_files"):
            fname, ftype = save_attachment(file, "att")
            if fname:
                att = SurveyAttachment(
                    survey_id=sv.id,
                    filename=fname,
                    original_name=file.filename,
                    file_type="pdf" if ftype == "pdf" else "image",
                    section=request.form.get("attachment_section", "findings"),
                    caption=request.form.get("attachment_caption", ""),
                    uploaded_by=current_user.id,
                )
                db.session.add(att)
        
        if sv.survey_type == 'LPR':
            sv.status = 'In Review'
            
        new_status = sv.status
        notes = request.form.get("review_notes", "")

        if old_status != new_status:
            if new_status == "In Review" and old_status == "Draft":
                phase, action = "submit", "SSR disubmit untuk review SPV/Manager"
                save_survey_version(sv, "submit", notes)
                notify_admins(
                    f"📤 SSR {sv.ssr_number} disubmit",
                    link=url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id, _external=False),
                )
                telegram_notify_ssr(sv, "submit", notes)
            elif new_status == "Approved":
                phase, action = "approved", "SSR disetujui ✅"
                add_notification(
                    sv.created_by,
                    f"✅ SSR {sv.ssr_number} telah disetujui oleh {current_user.full_name}",
                    link=url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id, _external=False),
                )

                # Integration: Update Handover Tracker & Sync to Assets
                ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(survey_id=sv.id).first()
                if not ho and sv.project_id:
                    ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(project_id=sv.project_id).first()
                    if ho:
                        ho.survey_id = sv.id

                if ho:
                    ho.status = "In Review"  # SSR Approved, now Build progress
                    log = json.loads(ho.historical_log or "[]")
                    log.append(
                        {
                            "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
                            "actor": current_user.full_name or current_user.username,
                            "action": "SSR Approved",
                            "note": f"SSR {sv.ssr_number} has been approved. Proceeding to implementation.",
                        }
                    )
                    ho.historical_log = json.dumps(log)

                sync_survey_to_assets(sv)
                sync_survey_to_handover(sv)

                # Automation: Mark Site Survey Report (SSR) Task as Done
                if sv.project_id:
                    tasks = ProjectTask.query.filter_by(project_id=sv.project_id).all()
                    ssr_task = next((t for t in tasks if 'Site Survey Report' in t.title or 'SSR' in t.title), None)
                    if ssr_task:
                        ssr_task.is_done = True
                        db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
                            project_id=sv.project.id,
                            engineer_id=current_user.id,
                            description=f'Otomatis: Task "{ssr_task.title}" selesai karena SSR {sv.ssr_number} disetujui.',
                            progress_snap=int(len([t for t in tasks if t.is_done]) / len(tasks) * 100) if tasks else 0,
                            status='approved'
                        ))

                telegram_notify_ssr(sv, "approved", notes)
            elif new_status == "Rejected":
                phase, action = "rejected", "SSR ditolak / perlu revisi 🔄"
                add_notification(
                    sv.created_by,
                    f'🔄 SSR {sv.ssr_number} perlu revisi: {notes or "—"}',
                    link=url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id, _external=False),
                )
                telegram_notify_ssr(sv, "revision", notes)
            elif new_status == "In Review" and old_status == "Draft":
                phase, action = "submit", "SSR disubmit untuk review SPV/Manager"
                save_survey_version(sv, "submit", notes)
                notify_admins(
                    f"📤 SSR {sv.ssr_number} disubmit",
                    link=url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id, _external=False),
                )
                telegram_notify_ssr(sv, "submit", notes)
            elif new_status == "In Review" and old_status == "Rejected":
                phase, action = "resubmit", "SSR disubmit ulang setelah revisi"
                notify_admins(
                    f"📨 SSR {sv.ssr_number} disubmit ulang",
                    link=url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id, _external=False),
                )
                telegram_notify_ssr(sv, "resubmit", notes)
                save_survey_version(sv, "resubmit", notes)
            else:
                phase, action = "edit", f"Status berubah: {old_status} → {new_status}"
            add_timeline(
                sv, phase, action, status_from=old_status, status_to=new_status, notes=notes
            )
        else:
            add_timeline(
                sv,
                "edit",
                "Data SSR diperbarui",
                status_from=old_status,
                status_to=new_status,
                notes=notes,
            )
        db.session.commit()
        add_notification(
            sv.created_by,
            f"SSR {sv.ssr_number} diperbarui",
            url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id),
        )
        return redirect(url_for("survey_detail", survey_id=sv.id))

    all_engineers = (
        User.query.filter(User.is_active_acc == True).order_by(User.full_name).all()
    )
    return render_template(
        "survey_form.html",
        settings=settings,
        project_types=PROJECT_TYPES,
        status_list=STATUS_LIST,
        survey=sv,
        mode="edit",
        all_engineers=all_engineers,
    )

@app.route('/surveys/<int:survey_id>/attachments/<int:att_id>/delete', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def attachment_delete(survey_id, att_id):
   att = SurveyAttachment.query.get_or_404(att_id)
   if att.survey_id != survey_id: abort(404)
   if not current_user.is_admin and att.uploaded_by != current_user.id: abort(403)
   try:
       os.remove(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], att.filename))
   except: pass
   db.session.delete(att); db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=survey_id))

@app.route('/surveys/<int:survey_id>/delete', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def survey_delete(survey_id):
   sv = Survey.query.get_or_404(survey_id)
   if not current_user.is_admin and sv.created_by != current_user.id:
       abort(403)
   db.session.delete(sv)
   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('surveys'))

@app.route('/surveys/<int:survey_id>/action', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def survey_action(survey_id):
   sv     = Survey.query.get_or_404(survey_id)
   action = request.form.get('action','')
   notes  = request.form.get('notes','').strip()

   if action == 'submit' and sv.status == 'Draft' and (current_user.is_admin or sv.created_by == current_user.id):
       sv.status = 'In Review'
       add_timeline(sv, 'submit', 'Disubmit untuk review SPV/Manager',
                    status_from='Draft', status_to='In Review', notes=notes)
       notify_admins(f'📤 SSR {sv.ssr_number} disubmit oleh {current_user.full_name}',
                     link=url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=sv.id, _external=False))
       telegram_notify_ssr(sv, 'submit', notes)
       save_survey_version(sv, 'submit', notes)

   elif action == 'approve' and (current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer) and sv.status in ('In Review','Draft'):
       sv.status   = 'Approved'
       sv.readiness = request.form.get('readiness', sv.readiness)
       add_timeline(sv, 'approved', 'SSR disetujui ✅',
                    status_from=sv.status, status_to='Approved', notes=notes)
       add_notification(sv.created_by,
                        f'✅ SSR {sv.ssr_number} telah disetujui oleh {current_user.full_name}',
                        link=url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=sv.id, _external=False))
       telegram_notify_ssr(sv, 'approved', notes)
       sync_survey_to_assets(sv)
       sync_survey_to_handover(sv)

       # Automatically complete SSR Task in Project Checklist
       if sv.project:
           tasks = sv.project.tasks
           ssr_task = next((t for t in tasks if 'Site Survey Report' in t.title or 'SSR' in t.title), None)
           if ssr_task:
               ssr_task.is_done = True
               db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
                   project_id=sv.project.id,
                   engineer_id=current_user.id,
                   description=f'Otomatis: Task "{ssr_task.title}" selesai karena SSR {sv.ssr_number} disetujui.',
                   progress_snap=int(len([t for t in tasks if t.is_done]) / len(tasks) * 100) if tasks else 0,
                   status='approved'
               ))

   elif action == 'reject' and (current_user.is_admin or current_user.is_lead_engineer) and sv.status in ('In Review','Draft'):
       sv.status = 'Rejected'
       add_timeline(sv, 'rejected', 'SSR ditolak / perlu revisi',
                    status_from=sv.status, status_to='Rejected', notes=notes)
       add_notification(sv.created_by,
                        f'🔄 SSR {sv.ssr_number} perlu revisi: {notes or "Lihat catatan."}',
                        link=url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=sv.id, _external=False))
       telegram_notify_ssr(sv, 'revision', notes)

   elif action == 'resubmit' and sv.status == 'Rejected' and (current_user.is_admin or sv.created_by == current_user.id):
       sv.status = 'In Review'
       add_timeline(sv, 'resubmit', 'SSR disubmit ulang setelah revisi',
                    status_from='Rejected', status_to='In Review', notes=notes)
       notify_admins(f'📨 SSR {sv.ssr_number} disubmit ulang oleh {current_user.full_name}',
                     link=url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=sv.id, _external=False))
       telegram_notify_ssr(sv, 'resubmit', notes)
       save_survey_version(sv, 'resubmit', notes)

   sv.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('survey_detail', survey_id=survey_id))
