@app.route('/handover-tracker/<int:ho_id>')
@login_required
def handover_tracker_detail(ho_id):
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
   # Auto-sync technical data if missing and survey is linked
   if (not ho.server_details or ho.server_details == '[]') and ho.survey:
      sync_survey_to_handover(ho.survey)
      db.session.refresh(ho)
      

   settings = get_settings()
   import json
   history = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
   
   # Find related documents for "View" buttons
   related_docs = {
      'ssr': f"/surveys/{ho.survey_id}" if ho.survey_id else None,
      'ba_live': None,
      'ba_optimasi': None
   }
   if ho.project:
      if not related_docs['ssr'] and ho.project.surveys:
         related_docs['ssr'] = f"/surveys/{ho.project.surveys[0].id}"
      
      if ho.project.location:
         loc_name = ho.project.location.name
         ba_live = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live', lokasi_text=loc_name).order_by(AssetIssueBA.id.desc()).first()
         if ba_live: related_docs['ba_live'] = f"/berita-acara/live/{ba_live.id}"
         
         ba_opt = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='optimasi', lokasi_text=loc_name).order_by(AssetIssueBA.id.desc()).first()
         if ba_opt: related_docs['ba_optimasi'] = f"/berita-acara/optimasi/{ba_opt.id}"


   # Sort tasks: SSR first, then others
   sorted_tasks = []
   if ho.project:
      all_tasks = list(ho.project.tasks)
      # Find SSR
      ssr_tasks = [t for t in all_tasks if 'ssr' in t.title.lower() or 'survey' in t.title.lower()]
      other_tasks = [t for t in all_tasks if t not in ssr_tasks]
      sorted_tasks = ssr_tasks + other_tasks

    run_team = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['run', 'lead_engineer']), User.is_active_acc==True).all()
    return render_template('handover_tracker_detail.html', settings=settings, ho=ho, history=history, related_docs=related_docs, sorted_tasks=sorted_tasks, run_team=run_team)

@app.route('/api/handover/<int:ho_id>/update', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_handover_update(ho_id):
    ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
    data = request.json
    
    if 'checklist_progress' in data:
        ho.checklist_progress = data['checklist_progress']
    if 'nuc_setup_details' in data:
        ho.nuc_setup_details = data['nuc_setup_details']
    if 'deployment_details' in data:
        ho.deployment_details = data['deployment_details']
    if 'server_details' in data:
        ho.server_details = data['server_details']
    if 'camera_details' in data:
        ho.camera_details = data['camera_details']
        
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"ok": True})


@app.route('/api/handover/<int:ho_id>/task-review', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_handover_task_review(ho_id):
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
   data = request.json
   task_id = str(data.get('task_id'))
   status = data.get('status') # Approved / Revised
   note = data.get('note', '')
   
   reviews = json.loads(ho.checklist_progress or '{}')
   reviews[task_id] = {'status': status, 'note': note, 'user': current_user.full_name, 'at': get_wib().isoformat()}
   ho.checklist_progress = json.dumps(reviews)
   
   # Log to history
   history = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
   history.append({
       'action': f"Task Review: {status}",
       'actor': current_user.full_name,
       'timestamp': get_wib().isoformat(),
       'note': f"Task ID {task_id}: {note}"
   })
   ho.historical_log = json.dumps(history)
   
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route('/api/handover/<int:ho_id>/assign', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_handover_assign(ho_id):
    ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
    data = request.json
    uid = data.get('user_id')
    if not uid: return jsonify({'ok': False, 'msg': 'User ID required'})
    
    user = User.query.get(uid)
    if not user: return jsonify({'ok': False, 'msg': 'User not found'})
    
    ho.run_assigned_to_id = uid
    
    # Log to history
    history = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
    history.append({
        'action': "Run Validator Assigned",
        'actor': current_user.full_name,
        'timestamp': get_wib().isoformat(),
        'note': f"Assigned to {user.full_name}"
    })
    ho.historical_log = json.dumps(history)
    
    # Notification
    add_notification(uid, f"Anda ditugaskan sebagai validator Handover #{ho.id} ({ho.location.name})", f"/handover-tracker/{ho.id}")
    
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route('/handover-tracker/<int:ho_id>/approve', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def handover_approve(ho_id):
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
   role = request.form.get('role')
   decision = request.form.get('decision')
   note = request.form.get('note')
   
   history = json.loads(ho.historical_log or '[]')
   action_str = f"{role.upper()} {decision.upper()}"
   
   if decision == 'approve':
      if role == 'build':
         ho.build_approved_by_id = current_user.id
         ho.build_approved_at = get_wib()
         ho.status = 'Review RUN'
      elif role == 'run':
         ho.run_approved_by_id = current_user.id
         ho.run_approved_at = get_wib()
         ho.status = 'Review Manager'
      elif role == 'manager':
         ho.manager_approved_by_id = current_user.id
         ho.manager_approved_at = get_wib()
         ho.status = 'Review Director'
      elif role == 'director':
         ho.director_approved_by_id = current_user.id
         ho.director_approved_at = get_wib()
         ho.status = 'Completed'
         # Finalize Project
         if ho.project:
            ho.project.status = 'completed'
            for t in ho.project.tasks:
               t.is_done = True
            
            db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
               project_id=ho.project_id,
               engineer_id=current_user.id,
               personnel_log="BAST Signed Off",
               description="Project formally closed via Handover Tracker BAST sign-off.",
               status='approved',
               progress_snap=100
            ))
            # Sync site status
            if ho.location:
               ho.location.status = 'active'
   else: # Revision
      ho.status = 'Revised'
      if role == 'run': ho.build_approved_at = None
      elif role == 'manager': ho.run_approved_at = None
      elif role == 'director': ho.manager_approved_at = None

   history.append({
      'action': action_str,
      'actor': current_user.full_name,
      'timestamp': get_wib().isoformat(),
      'note': note
   })
   ho.historical_log = json.dumps(history)
   db.session.commit()
   return redirect(url_for('handover_tracker_detail', ho_id=ho.id))
@app.route('/api/handover/<int:ho_id>/checklist', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_update_handover_checklist(ho_id):
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)

      return jsonify({'error': 'Unauthorized'}), 403
      
   data = request.json
   import json
   chk = json.loads(ho.checklist_progress or '{}')
   chk.update(data)
   ho.checklist_progress = json.dumps(chk)
   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({'success': True, 'checklist': chk})

@app.route('/api/handover', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_create_handover():
   import json
   data = request.form
   loc_id = data.get('location_id')
   
   # Create Handover Record
   new_ho = HandoverTracker(
       location_id=loc_id,
       project_id=data.get('project_id') or None,
       server_details=data.get('server_details', '[]'),
       camera_details=data.get('camera_details', '[]'),
       status=data.get('status', 'Draft'),
       created_by_id=current_user.id
   )
   db.session.add(new_ho)
   
   # INTEGRATION: Sync to AssetMonitoring (AssetDevice)
   try:
       servers = json.loads(data.get('server_details', '[]'))
       cameras = json.loads(data.get('camera_details', '[]'))
       
       # Sync Servers
       for s in servers:
           sop = s.get('sop', {})
           sop_list = [k for k,v in sop.items() if v]
           sop_note = f"SOP Check: {', '.join(sop_list)}" if sop_list else "No SOP checklist items marked."
           
           dev = AssetDevice(
               location_id=loc_id,
               device_name=f"Server {s.get('type')}",
               device_category='server',
               device_type='NUC / Server',
               ip_address=s.get('ip'),
               username=s.get('username'),
               password=s.get('password'),
               notes=f"{s.get('spec')} | {sop_note}"
           )
           db.session.add(dev)
           
       # Sync Cameras
       for c in cameras:
           asm = c.get('assessment', {})
           dev = AssetDevice(
               location_id=loc_id,
               device_name=c.get('gate_name') or 'LPR Camera',
               device_category='camera',
               device_type='LPR Camera',
               ip_address=c.get('ip'),
               username=c.get('username'),
               password=c.get('password'),
               port=c.get('port'),
               gate_name=c.get('gate_name'),
               brand_model=c.get('brand'),
               assessment=asm.get('note') if isinstance(asm, dict) else str(asm)
           )
           db.session.add(dev)
           
       # Update Location Counts
       loc = AssetLocation.query.get(loc_id)
       if loc:
           loc.nuc_count = (loc.nuc_count or 0) + len(servers)
           loc.camera_count = (loc.camera_count or 0) + len(cameras)
           loc.device_count = (loc.device_count or 0) + len(servers) + len(cameras)

   except Exception as e:
       print(f"Sync Error: {e}")

   db.session.commit()
   return jsonify({"success": True, "id": new_ho.id})

@app.route('/handover-tracker/<int:ho_id>/delete', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def handover_tracker_delete(ho_id):
   if not current_user.is_admin:
      flash("Unauthorized", "danger")
      return redirect(url_for('handover_tracker_detail', ho_id=ho_id))
      
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.get_or_404(ho_id)
   if ho.project:
      # Reset project status to allow re-submission
      ho.project.status = 'in_progress'
      # Log deletion in project activities
      db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
         project_id=ho.project_id,
         engineer_id=current_user.id,
         personnel_log="Handover Reset",
         description=f"Handover Tracker #{ho_id} was deleted/reset by Admin.",
         status='warning'
      ))
      
   db.session.delete(ho)
   db.session.commit()
   flash("Handover Tracker deleted and project status reset.", "success")
   return redirect(url_for('handover_tracker'))

@app.route('/api/projects/<int:pid>/handover/assign', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_project_handover_assign(pid):
   proj = Project.query.get_or_404(pid)
   ho = HandoverTracker.query.filter_by(project_id=pid).first_or_404()
   
   data = request.json
   run_uid = data.get('run_assigned_to_id')
   
   if run_uid:
      run_uid = int(run_uid)
      ho.run_assigned_to_id = run_uid
      ho.status = 'Handover'
      
      # 1. Mark Go-Live task as done
      go_live_task = ProjectTask.query.filter(
          ProjectTask.project_id == pid,
          (ProjectTask.title.ilike('%go-live%') | ProjectTask.title.ilike('%go live%') | ProjectTask.title.ilike('%handover%'))
      ).first()
      if go_live_task:
          go_live_task.is_done = True
      
      # 2. Assign user to project if not already assigned
      existing_assign = ProjectAssignment.query.filter_by(project_id=pid, user_id=run_uid).first()
      if not existing_assign:
          db.session.add(ProjectAssignment(project_id=pid, user_id=run_uid, role='run_team'))
      
      # 3. Create Notification
      run_user = User.query.get(run_uid)
      msg = f"Project Handover: {proj.name} telah diserahterimakan kepada Anda."
      db.session.add(Notification(
          user_id=run_uid,
          message=msg,
          link=f"/projects/{pid}"
      ))
      
      # 4. Log Activity
      db.session.add(ProjectActivity(
         project_id=pid,
         engineer_id=current_user.id,
         personnel_log=f"Handover Assigned: {run_user.full_name if run_user else 'Unknown'}",
         description=f"Project handed over from Build Team to Run Team. Assigned to {run_user.full_name if run_user else 'N/A'}.",
         status='approved',
         progress_snap=100
      ))
      
      db.session.commit()
      return jsonify({'ok': True})
   
   return jsonify({'ok': False, 'error': 'No Run Engineer selected'})

@app.route('/api/asset-movement', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def api_create_movement():
   data = request.form
   year = datetime.now().year
   count = AssetMovement.query.count() + 1
   m_type = data.get('movement_type', 'RMA')
   prefix = 'RMA' if m_type == 'RMA' else 'TRX'
   rma_num = f"{prefix}-{year}-{count:04d}"
   
   ret_date = None
   if data.get('return_date'):
       try:
           ret_date = datetime.fromisoformat(data.get('return_date'))
       except: pass

   new_m = AssetMovement(
       rma_number=rma_num,
       movement_type=m_type,
       asset_name=data.get('asset_name'),
       serial_number=data.get('serial_number'),
       from_location=data.get('from_location'),
       to_location=data.get('to_location'),
       reason=data.get('reason'),
       priority=data.get('priority', 'Medium'),
       description=data.get('description'),
       return_date=ret_date,
       created_by_id=current_user.id
   )
   db.session.add(new_m)
   db.session.commit()
   flash(f"Asset {m_type} {rma_num} created successfully.")
   return redirect(url_for('asset_movement'))

@app.route('/api/scheduler/tasks', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def api_scheduler_tasks():
    settings = get_settings()
    if request.method == 'GET':
        start = request.args.get('start')
        end = request.args.get('end')
        query = PreventiveScheduler.query
        if start:
            query = query.filter(PreventiveScheduler.start_date >= datetime.fromisoformat(start))
        if end:
            query = query.filter(PreventiveScheduler.start_date <= datetime.fromisoformat(end))
        tasks = query.all()
        return jsonify([{
            'id': t.id,
            'title': t.title,
            'start': t.start_date.isoformat(),
            'end': t.end_date.isoformat() if t.end_date else None,
            'type': t.task_type,
            'system_type': t.system_type,
            'description': t.description,
            'location_id': t.location_id,
            'location_name': t.location.name if t.location else 'N/A',
            'assigned_to': json.loads(t.assigned_to or '[]') if (t.assigned_to or '').startswith('[') else ([int(t.assigned_to)] if (t.assigned_to or '').isdigit() else []),
            'assignee_name': t.assignee_names_display,
            'status': t.status,
            'color': t.color
        } for t in tasks])

        # Conflict Detection
        assigned_ids = data.get('assigned_to', [])
        conflicts = []
        if assigned_ids:
            overlap_tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter(
                PreventiveScheduler.start_date < (datetime.fromisoformat(data['end']) if data.get('end') else datetime.fromisoformat(data['start']) + timedelta(hours=8)),
                PreventiveScheduler.end_date > datetime.fromisoformat(data['start']),
                PreventiveScheduler.location_id != data.get('location_id')
            ).all()
            for t in overlap_tasks:
                t_assigned = json.loads(t.assigned_to or '[]')
                common = set(assigned_ids) & set(t_assigned)
                if common:
                    conflicts.append(f"Konflik dengan: {t.title} di {t.location.name if t.location else 'N/A'}")

        new_task = PreventiveScheduler(
            title=data['title'],
            task_type=data.get('task_type', 'PM'),
            system_type=data.get('system_type'),
            description=data.get('description'),
            start_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['start']),
            end_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['end']) if data.get('end') else datetime.fromisoformat(data['start']) + timedelta(hours=8),
            location_id=data.get('location_id'),
            assigned_to=json.dumps(assigned_ids),
            created_by=current_user.id,
            status='Scheduled',
            color=data.get('color', '#3b82f6')
        )
        db.session.add(new_task)
        for uid in assigned_ids:
            add_notification(int(uid), f"Jadwal baru: {new_task.title}", url_for('scheduler_view'))
        db.session.commit()
        telegram_notify_scheduler(new_task, 'created')
        return jsonify({'ok': True, 'id': new_task.id, 'conflicts': conflicts})

@app.route('/api/scheduler/tasks/<int:tid>', methods=['PUT', 'DELETE'])
@login_required
def api_scheduler_task_detail(tid):
    task = PreventiveScheduler.query.get_or_404(tid)
    if request.method == 'DELETE':
        db.session.delete(task)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'ok': True})
    
    if request.method == 'PUT':
        data = request.json
        # Conflict Detection for Update
        assigned_ids = data.get('assigned_to', json.loads(task.assigned_to or '[]'))
        new_start = datetime.fromisoformat(data['start']) if data.get('start') else task.start_date
        new_end = datetime.fromisoformat(data['end']) if data.get('end') else task.end_date
        new_loc = data.get('location_id', task.location_id)
        
        conflicts = []
        if assigned_ids:
            overlap_tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter(
                PreventiveScheduler.id != tid,
                PreventiveScheduler.start_date < (new_end if new_end else new_start + timedelta(hours=8)),
                PreventiveScheduler.end_date > new_start,
                PreventiveScheduler.location_id != new_loc
            ).all()
            for t in overlap_tasks:
                t_assigned = json.loads(t.assigned_to or '[]')
                common = set(assigned_ids) & set(t_assigned)
                if common:
                    conflicts.append(f"Konflik dengan: {t.title} di {t.location.name if t.location else 'N/A'}")

        task.title = data.get('title', task.title)
        task.task_type = data.get('task_type', task.task_type)
        task.system_type = data.get('system_type', task.system_type)
        task.description = data.get('description', task.description)
        if data.get('start'):
            task.start_date = datetime.fromisoformat(data['start'])
        if data.get('end'):
            task.end_date = datetime.fromisoformat(data['end'])
        elif data.get('start'): # If start changes but no end provided, maintain duration or set default
            task.end_date = task.start_date + timedelta(hours=8)
            
        task.location_id = data.get('location_id', task.location_id)
        task.assigned_to = json.dumps(assigned_ids)
        task.status = data.get('status', task.status)
        task.color = data.get('color', task.color)
        
        for uid in assigned_ids:
            add_notification(int(uid), f"Jadwal diperbarui: {task.title}", url_for('scheduler_view'))
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'ok': True, 'conflicts': conflicts})

@app.route('/api/scheduler/insights')
@login_required
def api_scheduler_insights():
    month = int(request.args.get('month', datetime.now().month))
    year = int(request.args.get('year', datetime.now().year))
    
    start_of_month = datetime(year, month, 1)
    if month == 12:
        end_of_month = datetime(year + 1, 1, 1)
    else:
        end_of_month = datetime(year, month + 1, 1)
        
    tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter(
        PreventiveScheduler.start_date >= start_of_month,
        PreventiveScheduler.start_date < end_of_month
    ).all()
    
    total_tasks = len(tasks)
    completed_tasks = len([t for t in tasks if t.status == 'Completed'])
    sla = (completed_tasks / total_tasks * 100) if total_tasks > 0 else 100
    
    # Utilization: percentage of engineers with at least one task today
    today = datetime.now().date()
    today_tasks = PreventiveScheduler.query.filter(
        PreventiveScheduler.start_date >= datetime.combine(today, datetime.min.time()),
        PreventiveScheduler.start_date <= datetime.combine(today, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()
    
    engineers = User.query.filter(User.role.in_(['admin', 'build', 'run', 'lead_engineer'])).all()
    total_eng = len(engineers)
    
    busy_uids = set()
    for t in today_tasks:
        try:
            uids = json.loads(t.assigned_to or '[]')
            for u in uids: busy_uids.add(u)
        except: pass
        
    utilization = (len(busy_uids) / total_eng * 100) if total_eng > 0 else 0
    
    return jsonify({
        'total_tasks': total_tasks,
        'completed_tasks': completed_tasks,
        'sla': round(sla, 1),
        'utilization': round(utilization, 1),
        'busy_count': len(busy_uids),
        'available_count': total_eng - len(busy_uids)
    })

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/revise", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
@role_required("admin", "lead_engineer")
def ba_troubleshoot_revise(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    reason = request.form.get("reason", "")
    ba.status = "revised"
    ba.revision_reason = reason
    db.session.commit()
    telegram_notify_ba(ba, "revision", reason=reason)
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/edit", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_edit(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    # Check permission
    if ba.engineer_id != current_user.id and current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
        abort(403)
    # Only allow edit if pending or revised
    if ba.status == 'approved' and current_user.role != 'admin':
        flash("Hanya BA status pending atau revised yang dapat diedit")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_troubleshoot_detail', baid=baid))

    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
    import datetime
    now = datetime.datetime.now()
    
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}

    if request.method == "POST":
        ba.perihal = request.form.get("perihal")
        ba.lokasi_text = request.form.get("lokasi_text")
        ba.device_gate = request.form.get("device_gate")
        ba.kronologi = request.form.get("kronologi")
        ba.tindakan_dilakukan = request.form.get("action_taken")
        ba.analisa_rekomendasi = request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi")
        ba.intro_text = request.form.get("intro_text")
        ba.status = "pending" # Reset to pending after edit
        
        # Process attachments (simplified, keeping existing if no new)
        attachments = content_data.get('attachments', [])
        for i in range(1, 7):
            f = request.files.get(f"att_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"att_title_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                # Save new file
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
                # Update or add
                if len(attachments) >= i:
                    attachments[i-1] = {"filename": fname, "title": t}
                else:
                    attachments.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})
            elif i <= len(attachments) and t:
                # Update title only
                attachments[i-1]['title'] = t
        
        # Process devices
        devices = []
        d_namas = request.form.getlist("dev_nama[]")
        d_ips = request.form.getlist("dev_ip[]")
        d_gates = request.form.getlist("dev_gate[]")
        d_statuses = request.form.getlist("dev_status[]")
        for dn, dip, dg, ds in zip(d_namas, d_ips, d_gates, d_statuses):
            if dn: devices.append({"nama": dn, "ip": dip, "gate": dg, "status": ds})

        # Process signers
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        comps = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        signers = []
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, comps):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})

        ba.content_json = json.dumps({"attachments": attachments, "signers": signers, "devices": devices})
        db.session.commit()
        return redirect(url_for('ba_troubleshoot_detail', baid=ba.id))

    return render_template("ba_troubleshoot_form.html", settings=settings, locations=locations, ba=ba, content=content_data, now=now, 
                           users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all(),
                           device_types=DropdownOption.get('device_types'))

def telegram_notify_ba(ba, event_type="created", reason=None):
   st = get_settings()
   if not st.telegram_enabled or not st.tg_ba_enabled: return
   
   emoji = "📝"
   prefix = "TROUBLESHOOT"
   if ba.ba_type == "live": prefix = "LIVE"
   elif ba.ba_type == "optimasi": prefix = "OPTIMASI"
   
   title = f"NEW BERITA ACARA ({prefix})"
   if event_type == "approved":
       emoji = "✅"
       title = f"BA {prefix} APPROVED"
   elif event_type == "revision":
       emoji = "⚠️"
       title = f"BA {prefix} REVISION NEEDED"



   msg = f"<b>{emoji} {title}</b>\n"
   msg += f"----------------------------\n"
   msg += f"<b>No BA:</b> {ba.ba_number}\n"
   msg += f"<b>Perihal:</b> {html.escape(ba.perihal or '-')}\n"
   msg += f"<b>Lokasi:</b> {html.escape(ba.lokasi_text or '-')}\n"
   msg += f"<b>Engineer:</b> {html.escape(ba.engineer.full_name if ba.engineer else '-')}\n"
   
   if event_type == "approved":
      msg += f"<b>Status:</b> APPROVED\n"
      msg += f"<b>Approver:</b> {html.escape(ba.approver.full_name if ba.approver else '-')}\n"
   elif event_type == "revision":
      msg += f"<b>Status:</b> REVISION NEEDED\n"
      msg += f"<b>Reason:</b> {html.escape(reason or '-')}\n"
      
   msg += f"----------------------------\n"
   msg += f"Sistem: <b>ALFABETA ODMS</b>"
   send_telegram(msg)

   # Bell Notifications
   detail_route = 'ba_troubleshoot_detail'
   if ba.ba_type == 'live': detail_route = 'ba_live_detail'
   elif ba.ba_type == 'optimasi': detail_route = 'ba_optimasi_detail'

   if event_type == "created":
       notify_admins(f"📝 BA Baru: {ba.ba_number} ({ba.perihal})", url_for(detail_route, baid=ba.id))
   elif event_type == "approved":
       add_notification(ba.engineer_id, f"✅ BA Anda telah disetujui: {ba.ba_number}", url_for(detail_route, baid=ba.id))
   elif event_type == "revision":
       add_notification(ba.engineer_id, f"⚠️ BA Anda perlu revisi: {ba.ba_number}. Alasan: {reason}", url_for(detail_route, baid=ba.id))


def generate_ba_pdf(ba_id):
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.units import cm
    from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
    from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Table, TableStyle, Paragraph, Spacer, Image
    from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER, TA_LEFT
    import os, json, uuid, re
    from datetime import datetime
    import html as html_module

    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get(ba_id)
    if not ba: return None

    def clean_rl(text):
        if not text: return ""
        # Convert some tags to RL supported ones
        text = text.replace('</p>', '<br/>').replace('</div>', '<br/>').replace('</li>', '<br/>')
        # Strip all except b, i, u, font, br
        text = re.sub(r'<(?!/?(b|i|u|font|br)[ >]).*?>', '', text)
        return text
    
    if ba.ba_type == 'optimasi':
        safe_lokasi = "".join([c for c in (ba.lokasi_text or 'No Location') if c.isalnum() or c in (' ', '-', '_')]).strip()
        filename = f"BA Optimasi LPR - {safe_lokasi}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.pdf"
    else:
        filename = f"BA_{ba.ba_number.replace('/','_')}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.pdf"
    
    path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename)
    
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(path, pagesize=A4, rightMargin=1.5*cm, leftMargin=1.5*cm, topMargin=1.5*cm, bottomMargin=1.5*cm)
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    
    # Custom Styles
    style_body = ParagraphStyle('BodyText', parent=styles['Normal'], fontSize=10, leading=14)
    style_header_label = ParagraphStyle('HeaderLabel', fontSize=8, color=colors.grey)
    style_header_value = ParagraphStyle('HeaderValue', fontSize=10, fontName='Helvetica-Bold')
    style_section_title = ParagraphStyle('SectionTitle', parent=styles['Normal'], fontSize=11, fontName='Helvetica-Bold', color=colors.white, backColor=colors.HexColor("#0056b3"), borderPadding=5, spaceBefore=10, spaceAfter=8)
    style_label = ParagraphStyle('Label', parent=styles['Normal'], fontSize=9, fontName='Helvetica-Bold', spaceAfter=2)
    
    elements = []
    
    # 1. HEADER
    logo_path = os.path.join(app.root_path, "static", "img", "logo_alfabeta.png")
    if not os.path.exists(logo_path):
        logo_path = os.path.join(app.root_path, "static", "logo_alfabeta.png")
    
    logo_img = None
    if os.path.exists(logo_path):
        try:
            logo_img = Image(logo_path, width=4*cm, preserveAspectRatio=True)
            logo_img.hAlign = 'LEFT'
        except: pass
    
    header_info = [[
        logo_img if logo_img else "",
        Paragraph(f"<font color='#0056b3' size=14><b>PT Alfabeta Solusi Nusantara</b></font><br/><font size=8 color='grey'>OPERATIONS DESK MANAGEMENT SYSTEM</font>", style_body),
        Paragraph(f"<font color='#0056b3' size=11><b>{html_module.escape(ba.ba_number)}</b></font><br/><font size=7 color='grey'>Generated: {datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M')}</font>", style_body)
    ]]
    header_table = Table(header_info, colWidths=[4.5*cm, 10*cm, 3.5*cm])
    header_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'MIDDLE'),
        ('ALIGN', (2,0), (2,0), 'RIGHT'),
    ]))
    elements.append(header_table)
    elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))
    
    # 2. TITLE
    prefix = "OPTIMASI"
    if ba.ba_type == "live": prefix = "LIVE"
    elif ba.ba_type == "troubleshoot": prefix = "TROUBLESHOOTING"
    
    title_p = Paragraph(f"<b>BERITA ACARA {prefix}: {html_module.escape(ba.perihal or '-')}</b>", style_section_title)
    elements.append(title_p)
    elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
    
    # 3. INFO GRID
    info_data = [
        [Paragraph("<b>SITE / LOKASI</b>", style_header_label), Paragraph("<b>ENGINEER</b>", style_header_label), Paragraph("<b>TANGGAL</b>", style_header_label)],
        [Paragraph(html_module.escape(ba.lokasi_text or "-"), style_header_value), Paragraph(html_module.escape(ba.engineer.full_name if ba.engineer else "-"), style_header_value), Paragraph(ba.created_at.strftime('%d %B %Y') if ba.created_at else "-", style_header_value)]
    ]
    info_table = Table(info_data, colWidths=[6*cm, 6*cm, 6*cm])
    info_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('BOX', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.grey),
        ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.grey),
        ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,0), colors.whitesmoke),
        ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'TOP'),
        ('PADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
    ]))
    elements.append(info_table)
    elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.8*cm))

    try:
        content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except:
        content_data = {}

    # 4. CONTENT SECTIONS
    if ba.intro_text:
        elements.append(Paragraph(clean_rl(ba.intro_text), style_body))
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))

    sections = []
    if ba.ba_type == "optimasi":
        sections = [
            ("A. Kronologi / Latar Belakang", ba.kronologi),
            ("B. Analisa & Rekomendasi", ba.analisa_rekomendasi)
        ]
    else:
        sections = [
            ("I. Kronologi Kejadian", ba.kronologi),
            ("II. Tindakan yang Dilakukan", ba.tindakan_dilakukan),
            ("III. Analisa & Rekomendasi", ba.analisa_rekomendasi)
        ]

    for title, content in sections:
        if content:
            elements.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{title}</b>", style_label))
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.1*cm))
            elements.append(Paragraph(clean_rl(content), style_body))
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))

    # 5. SPECIAL CONTENT (TABLES)
    if ba.ba_type == "optimasi":
        pengajuan = content_data.get('pengajuan', [])
        if pengajuan:
            elements.append(Paragraph("<b>C. Tabel Pengajuan Optimasi</b>", style_label))
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
            p_data = [["No", "Gate / Lokasi", "Kamera", "Rekomendasi"]]
            for idx, p in enumerate(pengajuan, 1):
                p_data.append([str(idx), p.get('gate','-'), p.get('kamera','-'), p.get('rekomendasi','-')])
            
            p_table = Table(p_data, colWidths=[1*cm, 5*cm, 5*cm, 7*cm])
            p_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
                ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.grey),
                ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,0), colors.whitesmoke),
                ('FONTNAME', (0,0), (-1,0), 'Helvetica-Bold'),
                ('FONTSIZE', (0,0), (-1,-1), 9),
            ]))
            elements.append(p_table)
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))
    elif ba.ba_type == "live":
        devices = content_data.get('devices', [])
        if devices:
            elements.append(Paragraph("<b>Implementasi / Instalasi Perangkat</b>", style_label))
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
            d_data = [["No", "Nama Perangkat", "IP Address", "Gate", "Status"]]
            for idx, d in enumerate(devices, 1):
                d_data.append([str(idx), d.get('nama','-'), d.get('ip','-'), d.get('gate','-'), d.get('status','-')])
            
            d_table = Table(d_data, colWidths=[1*cm, 5*cm, 4*cm, 4*cm, 4*cm])
            d_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
                ('GRID', (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.5, colors.grey),
                ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,0), colors.whitesmoke),
                ('FONTSIZE', (0,0), (-1,-1), 9),
            ]))
            elements.append(d_table)
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))

    # 6. ATTACHMENTS
    attachments = []
    if ba.ba_type == "optimasi":
        attachments = content_data.get('grid_attachments', [])
    else:
        attachments = content_data.get('attachments', [])

    if attachments:
        elements.append(Paragraph("<b>Dokumentasi / Lampiran Foto</b>", style_label))
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
        
        img_cells = []
        for att in attachments:
            if not att.get('filename'): continue
            img_p = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], att['filename'])
            if os.path.exists(img_p):
                try:
                    img = Image(img_p, width=8*cm, height=5*cm)
                    img_cells.append([img, Paragraph(att.get('title', '') or 'Lampiran', style_body)])
                except: pass
        
        if img_cells:
            grid_data = []
            for i in range(0, len(img_cells), 2):
                row = img_cells[i:i+2]
                if len(row) == 1:
                    grid_data.append([row[0][0], ""])
                    grid_data.append([row[0][1], ""])
                else:
                    grid_data.append([row[0][0], row[1][0]])
                    grid_data.append([row[0][1], row[1][1]])
            
            att_table = Table(grid_data, colWidths=[9*cm, 9*cm])
            att_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
                ('ALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'CENTER'),
                ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'TOP'),
                ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 10),
            ]))
            elements.append(att_table)
            elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))

    # 7. SIGNATURES
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    if signers:
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 1*cm))
        elements.append(Paragraph("<b>Tanda Tangan Persetujuan:</b>", style_label))
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 0.5*cm))
        
        sign_rows = []
        for i in range(0, len(signers), 3):
            chunk = signers[i:i+3]
            row_roles = [Paragraph(f"<b>{s['role']}</b>", style_body) for s in chunk]
            row_signs = []
            for s in chunk:
                if s.get('signature'):
                    try:
                        import base64, io
                        img_data = s['signature'].split(',')[1]
                        sig_img = Image(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(img_data)), width=4*cm, height=2*cm)
                        row_signs.append(sig_img)
                    except:
                        row_signs.append(Spacer(1, 2*cm))
                else:
                    row_signs.append(Spacer(1, 2*cm))
            
            row_names = [Paragraph(f"<u>{s['name']}</u><br/>{s['company']}", style_body) for s in chunk]
            
            while len(row_roles) < 3:
                row_roles.append("")
                row_signs.append("")
                row_names.append("")
            
            sign_rows.append(row_roles)
            sign_rows.append(row_signs)
            sign_rows.append(row_names)
            sign_rows.append([Spacer(1, 0.8*cm)]*3)

        sig_table = Table(sign_rows, colWidths=[6*cm, 6*cm, 6*cm])
        sig_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('ALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'CENTER'),
            ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'MIDDLE'),
        ]))
        elements.append(sig_table)

    try:
        doc.build(elements)
    except Exception as e:
        # Log error for debugging
        with open(os.path.join(app.root_path, "pdf_error.log"), "a") as f:
            f.write(f"{datetime.now()}: Error building PDF {filename} for BA {ba.id}: {str(e)}\n")
        return None
        
    return filename

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/new", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_new():
    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
    now = get_wib()
    year_month = now.strftime("%Y%m")
    prefix = f"ASN-BA-TS-{year_month}"
    last_ba = AssetIssueBA.query.filter(AssetIssueBA.ba_number.like(f"{prefix}-%")).order_by(AssetIssueBA.id.desc()).first()
    new_num = 1
    if last_ba:
        try: new_num = int(last_ba.ba_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
        except: pass
    generated_ba_number = f"{prefix}-{new_num:03d}"

    if request.method == "POST":
        attachments = []
        for i in range(1, 7):
            f = request.files.get(f"att_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"att_title_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
                attachments.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})
        
        # Process devices
        devices = []
        d_namas = request.form.getlist("dev_nama[]")
        d_ips = request.form.getlist("dev_ip[]")
        d_gates = request.form.getlist("dev_gate[]")
        d_statuses = request.form.getlist("dev_status[]")
        for dn, dip, dg, ds in zip(d_namas, d_ips, d_gates, d_statuses):
            if dn: devices.append({"nama": dn, "ip": dip, "gate": dg, "status": ds})

        # Process signers
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        comps = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        signers = []
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, comps):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})

        ba = AssetIssueBA(
            engineer_id=current_user.id, ba_number=generated_ba_number,
            perihal=request.form.get("perihal"), lokasi_text=request.form.get("lokasi_text"),
            device_gate=request.form.get("device_gate"), kronologi=request.form.get("kronologi"),
            tindakan_dilakukan=request.form.get("action_taken"), analisa_rekomendasi=request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi"),
            intro_text=request.form.get("intro_text"),
            content_json=json.dumps({"attachments": attachments, "signers": signers, "devices": devices})
        )
        db.session.add(ba); db.session.commit()
        notify_mentions(ba.kronologi, f"Anda dimention dalam kronologi BA {ba.ba_number}", url_for("ba_troubleshoot_detail", baid=ba.id))
        notify_mentions(ba.tindakan_dilakukan, f"Anda dimention dalam tindakan BA {ba.ba_number}", url_for("ba_troubleshoot_detail", baid=ba.id))
        notify_mentions(ba.analisa_rekomendasi, f"Anda dimention dalam analisa BA {ba.ba_number}", url_for("ba_troubleshoot_detail", baid=ba.id))
        telegram_notify_ba(ba, "created")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_troubleshoot_list'))
    return render_template("ba_troubleshoot_form.html", settings=settings, locations=locations, now=now, 
                           users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all(),
                           device_types=DropdownOption.get('device_types'))

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>")
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_detail(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    settings = get_settings()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    return render_template("ba_troubleshoot_detail.html", ba=ba, content=content_data, settings=settings)

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/approve", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_approve(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'troubleshoot': abort(404)
    
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    unsigned = [s['name'] for s in signers if not s.get('signature') and User.query.filter_by(full_name=s['name']).first()]
    if unsigned:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "msg": f"Approval gagal. Menunggu tanda tangan dari pihak internal: {', '.join(unsigned)}"}), 400

    ba.status = "approved"; ba.approver_id = current_user.id; ba.approved_at = get_wib()
    ba.pdf_filename = generate_ba_pdf(ba.id)
    db.session.commit()
    telegram_notify_ba(ba, "approved")
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/sign", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_sign(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'troubleshoot': abort(404)
    index = int(request.form.get("index", -1))
    signature = request.form.get("signature", "")
    if not signature or index == -1:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "msg": "Invalid data"}), 400
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    if index < len(signers):
        signers[index]['signature'] = signature
        signers[index]['signed_at'] = get_wib().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        if current_user.job_position:
            signers[index]['job_position'] = current_user.job_position
    content_data['signers'] = signers
    ba.content_json = json.dumps(content_data)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/delete", methods=["DELETE", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_delete(baid):
    if current_user.role != 'admin': return jsonify({"ok": False}), 403
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    db.session.delete(ba); db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/<int:baid>/print")
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_print(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type == 'optimasi':
        return redirect(url_for('ba_optimasi_print', baid=ba.id))
    if ba.ba_type == 'live':
        return redirect(url_for('ba_live_print', baid=ba.id))
    
    settings = get_settings()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    return render_template("ba_troubleshoot_pdf.html", ba=ba, content=content_data, settings=settings)

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot")
@login_required
def ba_troubleshoot_list():
    settings = get_settings()
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    status_f = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
    date_from = request.args.get('date_from', '').strip()
    date_to = request.args.get('date_to', '').strip()

    query = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='troubleshoot')
    
    if q:
        query = query.filter(
            (AssetIssueBA.ba_number.ilike(f"%{q}%")) |
            (AssetIssueBA.perihal.ilike(f"%{q}%")) |
            (AssetIssueBA.lokasi_text.ilike(f"%{q}%"))
        )
    if status_f:
        query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.status == status_f)
    if date_from:
        try:
            df = datetime.strptime(date_from, '%Y-%m-%d')
            query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.created_at >= df)
        except: pass
    if date_to:
        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(date_to, '%Y-%m-%d') + timedelta(days=1)
            query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.created_at < dt)
        except: pass

    bas = query.order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).all()
    
    # Summaries
    all_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='troubleshoot').count()
    pending_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='troubleshoot', status='pending').count()
    approved_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='troubleshoot', status='approved').count()
    
    return render_template("ba_troubleshoot_list.html", 
                           bas=bas, settings=settings,
                           all_count=all_count, pending_count=pending_count, approved_count=approved_count,
                           q=q, status_f=status_f, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to)

@app.route("/berita-acara/troubleshoot/export")
@login_required
def export_ba_troubleshoot():
    # Simple CSV export for now
    import csv
    import io
    from flask import Response
    
    bas = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='troubleshoot').order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).all()
    
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output)
    writer.writerow(['Date', 'BA Number', 'Perihal', 'Lokasi', 'Engineer', 'Status'])
    
    for ba in bas:
        writer.writerow([
            ba.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if ba.created_at else '',
            ba.ba_number or '',
            ba.perihal or '',
            ba.lokasi_text or '',
            ba.engineer.full_name if ba.engineer else 'System',
            ba.status or 'pending'
        ])
    
    return Response(
        output.getvalue(),
        mimetype="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-disposition": "attachment; filename=ba_troubleshoot_export.csv"}
    )

@app.route("/berita-acara/live")
@login_required
def ba_live_list():
    settings = get_settings()
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    status_f = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
    date_from = request.args.get('date_from', '').strip()
    date_to = request.args.get('date_to', '').strip()

    query = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live')
    
    if q:
        query = query.filter(
            (AssetIssueBA.ba_number.ilike(f"%{q}%")) |
            (AssetIssueBA.perihal.ilike(f"%{q}%")) |
            (AssetIssueBA.lokasi_text.ilike(f"%{q}%"))
        )
    if status_f:
        query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.status == status_f)
    if date_from:
        try:
            df = datetime.strptime(date_from, '%Y-%m-%d')
            query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.created_at >= df)
        except: pass
    if date_to:
        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(date_to, '%Y-%m-%d') + timedelta(days=1)
            query = query.filter(AssetIssueBA.created_at < dt)
        except: pass

    bas = query.order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).all()
    
    # Summaries
    all_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live').count()
    pending_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live', status='pending').count()
    approved_count = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live', status='approved').count()
    
    return render_template("ba_live_list.html", 
                           bas=bas, settings=settings,
                           all_count=all_count, pending_count=pending_count, approved_count=approved_count,
                           q=q, status_f=status_f, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to)

@app.route("/berita-acara/live/new", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_live_new():
    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
    now = get_wib()
    year_month = now.strftime("%Y%m")
    prefix = f"ASN-BA-LV-{year_month}"
    last_ba = AssetIssueBA.query.filter(AssetIssueBA.ba_number.like(f"{prefix}-%")).order_by(AssetIssueBA.id.desc()).first()
    new_num = 1
    if last_ba:
        try: new_num = int(last_ba.ba_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
        except: pass
    generated_ba_number = f"{prefix}-{new_num:03d}"

    prefill_loc = request.args.get('location_name')
    prefill_proj_id = request.args.get('project_id')
    prefill_perihal = ""
    if prefill_proj_id:
        proj = Project.query.get(prefill_proj_id)
        if proj:
            prefill_perihal = f"Go Live - {proj.name}"
            if not prefill_loc and proj.location:
                prefill_loc = proj.location.name

    if request.method == "POST":
        attachments = []
        for i in range(1, 10):
            f = request.files.get(f"att_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"att_title_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
                attachments.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})
        
        pihak1 = {
            "nama": request.form.get("p1_nama", ""),
            "jabatan": request.form.get("p1_jabatan", ""),
            "perusahaan": request.form.get("p1_perusahaan", "")
        }
        pihak2 = {
            "nama": request.form.get("p2_nama", ""),
            "jabatan": request.form.get("p2_jabatan", ""),
            "perusahaan": request.form.get("p2_perusahaan", "")
        }
        
        devices = []
        d_namas = request.form.getlist("dev_nama[]")
        d_ips = request.form.getlist("dev_ip[]")
        d_gates = request.form.getlist("dev_gate[]")
        d_statuses = request.form.getlist("dev_status[]")
        for dn, dip, dg, ds in zip(d_namas, d_ips, d_gates, d_statuses):
            if dn: devices.append({"nama": dn, "ip": dip, "gate": dg, "status": ds})

        signers = []
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        companies = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, companies):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})
        ba = AssetIssueBA(
            engineer_id=current_user.id, ba_number=generated_ba_number,
            perihal=request.form.get("perihal"), lokasi_text=request.form.get("lokasi_text"),
            device_gate=request.form.get("device_gate"), kronologi=request.form.get("kronologi"),
            tindakan_dilakukan=request.form.get("action_taken"), analisa_rekomendasi=request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi"),
            intro_text=request.form.get("intro_text"), ba_type='live',
            content_json=json.dumps({"attachments": attachments, "signers": signers, "pihak1": pihak1, "pihak2": pihak2, "devices": devices})
        )
        db.session.add(ba); db.session.commit()

        # Auto-complete task if project_id is present
        project_id = request.form.get("project_id")
        if project_id:
            proj = Project.query.get(project_id)
            if proj:
                task = ProjectTask.query.filter(
                    ProjectTask.project_id == proj.id,
                    ProjectTask.title.ilike('%ba live%')
                ).first()
                if task:
                    task.is_done = True
                    log = ProjectActivity(
                        project_id=proj.id,
                        engineer_id=current_user.id,
                        personnel_log=f"Completed: {task.title}",
                        description=f"BA Live {ba.ba_number} has been created and submitted.",
                        status='approved',
                        progress_snap=100
                    )
                    db.session.add(log)
                    db.session.commit()

        notify_mentions(ba.kronologi, f"Anda dimention dalam kronologi BA {ba.ba_number}", url_for("ba_live_detail", baid=ba.id))
        notify_mentions(ba.tindakan_dilakukan, f"Anda dimention dalam tindakan BA {ba.ba_number}", url_for("ba_live_detail", baid=ba.id))
        db.session.commit()
        # Notify Pihak Pertama
        p1_user = User.query.filter_by(full_name=pihak1['nama']).first()
        if p1_user:
            add_notification(p1_user.id, f"📝 Anda ditunjuk sebagai Pihak Pertama pada BA Live: {ba.ba_number}", url_for('ba_live_detail', baid=ba.id))
        # Notify Signers
        for s in signers:
            target_user = User.query.filter_by(full_name=s['name']).first()
            if target_user:
                add_notification(target_user.id, f"📝 Anda diminta menandatangani BA Live: {ba.ba_number}", url_for('ba_live_detail', baid=ba.id))
        
        telegram_notify_ba(ba, "created")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_live_list'))
    return render_template("ba_live_form.html", 
                           settings=settings, locations=locations, now=now, 
                           users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all(),
                           device_types=DropdownOption.get('device_types'),
                           prefill_loc=prefill_loc, prefill_perihal=prefill_perihal)

@app.route("/berita-acara/live/<int:baid>")
@login_required
def ba_live_detail(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'live': abort(404)
    settings = get_settings()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    return render_template("ba_live_detail.html", ba=ba, content=content_data, settings=settings)

@app.route("/berita-acara/live/<int:baid>/edit", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_live_edit(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'live': abort(404)
    if ba.engineer_id != current_user.id and current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
        abort(403)
    if ba.status == 'approved' and current_user.role != 'admin':
        flash("Hanya BA status pending yang dapat diedit")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_live_detail', baid=baid))

    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
    now = get_wib()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}

    if request.method == "POST":
        ba.perihal = request.form.get("perihal")
        ba.lokasi_text = request.form.get("lokasi_text")
        ba.device_gate = request.form.get("device_gate")
        ba.kronologi = request.form.get("kronologi")
        ba.tindakan_dilakukan = request.form.get("action_taken")
        ba.analisa_rekomendasi = request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi")
        ba.intro_text = request.form.get("intro_text")
        ba.status = "pending"
        
        attachments = content_data.get('attachments', [])
        for i in range(1, 10):
            f = request.files.get(f"att_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"att_title_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
                if len(attachments) >= i:
                    attachments[i-1] = {"filename": fname, "title": t}
                else:
                    attachments.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})
            elif i <= len(attachments) and t:
                attachments[i-1]['title'] = t
        
        pihak1 = {
            "nama": request.form.get("p1_nama", ""),
            "jabatan": request.form.get("p1_jabatan", ""),
            "perusahaan": request.form.get("p1_perusahaan", "")
        }
        pihak2 = {
            "nama": request.form.get("p2_nama", ""),
            "jabatan": request.form.get("p2_jabatan", ""),
            "perusahaan": request.form.get("p2_perusahaan", "")
        }
        
        devices = []
        d_namas = request.form.getlist("dev_nama[]")
        d_ips = request.form.getlist("dev_ip[]")
        d_gates = request.form.getlist("dev_gate[]")
        d_statuses = request.form.getlist("dev_status[]")
        for dn, dip, dg, ds in zip(d_namas, d_ips, d_gates, d_statuses):
            if dn: devices.append({"nama": dn, "ip": dip, "gate": dg, "status": ds})
            
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        comps = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        signers = []
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, comps):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})
            
        ba.content_json = json.dumps({"attachments": attachments, "signers": signers, "pihak1": pihak1, "pihak2": pihak2, "devices": devices})
        db.session.commit()
        # Notify Signers (if added/updated)
        for s in signers:
            target_user = User.query.filter_by(full_name=s['name']).first()
            if target_user:
                add_notification(target_user.id, f"📝 Anda diminta menandatangani BA Live: {ba.ba_number} (Updated)", url_for('ba_live_detail', baid=ba.id))
                
        return redirect(url_for('ba_live_detail', baid=ba.id))

    return render_template("ba_live_form.html", settings=settings, locations=locations, ba=ba, content=content_data, now=now, 
                           users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all(),
                           device_types=DropdownOption.get('device_types'))

@app.route("/berita-acara/live/<int:baid>/approve", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def ba_live_approve(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'live': abort(404)

    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    unsigned = [s['name'] for s in signers if not s.get('signature') and User.query.filter_by(full_name=s['name']).first()]
    if unsigned:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "msg": f"Approval gagal. Menunggu tanda tangan dari pihak internal: {', '.join(unsigned)}"}), 400

    ba.status = "approved"; ba.approver_id = current_user.id; ba.approved_at = get_wib()
    ba.pdf_filename = generate_ba_pdf(ba.id)
    db.session.commit()
    telegram_notify_ba(ba, "approved")
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

def ba_live_sign(baid):

@app.route("/berita-acara/live/export")
@login_required
def export_ba_live():
    import csv, io
    from flask import Response
    bas = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='live').order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).all()
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output)
    writer.writerow(['Date', 'BA Number', 'Perihal', 'Lokasi', 'Engineer', 'Status'])
    for ba in bas:
        writer.writerow([
            ba.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if ba.created_at else '',
            ba.ba_number or '',
            ba.perihal or '',
            ba.lokasi_text or '',
            ba.engineer.full_name if ba.engineer else 'System',
            ba.status or 'pending'
        ])
    return Response(
        output.getvalue(),
        mimetype="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-disposition": "attachment; filename=ba_live_export.csv"}
    )

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/new", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_new():
    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.order_by(AssetLocation.name.asc()).all()
    now = get_wib()
    year_month = now.strftime("%Y%m")
    prefix = f"ASN-BA-OP-{year_month}"
    last_ba = AssetIssueBA.query.filter(AssetIssueBA.ba_number.like(f"{prefix}-%")).order_by(AssetIssueBA.id.desc()).first()
    new_num = 1
    if last_ba:
        try: new_num = int(last_ba.ba_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
        except: pass
    generated_ba_number = f"{prefix}-{new_num:03d}"

    prefill_loc = request.args.get('location_name')
    prefill_proj_id = request.args.get('project_id')
    prefill_perihal = ""
    if prefill_proj_id:
        proj = Project.query.get(prefill_proj_id)
        if proj:
            prefill_perihal = f"Optimasi Akurasi - {proj.name}"
            if not prefill_loc and proj.location:
                prefill_loc = proj.location.name

    if request.method == "POST":
        # Handle Pengajuan Table Images
        pengajuan = []
        gates = request.form.getlist("p_gate[]")
        kams = request.form.getlist("p_kamera[]")
        reks = request.form.getlist("p_rekomendasi[]")
        for i, (g, k, r) in enumerate(zip(gates, kams, reks)):
            if not g: continue
            fname = None
            f = request.files.get(f"p_image_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            pengajuan.append({"gate": g, "kamera": k, "rekomendasi": r, "image": fname})

        # Handle 2x2 Grid Images
        grid_attachments = []
        for i in range(1, 5):
            f = request.files.get(f"grid_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"grid_title_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
                grid_attachments.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})
            else:
                grid_attachments.append({"filename": None, "title": t})

        # Handle Summary Table
        summary = []
        mats = request.form.getlist("s_material[]")
        qntys = request.form.getlist("s_quantity[]")
        for m, q in zip(mats, qntys):
            if m: summary.append({"material": m, "quantity": q})

        signers = []
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        companies = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, companies):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})

        ba = AssetIssueBA(
            engineer_id=current_user.id, ba_number=generated_ba_number,
            perihal=request.form.get("perihal"), lokasi_text=request.form.get("lokasi_text"),
            device_gate=request.form.get("device_gate"), kronologi=request.form.get("kronologi"),
            tindakan_dilakukan=request.form.get("tindakan_dilakukan"), analisa_rekomendasi=request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi"),
            intro_text=request.form.get("intro_text"), ba_type='optimasi',
            content_json=json.dumps({
                "pengajuan": pengajuan,
                "grid_attachments": grid_attachments,
                "summary": summary,
                "closing_text": request.form.get("closing_text"),
                "signers": signers
            })
        )
        db.session.add(ba); db.session.commit()

        # Auto-complete task if project_id is present
        project_id = request.form.get("project_id")
        if project_id:
            proj = Project.query.get(project_id)
            if proj:
                task = ProjectTask.query.filter(
                    ProjectTask.project_id == proj.id,
                    ProjectTask.title.ilike('%ba optimasi%')
                ).first()
                if task:
                    task.is_done = True
                    log = ProjectActivity(
                        project_id=proj.id,
                        engineer_id=current_user.id,
                        personnel_log=f"Completed: {task.title}",
                        description=f"BA Optimasi {ba.ba_number} has been created and submitted.",
                        status='approved',
                        progress_snap=100
                    )
                    db.session.add(log)
                    db.session.commit()

        telegram_notify_ba(ba, "created")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_optimasi_list'))
    return render_template("ba_optimasi_form.html", 
                           settings=settings, locations=locations, now=now, 
                           users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all(),
                           prefill_loc=prefill_loc, prefill_perihal=prefill_perihal)

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/<int:baid>")
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_detail(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'optimasi': abort(404)
    settings = get_settings()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    return render_template("ba_optimasi_detail.html", ba=ba, content=content_data, settings=settings)

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/<int:baid>/edit", methods=["GET", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_edit(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'optimasi': abort(404)
    if ba.engineer_id != current_user.id and current_user.role not in ['admin', 'lead_engineer']:
        abort(403)
    if ba.status == 'approved' and current_user.role != 'admin':
        flash("Hanya BA status pending yang dapat diedit")
        return redirect(url_for('ba_optimasi_detail', baid=baid))

    settings = get_settings()
    locations = AssetLocation.query.all()
    now = get_wib()
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}

    if request.method == "POST":
        ba.perihal = request.form.get("perihal")
        ba.lokasi_text = request.form.get("lokasi_text")
        ba.device_gate = request.form.get("device_gate")
        ba.kronologi = request.form.get("kronologi")
        ba.tindakan_dilakukan = request.form.get("tindakan_dilakukan")
        ba.analisa_rekomendasi = request.form.get("analisa_rekomendasi")
        ba.intro_text = request.form.get("intro_text")
        ba.status = "pending"
        
        # Update Pengajuan
        pengajuan = content_data.get('pengajuan', [])
        gates = request.form.getlist("p_gate[]")
        kams = request.form.getlist("p_kamera[]")
        reks = request.form.getlist("p_rekomendasi[]")
        new_pengajuan = []
        for i, (g, k, r) in enumerate(zip(gates, kams, reks)):
            if not g: continue
            fname = pengajuan[i]['image'] if i < len(pengajuan) else None
            f = request.files.get(f"p_image_{i}")
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            new_pengajuan.append({"gate": g, "kamera": k, "rekomendasi": r, "image": fname})

        # Update Grid
        grid_attachments = content_data.get('grid_attachments', [])
        new_grid = []
        for i in range(1, 5):
            f = request.files.get(f"grid_file_{i}")
            t = request.form.get(f"grid_title_{i}")
            fname = grid_attachments[i-1]['filename'] if i <= len(grid_attachments) else None
            if f and f.filename:
                fname = secure_filename(f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{f.filename}")
                f.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], fname))
            new_grid.append({"filename": fname, "title": t})

        # Update Summary
        summary = []
        mats = request.form.getlist("s_material[]")
        qntys = request.form.getlist("s_quantity[]")
        for m, q in zip(mats, qntys):
            if m: summary.append({"material": m, "quantity": q})

        # Update Signers
        names = request.form.getlist("signer_name[]")
        roles = request.form.getlist("signer_role[]")
        comps = request.form.getlist("signer_company[]")
        signers = []
        for n, r, c in zip(names, roles, comps):
            if n: signers.append({"name": n, "role": r, "company": c})
            
        ba.content_json = json.dumps({
            "pengajuan": new_pengajuan,
            "grid_attachments": new_grid,
            "summary": summary,
            "closing_text": request.form.get("closing_text"),
            "signers": signers
        })
        db.session.commit()
        return redirect(url_for('ba_optimasi_detail', baid=ba.id))

    return render_template("ba_optimasi_form.html", settings=settings, locations=locations, ba=ba, content=content_data, now=now, users=User.query.order_by(User.full_name).all())

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/<int:baid>/approve", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_approve(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'optimasi': abort(404)

    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    unsigned = [s['name'] for s in signers if not s.get('signature') and User.query.filter_by(full_name=s['name']).first()]
    if unsigned:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "msg": f"Approval gagal. Menunggu tanda tangan dari pihak internal: {', '.join(unsigned)}"}), 400

    ba.status = "approved"; ba.approver_id = current_user.id; ba.approved_at = get_wib()
    ba.pdf_filename = generate_ba_pdf(ba.id)
    db.session.commit()
    telegram_notify_ba(ba, "approved")
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/<int:baid>/sign", methods=["POST"])
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_sign(baid):
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'optimasi': abort(404)
    index = int(request.form.get("index", -1))
    signature = request.form.get("signature", "")
    if not signature or index == -1:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "msg": "Invalid data"}), 400
    try: content_data = json.loads(ba.content_json) if ba.content_json else {}
    except: content_data = {}
    signers = content_data.get('signers', [])
    if index < len(signers):
        signers[index]['signature'] = signature
        signers[index]['signed_at'] = get_wib().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        if current_user.job_position:
            signers[index]['job_position'] = current_user.job_position
    content_data['signers'] = signers
    ba.content_json = json.dumps(content_data)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/<int:baid>/delete", methods=["DELETE", "POST"])
@login_required
def ba_optimasi_delete(baid):
    if current_user.role != 'admin': return jsonify({"ok": False}), 403
    ba = AssetIssueBA.query.get_or_404(baid)
    if ba.ba_type != 'optimasi': abort(404)
    db.session.delete(ba); db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"ok": True})

@app.route("/berita-acara/optimasi/export")
@login_required
def export_ba_optimasi():
    import csv, io
    from flask import Response
    bas = AssetIssueBA.query.filter_by(ba_type='optimasi').order_by(AssetIssueBA.created_at.desc()).all()
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output)
    writer.writerow(['Date', 'BA Number', 'Perihal', 'Lokasi', 'Engineer', 'Status'])
    for ba in bas:
        writer.writerow([
            ba.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if ba.created_at else '',
            ba.ba_number or '',
            ba.perihal or '',
            ba.lokasi_text or '',
            ba.engineer.full_name if ba.engineer else 'System',
            ba.status or 'pending'
        ])
    return Response(
        output.getvalue(),
        mimetype="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-disposition": "attachment; filename=ba_optimasi_export.csv"}
    )

@app.route('/api/user/signature', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def update_user_signature():
    data = request.json or {}
    sig = data.get('signature')
    if not sig:
        return jsonify({'ok': False, 'msg': 'No signature provided'}), 400
    
    current_user.user_signature = sig
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'ok': True})

@app.route('/api/user/signature', methods=['GET'])
@login_required
def get_user_signature():
    return jsonify({'ok': True, 'signature': current_user.user_signature})

@app.context_processor
def inject_user_signature():
    return dict(current_user_signature=current_user.user_signature if current_user.is_authenticated else None)

if __name__ == '__main__':
   app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=5006, debug=False)
